==> m14_cinepro/app/src/main/java/com/m14/cinepro/camera/CameraEngine.kt <==
package com.m14.cinepro.camera

import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Rect
import android.graphics.SurfaceTexture
import android.hardware.camera2.CameraAccessException
import android.hardware.camera2.CameraCaptureSession
import android.hardware.camera2.CameraCharacteristics
import android.hardware.camera2.CameraConstrainedHighSpeedCaptureSession
import android.hardware.camera2.CameraDevice
import android.hardware.camera2.CameraManager
import android.hardware.camera2.CaptureFailure
import android.hardware.camera2.CaptureRequest
import android.hardware.camera2.CaptureResult
import android.hardware.camera2.TotalCaptureResult
import android.hardware.camera2.params.OutputConfiguration
import android.hardware.camera2.params.SessionConfiguration
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.HandlerThread
import android.os.Looper
import android.os.Process
import android.os.SystemClock
import android.util.Log
import android.util.Range
import android.view.Surface
import com.m14.cinepro.core.EngineListener
import com.m14.cinepro.core.Models
import com.m14.cinepro.core.SettingsStore
import java.util.concurrent.Executor

private const val TAG = "M14Cam"

/** Camera 0 = back camera (task law; M14 single rear 50MP S5KJN1). */
private const val CAMERA_ID = "0"

/** CameraEngine handler-thread priority (foreground-ish, per task law setThreadPriority(tid, -2)). */
private const val CAM_THREAD_PRIORITY = -2

/** Camera telemetry to the HUD is throttled to >= 3 Hz (333 ms floor). */
private const val TELEMETRY_MIN_INTERVAL_MS = 333L

/** "frame drop (HAL)" info lines are throttled to at most one per second. */
private const val FRAME_DROP_INFO_INTERVAL_MS = 1_000L

/** Delay before the post-tap repeating request flips CONTROL_AF_TRIGGER START -> IDLE (~150 ms law). */
private const val AF_TRIGGER_IDLE_RESET_DELAY_MS = 160L

/** HFR (720p high-speed) target fps — verified output of the M14 high-speed pipeline. */
private const val HFR_FPS = 60

/** Raised for CameraDevice.StateCallback.onError codes (no dedicated platform exception type). */
private class CameraDeviceException(message: String) : RuntimeException(message)

/** Raised for CameraCaptureSession.StateCallback.onConfigureFailed. */
private class SessionException(message: String) : RuntimeException(message)

/**
 * Sanitized request-key law (M14): setting a key the platform does not accept must throw
 * IllegalArgumentException at most into this guard — never up into request construction.
 */
fun <T> CaptureRequest.Builder.trySet(key: CaptureRequest.Key<T>, value: T) {
    try {
        set(key, value)
    } catch (e: IllegalArgumentException) {
        Log.w(TAG, "request key rejected by platform: ${key.name}", e)
    }
}

/**
 * M14 CinePro — Camera2 orchestrator.
 *
 * Owns the camera device, the capture session (regular or constrained high-speed) and the single
 * repeating request that carries the manual/auto exposure, WB and AF state applied by
 * [ExposureController], [WhiteBalanceController] and [FocusController].
 *
 * Key laws implemented here (stage3_camera2.md + M14_VERIFIED_TRUTH.md):
 *  - camera id "0" opened on a dedicated HandlerThread "CameraEngine" (priority -2);
 *  - regular sessions via SessionConfiguration(SESSION_REGULAR), HFR (settings.resolution.highSpeed)
 *    via createConstrainedHighSpeedCaptureSession + createHighSpeedRequestList with
 *    CONTROL_AE_TARGET_FPS_RANGE from the §2.4 e(fps) selection law (upper == 60, prefer lower == 60);
 *  - base request template TEMPLATE_RECORD (TEMPLATE_PREVIEW for HFR, required by CONSTRAINED_HIGH_SPEED),
 *    CONTROL_CAPTURE_INTENT = VIDEO_RECORD (non-HFR only), CONTROL_MODE_AUTO,
 *    CONTROL_AE_TARGET_FPS_RANGE = Range(fps, fps), NOISE_REDUCTION_MODE_FAST (via FocusController);
 *    CONTROL_ENABLE_ZSL is never set;
 *  - capture-callback telemetry (AE/AF state, STATISTICS_SCENE_FLICKER) to [EngineListener] at >= 3 Hz,
 *    frame drops surfaced as throttled info lines — never a crash;
 *  - one automatic session rebuild on session failure.
 *
 * All listener callbacks are delivered on the main thread.
 */
class CameraEngine(
    private val context: Context,
    private val settings: SettingsStore,
    private val listener: EngineListener
) {

    /** Exposure controller — public for direct HUD reads (caps/labels). */
    val exposureController = ExposureController(settings)

    /** Focus controller — public for direct HUD use (tap state queries / explicit restore). */
    val focusController = FocusController(settings)

    /** White-balance controller — public for direct HUD use (normalizedShaderGains for the GL trim). */
    val whiteBalanceController = WhiteBalanceController(settings)

    /** Snapshot of the exposure-relevant HAL capabilities for the HUD. */
    data class CapsInfo(
        val exposureMinNs: Long,
        val exposureMaxNs: Long,
        val isoMin: Int,
        val isoMax: Int
    )

    private val mainHandler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())

    /** Session-configuration executor: dispatches session callbacks onto the camera thread. */
    private val sessionExecutor = Executor { runnable ->
        val handler = camHandler
        if (handler != null) handler.post(runnable) else runnable.run()
    }

    /** Post-tap delayed refresh: flips CONTROL_AF_TRIGGER back to IDLE ~150 ms after a tap. */
    private val tapIdleReset = Runnable { applySettingsInternal() }

    @Volatile private var handlerThread: HandlerThread? = null
    @Volatile private var camHandler: Handler? = null

    @Volatile private var cameraManager: CameraManager? = null
    @Volatile private var device: CameraDevice? = null
    @Volatile private var session: CameraCaptureSession? = null
    @Volatile private var sessionConfigured = false
    @Volatile private var deviceWanted = false

    @Volatile private var sensorOrientationDeg = 90
    @Volatile private var activeArrayRect: Rect? = null
    @Volatile private var exposureCaps: ExposureController.CameraCaps? = null
    @Volatile private var highSpeedFpsRanges: Array<Range<Int>>? = null

    private var currentRecordSurface: Surface? = null
    private var currentPreviewSurface: Surface? = null
    private var currentPreviewTexture: SurfaceTexture? = null
    private var pendingOnReady: (() -> Unit)? = null
    private var sessionRebuildAttempted = false

    private var lastTelemetryAtMs = 0L
    private var lastFrameDropInfoAtMs = 0L

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Public API (MainActivity wiring)
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    /**
     * Open camera id "0" (back), cache characteristics/caps, then invoke [onReady] on the main
     * thread. Failures are reported through [EngineListener.onError] ("open"/"device"/"open-cam")
     * and leave [onReady] uncalled. Idempotent: calling open while already open re-invokes onReady.
     */
    fun open(onReady: () -> Unit) {
        val handler = ensureHandler()
        handler.post {
            deviceWanted = true
            if (device != null) {
                cacheCharacteristics()
                mainNotify { onReady() }
                return@post
            }
            pendingOnReady = onReady
            try {
                val manager = context.getSystemService(Context.CAMERA_SERVICE) as CameraManager
                cameraManager = manager
                manager.openCamera(CAMERA_ID, deviceCallback, handler)
            } catch (e: SecurityException) {
                failOpen(e)
            } catch (e: IllegalArgumentException) {
                failOpen(e)
            } catch (e: CameraAccessException) {
                failOpen(e)
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                failOpen(e)
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Tear down the capture session and close the camera device. Idempotent; safe to call from
     * any thread. The handler thread stays alive so the engine can be re-opened after close().
     */
    fun close() {
        deviceWanted = false
        pendingOnReady = null
        val thread = handlerThread ?: return
        if (!thread.isAlive) return
        val handler = camHandler ?: return
        handler.post {
            session?.let { s ->
                try { s.stopRepeating() } catch (t: Throwable) { Log.w(TAG, "stopRepeating failed", t) }
                try { s.abortCaptures() } catch (t: Throwable) { Log.w(TAG, "abortCaptures failed", t) }
                try { s.close() } catch (t: Throwable) { Log.w(TAG, "session close failed", t) }
            }
            session = null
            sessionConfigured = false
            device?.let { d ->
                try { d.close() } catch (t: Throwable) { Log.w(TAG, "device close failed", t) }
            }
            device = null
        }
    }

    /**
     * Build the capture session for [settings.resolution].
     *
     * Outputs: [recordSurface] (encoder input; may be null for preview-only operation) plus the
     * mandatory 1920x1080 viewfinder surface created from [previewTexture]
     * (setDefaultBufferSize(1920, 1080) is applied to the SurfaceTexture BEFORE the Surface is
     * created — Architecture A law). Regular resolutions use
     * SessionConfiguration(SESSION_REGULAR); high-speed resolutions use
     * createConstrainedHighSpeedCaptureSession and a createHighSpeedRequestList repeating burst.
     *
     * [onConfigured] is invoked exactly once on the main thread with the outcome; on failure one
     * automatic rebuild is attempted before reporting false.
     */
    fun createSession(recordSurface: Surface?, previewTexture: SurfaceTexture, onConfigured: (Boolean) -> Unit) {
        val handler = ensureHandler()
        handler.post {
            currentRecordSurface = recordSurface
            currentPreviewTexture = previewTexture
            sessionRebuildAttempted = false
            try {
                previewTexture.setDefaultBufferSize(Models.VIEWFINDER_WIDTH, Models.VIEWFINDER_HEIGHT)
            } catch (t: Throwable) {
                Log.w(TAG, "setDefaultBufferSize failed", t)
            }
            closeSessionQuietly()
            val previewSurface = Surface(previewTexture)
            currentPreviewSurface = previewSurface
            createSessionOutputs(recordSurface, previewSurface, onConfigured)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Rebuild and re-push the repeating request with the current settings state (exposure, WB,
     * focus, torch, fps). No-op when no session is configured.
     */
    fun applySettings() {
        val handler = ensureHandler()
        handler.post { applySettingsInternal() }
    }

    /**
     * Tap-to-focus at viewfinder-normalized coordinates ([nx], [ny] in the upright, display-
     * rotated viewfinder, y down). Arms the FocusController two-phase AF sequence and refreshes
     * the repeating request; a delayed refresh (~150 ms) resets the AF trigger to IDLE.
     */
    fun focusAt(nx: Float, ny: Float) {
        val handler = ensureHandler()
        handler.post {
            val currentSession = session ?: return@post
            val activeArray = activeArrayRect ?: return@post
            val builder = buildBaseRequest() ?: return@post
            focusController.startTapFocus(FocusController.FocusPoint(nx, ny), activeArray, sensorOrientationDeg, builder)
            setRepeating(currentSession, builder)
            handler.removeCallbacks(tapIdleReset)
            handler.postDelayed(tapIdleReset, AF_TRIGGER_IDLE_RESET_DELAY_MS)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Torch toggle. Preferred path is request-level FLASH_MODE_TORCH inside the session
     * (stage3 §2.9 x()), so this just records the state and re-applies settings; the value is
     * persisted and also honored by the next session build.
     */
    fun setTorch(on: Boolean) {
        settings.torchOn = on
        val handler = ensureHandler()
        handler.post { applySettingsInternal() }
    }

    /** True while a configured capture session with a record output is live. */
    fun isRecordingSessionActive(): Boolean = sessionConfigured && currentRecordSurface != null

    /** Exposure/ISO capability snapshot for the HUD, or null before the camera is opened. */
    fun capsSnapshot(): CapsInfo? {
        val caps = exposureCaps ?: return null
        val exposureRange = caps.exposureRange
        val isoRange = caps.isoRange
        return CapsInfo(
            exposureMinNs = exposureRange?.lower ?: Models.EXPOSURE_MIN_NS,
            exposureMaxNs = exposureRange?.upper ?: Models.EXPOSURE_MAX_NS,
            isoMin = isoRange?.lower ?: Models.ISO_MIN,
            isoMax = isoRange?.upper ?: Models.ISO_MAX
        )
    }

    /** Sensor mount orientation in degrees (0/90/180/270); 90 until characteristics are read. */
    fun sensorOrientation(): Int = sensorOrientationDeg

    /** SENSOR_INFO_ACTIVE_ARRAY_SIZE, or null before the camera is opened. */
    fun activeArray(): Rect? = activeArrayRect

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Camera device lifecycle
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    private fun failOpen(e: Exception) {
        pendingOnReady = null
        Log.w(TAG, "openCamera failed", e)
        mainNotify { listener.onError("open", e) }
    }

    private val deviceCallback = object : CameraDevice.StateCallback() {
        override fun onOpened(camera: CameraDevice) {
            if (!deviceWanted) {
                // close() raced the HAL — discard the unwanted handle.
                try { camera.close() } catch (t: Throwable) { Log.w(TAG, "unwanted device close failed", t) }
                return
            }
            device = camera
            cacheCharacteristics()
            val ready = pendingOnReady
            pendingOnReady = null
            if (ready != null) mainNotify { ready() }
        }

        override fun onDisconnected(camera: CameraDevice) {
            try { camera.close() } catch (t: Throwable) { Log.w(TAG, "device close failed", t) }
            device = null
            sessionConfigured = false
            mainNotify { listener.onInfo("camera disconnected") }
        }

        override fun onError(camera: CameraDevice, error: Int) {
            try { camera.close() } catch (t: Throwable) { Log.w(TAG, "device close failed", t) }
            device = null
            sessionConfigured = false
            mainNotify { listener.onError("device", CameraDeviceException("CameraDevice error $error")) }
        }
    }

    private fun cacheCharacteristics() {
        try {
            val manager = cameraManager
                ?: (context.getSystemService(Context.CAMERA_SERVICE) as CameraManager).also { cameraManager = it }
            val characteristics = manager.getCameraCharacteristics(CAMERA_ID)
            exposureCaps = exposureController.readCaps(characteristics)
            sensorOrientationDeg = characteristics.get(CameraCharacteristics.SENSOR_ORIENTATION) ?: 90
            activeArrayRect = characteristics.get(CameraCharacteristics.SENSOR_INFO_ACTIVE_ARRAY_SIZE)
            highSpeedFpsRanges = try {
                characteristics.get(CameraCharacteristics.SCALER_STREAM_CONFIGURATION_MAP)?.highSpeedVideoFpsRanges
            } catch (t: Throwable) {
                null
            }
        } catch (e: Throwable) {
            Log.w(TAG, "characteristics unavailable", e)
        }
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Session lifecycle
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    private fun closeSessionQuietly() {
        val current = session ?: return
        try { current.stopRepeating() } catch (t: Throwable) { Log.w(TAG, "stopRepeating failed", t) }
        try { current.close() } catch (t: Throwable) { Log.w(TAG, "session close failed", t) }
        if (session === current) session = null
        sessionConfigured = false
    }

    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
    private fun createSessionOutputs(recordSurface: Surface?, previewSurface: Surface, onConfigured: (Boolean) -> Unit) {
        val cameraDevice = device
        if (cameraDevice == null) {
            Log.w(TAG, "createSession without an open device")
            mainNotify { onConfigured(false) }
            return
        }
        val highSpeed = settings.resolution.highSpeed

        val outputs = ArrayList<Surface>(2)
        if (recordSurface != null) outputs.add(recordSurface)
        outputs.add(previewSurface)

        val stateCallback = object : CameraCaptureSession.StateCallback() {
            override fun onConfigured(configured: CameraCaptureSession) {
                session = configured
                val builder = buildBaseRequest()
                val ok = builder != null && setRepeating(configured, builder)
                if (ok) sessionRebuildAttempted = false
                sessionConfigured = ok
                mainNotify { onConfigured(ok) }
            }

            override fun onConfigureFailed(configured: CameraCaptureSession) {
                Log.w(TAG, "session configuration failed")
                if (session === configured) session = null
                sessionConfigured = false
                mainNotify { onConfigured(false) }
                handleSessionFailure(SessionException("session configuration failed"))
            }

            override fun onClosed(closed: CameraCaptureSession) {
                if (session === closed) {
                    session = null
                    sessionConfigured = false
                }
            }
        }

        try {
            if (highSpeed) {
                cameraDevice.createConstrainedHighSpeedCaptureSession(outputs, stateCallback, camHandler)
            } else {
                val outputConfigs = outputs.map { OutputConfiguration(it) }
                val sessionConfiguration = SessionConfiguration(
                    SessionConfiguration.SESSION_REGULAR,
                    outputConfigs,
                    sessionExecutor,
                    stateCallback
                )
                cameraDevice.createCaptureSession(sessionConfiguration)
            }
        } catch (e: CameraAccessException) {
            failSessionCreate(e, onConfigured)
        } catch (e: IllegalArgumentException) {
            failSessionCreate(e, onConfigured)
        } catch (e: SecurityException) {
            failSessionCreate(e, onConfigured)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            failSessionCreate(e, onConfigured)
        }
    }

    private fun failSessionCreate(e: Exception, onConfigured: (Boolean) -> Unit) {
        Log.w(TAG, "createSession failed", e)
        mainNotify { listener.onError("session", e) }
        mainNotify { onConfigured(false) }
    }

    /** One-shot session rebuild law: on session failure, rebuild exactly once. */
    private fun handleSessionFailure(e: Throwable) {
        Log.w(TAG, "session failure", e)
        sessionConfigured = false
        mainNotify { listener.onError("session", e) }
        if (!sessionRebuildAttempted) {
            sessionRebuildAttempted = true
            val previewSurface = currentPreviewSurface
            if (previewSurface != null) {
                createSessionOutputs(currentRecordSurface, previewSurface) { ok ->
                    if (!ok) Log.w(TAG, "session rebuild failed")
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Repeating request
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    /**
     * Build the base repeating request: template + targets + static keys + the three controllers.
     * Null when the device or the mandatory preview surface is gone.
     */
    private fun buildBaseRequest(): CaptureRequest.Builder? {
        val cameraDevice = device ?: return null
        val previewSurface = currentPreviewSurface ?: return null
        val recordSurface = currentRecordSurface
        val highSpeed = settings.resolution.highSpeed

        val template = if (highSpeed) CameraDevice.TEMPLATE_PREVIEW else CameraDevice.TEMPLATE_RECORD
        val builder = try {
            cameraDevice.createCaptureRequest(template)
        } catch (e: Throwable) {
            Log.w(TAG, "createCaptureRequest failed", e)
            null
        } ?: return null

        try {
            builder.addTarget(previewSurface)
        } catch (t: Throwable) {
            Log.w(TAG, "preview target rejected", t)
        }
        if (recordSurface != null) {
            try {
                builder.addTarget(recordSurface)
            } catch (t: Throwable) {
                Log.w(TAG, "record target rejected", t)
            }
        }

        if (highSpeed) {
            // HFR repeating burst: §2.4 e(fps) selection (upper == 60, prefer lower == 60).
            builder.trySet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_TARGET_FPS_RANGE, hfrFpsRange())
        } else {
            builder.trySet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_CAPTURE_INTENT, CaptureRequest.CONTROL_CAPTURE_INTENT_VIDEO_RECORD)
            val fps = settings.fps
            if (fps > 0) builder.trySet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_TARGET_FPS_RANGE, Range(fps, fps))
        }
        builder.trySet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_MODE, CaptureRequest.CONTROL_MODE_AUTO)
        // CONTROL_ENABLE_ZSL is never set (M14 law: deterministic non-ZSL video pipeline).

        exposureController.apply(builder, exposureCaps, settings.fps)
        whiteBalanceController.apply(builder, whiteBalanceController.currentCcm())
        focusController.apply(builder, activeArrayRect, sensorOrientationDeg)

        builder.trySet(
            CaptureRequest.FLASH_MODE,
            if (settings.torchOn) CaptureRequest.FLASH_MODE_TORCH else CaptureRequest.FLASH_MODE_OFF
        )
        return builder
    }

    /**
     * Push [builder] as the repeating request (regular) or as the high-speed burst list (HFR).
     * Returns false and routes through the one-rebuild law on session-level failures; a rejected
     * request payload is logged + surfaced as info without a rebuild loop.
     */
    private fun setRepeating(targetSession: CameraCaptureSession, builder: CaptureRequest.Builder): Boolean {
        val handler = camHandler
        return try {
            if (targetSession is CameraConstrainedHighSpeedCaptureSession) {
                val list = targetSession.createHighSpeedRequestList(builder.build())
                targetSession.setRepeatingBurst(list, captureCallback, handler)
            } else {
                targetSession.setRepeatingRequest(builder.build(), captureCallback, handler)
            }
            true
        } catch (e: CameraAccessException) {
            handleSessionFailure(e)
            false
        } catch (e: IllegalStateException) {
            handleSessionFailure(e)
            false
        } catch (e: IllegalArgumentException) {
            Log.w(TAG, "repeating request rejected", e)
            mainNotify { listener.onInfo("request rejected: ${e.message ?: "bad request"}") }
            false
        }
    }

    private fun applySettingsInternal() {
        val currentSession = session ?: return
        val builder = buildBaseRequest() ?: return
        setRepeating(currentSession, builder)
    }

    /** stage3 §2.4 e(fps): pick the high-speed range with upper == HFR_FPS, prefer lower == HFR_FPS. */
    private fun hfrFpsRange(): Range<Int> {
        val ranges = highSpeedFpsRanges
        if (ranges != null) {
            var minLower = -1
            var bestWithMinLower: Range<Int>? = null
            for (range in ranges) {
                if (range.upper != HFR_FPS) continue
                if (range.lower == HFR_FPS) return range
                if (minLower < 0 || range.lower < minLower) {
                    minLower = range.lower
                    bestWithMinLower = range
                }
            }
            if (bestWithMinLower != null) return bestWithMinLower
        }
        return Range(HFR_FPS, HFR_FPS)
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Capture callback: telemetry / AF drive / CCM cache / frame drops
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    private val captureCallback = object : CameraCaptureSession.CaptureCallback() {
        override fun onCaptureCompleted(
            session: CameraCaptureSession,
            request: CaptureRequest,
            result: TotalCaptureResult
        ) {
            try {
                val aeState = result.get(CaptureResult.CONTROL_AE_STATE) ?: 0
                val afState = result.get(CaptureResult.CONTROL_AF_STATE) ?: 0
                val flicker = result.get(CaptureResult.STATISTICS_SCENE_FLICKER) ?: 0

                val afWantsRefresh = focusController.onAfResult(afState)
                settings.sceneFlicker = flicker
                whiteBalanceController.onCaptureResult(result)
                if (afWantsRefresh) applySettingsInternal()

                val now = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
                if (now - lastTelemetryAtMs >= TELEMETRY_MIN_INTERVAL_MS) {
                    lastTelemetryAtMs = now
                    mainNotify { listener.onCameraTelemetry(aeState, afState, flicker) }
                }
            } catch (t: Throwable) {
                Log.w(TAG, "capture telemetry failed", t)
            }
        }

        override fun onCaptureFailed(
            session: CameraCaptureSession,
            request: CaptureRequest,
            failure: CaptureFailure
        ) {
            try {
                val now = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
                if (now - lastFrameDropInfoAtMs >= FRAME_DROP_INFO_INTERVAL_MS) {
                    lastFrameDropInfoAtMs = now
                    mainNotify { listener.onInfo("frame drop (HAL)") }
                }
            } catch (t: Throwable) {
                Log.w(TAG, "frame-drop notify failed", t)
            }
        }
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Threading
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    /** Handler thread lifecycle: (re)created lazily so open() works after close(). */
    private fun ensureHandler(): Handler {
        var thread = handlerThread
        if (thread == null || !thread.isAlive) {
            thread = HandlerThread("CameraEngine")
            thread.start()
            try {
                Process.setThreadPriority(thread.threadId, CAM_THREAD_PRIORITY)
            } catch (t: Throwable) {
                Log.w(TAG, "thread priority set failed", t)
            }
            handlerThread = thread
            camHandler = Handler(thread.looper)
        } else if (camHandler == null) {
            camHandler = Handler(thread.looper)
        }
        return camHandler!!
    }

    /** All listener deliveries go to the main thread. */
    private fun mainNotify(block: () -> Unit) {
        mainHandler.post(block)
    }
}

==> m14_cinepro/app/src/main/java/com/m14/cinepro/camera/ExposureController.kt <==
package com.m14.cinepro.camera

import android.hardware.camera2.CameraCharacteristics
import android.hardware.camera2.CaptureRequest
import android.util.Log
import android.util.Range
import com.m14.cinepro.core.Models
import com.m14.cinepro.core.SettingsStore

private const val TAG = "M14Cam"

/** Default EV compensation step (1/3 EV) when the HAL does not report CONTROL_AE_COMPENSATION_STEP. */
private const val DEFAULT_EV_STEP = 1f / 3f

/**
 * M14 CinePro — manual/auto exposure CaptureRequest application (stage3_camera2.md §2.1–2.4).
 *
 * Manual path ([SettingsStore.manualExposure] == true) implements the verified "stealth unlock"
 * (M14_VERIFIED_TRUTH.md §4.D): with CONTROL_AE_MODE_OFF the Exynos 1330 HAL executes
 * SENSOR_EXPOSURE_TIME / SENSOR_SENSITIVITY directly (30 µs..100 ms, ISO 100..3200), even though
 * both keys are missing from the sanitized `availableRequestKeys` table of Camera 0.
 *
 * - SENSOR_EXPOSURE_TIME  = settings.effectiveExposureNs() (110 µs headroom-snapped by Models),
 *                           additionally clamped to SENSOR_INFO_EXPOSURE_TIME_RANGE.
 * - SENSOR_SENSITIVITY    = settings.iso clamped to the 100..3200 law, then to
 *                           SENSOR_INFO_SENSITIVITY_RANGE.
 * - SENSOR_FRAME_DURATION = 1e9 / fps (stage3 §2.1/§2.4 pacing law; the HAL may silently ignore
 *                           it — VERIFIED_TRUTH B2 — so it is never treated as fatal).
 *
 * Auto path (§2.3): CONTROL_AE_MODE_ON + CONTROL_AE_LOCK + EV compensation converted to steps
 * via `round(ev / step)` clamped to CONTROL_AE_COMPENSATION_RANGE.
 *
 * Every key set goes through [guardedSet] (catches IllegalArgumentException) so an unavailable
 * key can never crash request construction (M14 sanitized request-key law).
 */
class ExposureController(private val settings: SettingsStore) {

    /** Capability snapshot read once at camera-open from [CameraCharacteristics]. */
    data class CameraCaps(
        val exposureRange: Range<Long>?,
        val isoRange: Range<Int>?,
        val evStep: Float,
        val evSteps: Range<Int>?,
        val antibandingModes: IntArray,
        val maxFrameDurationNs: Long
    )

    /**
     * Read the exposure-relevant capability set from the camera characteristics.
     * Every individual read is guarded; a missing key degrades to null / safe default.
     */
    fun readCaps(characteristics: CameraCharacteristics): CameraCaps {
        val exposureRange = readKey(characteristics, CameraCharacteristics.SENSOR_INFO_EXPOSURE_TIME_RANGE)
        val isoRange = readKey(characteristics, CameraCharacteristics.SENSOR_INFO_SENSITIVITY_RANGE)
        val evSteps = readKey(characteristics, CameraCharacteristics.CONTROL_AE_COMPENSATION_RANGE)
        val stepRational = readKey(characteristics, CameraCharacteristics.CONTROL_AE_COMPENSATION_STEP)
        var evStep = stepRational?.toFloat() ?: DEFAULT_EV_STEP
        if (evStep <= 0f || evStep.isNaN() || evStep.isInfinite()) evStep = DEFAULT_EV_STEP
        val antibandingModes = readKey(
            characteristics,
            CameraCharacteristics.CONTROL_AE_AVAILABLE_ANTIBANDING_MODES
        ) ?: IntArray(0)
        val maxFrameDurationNs = readKey(
            characteristics,
            CameraCharacteristics.SENSOR_INFO_MAX_FRAME_DURATION
        ) ?: 0L
        return CameraCaps(exposureRange, isoRange, evStep, evSteps, antibandingModes, maxFrameDurationNs)
    }

    /**
     * Apply the current exposure state onto [builder]. [fps] is the recording frame rate
     * (settings.fps) used for the SENSOR_FRAME_DURATION pacing law; [caps] may be null when the
     * characteristics have not been read yet (all clamps then fall back to the Models law).
     */
    fun apply(builder: CaptureRequest.Builder, caps: CameraCaps?, fps: Int) {
        val fpsSafe = if (fps > 0) fps else 30
        val frameDurationNs = 1_000_000_000L / fpsSafe

        if (settings.manualExposure) {
            // stage3 §2.1 manual law — AE OFF + direct sensor injection (stealth manual exposure).
            builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_MODE, CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_MODE_OFF)
            var exposureNs = settings.effectiveExposureNs()
            caps?.exposureRange?.let { exposureNs = clampLong(exposureNs, it) }
            builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.SENSOR_EXPOSURE_TIME, exposureNs)

            var iso = settings.iso.coerceIn(Models.ISO_MIN, Models.ISO_MAX)
            caps?.isoRange?.let { iso = clampInt(iso, it) }
            builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.SENSOR_SENSITIVITY, iso)

            // Frame pacing: HAL may silently ignore (VERIFIED_TRUTH B2) — requested, never fatal.
            builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.SENSOR_FRAME_DURATION, frameDurationNs)
        } else {
            // stage3 §2.3 auto path: AE ON + lock + EV compensation in integer steps.
            builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_MODE, CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_MODE_ON)
            builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_LOCK, settings.aeLock)
            if (caps != null) {
                val steps = caps.evSteps
                if (steps != null && caps.evStep > 0f) {
                    val rawSteps = Math.round(settings.evCompensation / caps.evStep)
                    builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_EXPOSURE_COMPENSATION, clampInt(rawSteps, steps))
                }
            }
            // stage3 §2.4 fps pacing law: 1e9/fps is requested in AE-ON as well.
            builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.SENSOR_FRAME_DURATION, frameDurationNs)
        }

        // Antibanding only when the mode is advertised by the HAL (stage3 §2.10 h()).
        val abMode = settings.antiBanding.camera2Value
        val modes = caps?.antibandingModes
        if (modes == null || modes.contains(abMode)) {
            builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_ANTIBANDING_MODE, abMode)
        }
    }

    private fun <T> readKey(characteristics: CameraCharacteristics, key: CameraCharacteristics.Key<T>): T? =
        try {
            characteristics.get(key)
        } catch (t: Throwable) {
            null
        }

    private fun clampLong(value: Long, range: Range<Long>): Long {
        val lo = minOf(range.lower, range.upper)
        val hi = maxOf(range.lower, range.upper)
        return value.coerceIn(lo, hi)
    }

    private fun clampInt(value: Int, range: Range<Int>): Int {
        val lo = minOf(range.lower, range.upper)
        val hi = maxOf(range.lower, range.upper)
        return value.coerceIn(lo, hi)
    }
}

/**
 * Sanitized request-key law: setting a key the platform/HAL does not accept must throw
 * IllegalArgumentException at most into this guard — never up into request construction.
 */
private fun <T> CaptureRequest.Builder.guardedSet(key: CaptureRequest.Key<T>, value: T) {
    try {
        set(key, value)
    } catch (e: IllegalArgumentException) {
        Log.w(TAG, "request key rejected by platform: ${key.name}", e)
    }
}

==> m14_cinepro/app/src/main/java/com/m14/cinepro/camera/FocusController.kt <==
package com.m14.cinepro.camera

import android.graphics.Rect
import android.hardware.camera2.CaptureRequest
import android.hardware.camera2.CaptureResult
import android.hardware.camera2.params.MeteringRectangle
import android.os.SystemClock
import android.util.Log
import com.m14.cinepro.core.Models
import com.m14.cinepro.core.SettingsStore

private const val TAG = "M14Cam"

/** Tap/marker metering weight — 0x3e8 law (stage3_camera2.md §2.6, ah0.e). */
private const val METERING_WEIGHT = 1000

/** Delay before the repeating request flips CONTROL_AF_TRIGGER back to IDLE (task law ~150 ms). */
private const val AF_TRIGGER_IDLE_DELAY_MS = 150L

/** A tap-focus hold lasts this long before CONTINUOUS_PICTURE is restored automatically. */
private const val AF_TAP_HOLD_MS = 5_000L

/** Shared zero-weight sentinel region used to "clear" AF/AE regions (stage3 §2.6 ah0.a). */
private val ZERO_REGION = arrayOf(MeteringRectangle(Rect(0, 0, 0, 0), 0))

/**
 * M14 CinePro — focus control.
 *
 * M14 HARD RULES (M14_VERIFIED_TRUTH.md §4.D — violating them = immediate onCaptureFailed reason 0):
 *  - NEVER set CONTROL_AF_MODE_OFF(0): the HAL rejects it (available AF modes are [1, 3, 4] only).
 *  - NEVER set LENS_FOCUS_DISTANCE: the driver aborts the frame (CAPTURE_FAILURE_REASON_ERROR).
 *  - Only CONTROL_AF_MODE_AUTO(1) / CONTINUOUS_VIDEO(3) / CONTINUOUS_PICTURE(4) are ever written.
 *  - NEVER NOISE_REDUCTION_MODE_OFF(0); NOISE_REDUCTION_MODE_FAST(1) is applied on every request
 *    this controller touches.
 *
 * Tap-to-focus is a two-phase state machine (stage3 §2.6 C()):
 *  1. [startTapFocus] arms AF_MODE_AUTO + AF_TRIGGER_START + 1000-weight AF/AE metering regions
 *     (plus AE_MODE_ON + AE_PRECAPTURE_TRIGGER_START when AE is not manually overridden).
 *  2. The next request built ≥150 ms later (CameraEngine's applySettings cycle / delayed reset)
 *     flips the trigger to IDLE while keeping the ROI, so the HAL converges and reports
 *     FOCUSED_LOCKED.
 *  3. [onAfResult] (fed by CameraEngine from CaptureResult.CONTROL_AF_STATE) returns true when the
 *     tap sequence must move on; CameraEngine then refreshes the repeating request. NOT_FOCUSED_LOCKED
 *     and a 5 s hold both fall back to CONTINUOUS_PICTURE so CAF can never wedge off.
 *
 * F-markers (settings.focusMarkers, normalized viewfinder coords) are honored as persistent AF/AE
 * metering regions while in CONTINUOUS phase — the only meaningful "marker focus" on M14, where
 * LENS_FOCUS_DISTANCE is HAL-blocked.
 */
class FocusController(private val settings: SettingsStore) {

    data class FocusPoint(val normalizedX: Float, val normalizedY: Float)

    private enum class Phase { CONTINUOUS, TAP_START, TAP_ACTIVE }

    @Volatile private var phase = Phase.CONTINUOUS
    @Volatile private var tapRegions: Array<MeteringRectangle>? = null
    private var tapStartedAtMs = 0L

    /**
     * Map a viewfinder tap (normalized in the upright, display-rotated viewfinder, y down) into a
     * sensor-space [MeteringRectangle] with weight 1000.
     *
     * Mapping: (1) the HAL center-crops the active array to the viewfinder aspect — compute that
     * visible window; (2) undo the sensor mount rotation ([sensorOrientation] in {0, 90, 180, 270})
     * to invert the display rotation; (3) place a ~1/8-of-visible-window rect centered on the tap,
     * clamped inside [activeArray].
     */
    fun meteringFor(
        point: FocusPoint,
        activeArray: Rect,
        sensorOrientation: Int,
        viewfinderW: Int,
        viewfinderH: Int
    ): Array<MeteringRectangle> {
        val aw = activeArray.width()
        val ah = activeArray.height()
        if (aw <= 0 || ah <= 0) {
            return arrayOf(MeteringRectangle(Rect(0, 0, 0, 0), METERING_WEIGHT))
        }

        // (1) visible (aspect-cropped) window of the active array.
        val vfAspect = if (viewfinderH > 0) {
            viewfinderW.toFloat() / viewfinderH.toFloat()
        } else {
            Models.VIEWFINDER_WIDTH.toFloat() / Models.VIEWFINDER_HEIGHT.toFloat()
        }
        val arrayAspect = aw.toFloat() / ah.toFloat()
        var visW: Float
        var visH: Float
        if (arrayAspect > vfAspect) {
            visH = ah.toFloat()
            visW = ah.toFloat() * vfAspect
        } else {
            visW = aw.toFloat()
            visH = aw.toFloat() / vfAspect
        }
        if (visW <= 0f || visH <= 0f) {
            visW = aw.toFloat()
            visH = ah.toFloat()
        }
        val visLeft = (aw - visW) * 0.5f
        val visTop = (ah - visH) * 0.5f

        // (2) display -> sensor rotation (viewfinder is shown upright; undo the mount rotation).
        val nx = point.normalizedX.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
        val ny = point.normalizedY.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
        val sx: Float
        val sy: Float
        when (((sensorOrientation % 360) + 360) % 360) {
            90 -> { sx = ny; sy = 1f - nx }
            180 -> { sx = 1f - nx; sy = 1f - ny }
            270 -> { sx = 1f - ny; sy = nx }
            else -> { sx = nx; sy = ny }
        }
        val cx = visLeft + sx * visW
        val cy = visTop + sy * visH

        // (3) tap region ≈ 1/8 of the visible window (half-extent = 1/16).
        val halfW = (visW / 16f).coerceAtLeast(1f)
        val halfH = (visH / 16f).coerceAtLeast(1f)
        val left = (cx - halfW).toInt().coerceIn(activeArray.left, activeArray.right - 1)
        val top = (cy - halfH).toInt().coerceIn(activeArray.top, activeArray.bottom - 1)
        val right = (cx + halfW).toInt().coerceIn(left + 1, activeArray.right)
        val bottom = (cy + halfH).toInt().coerceIn(top + 1, activeArray.bottom)
        return arrayOf(MeteringRectangle(Rect(left, top, right, bottom), METERING_WEIGHT))
    }

    /**
     * Arm a tap-focus cycle onto [builder] and record the internal START phase. CameraEngine calls
     * this from the camera thread right before pushing the repeating request; the next request
     * built ≥150 ms later resets the trigger to IDLE (see [apply]).
     */
    fun startTapFocus(point: FocusPoint, activeArray: Rect, sensorOrientation: Int, builder: CaptureRequest.Builder) {
        val regions = meteringFor(point, activeArray, sensorOrientation, Models.VIEWFINDER_WIDTH, Models.VIEWFINDER_HEIGHT)
        tapRegions = regions
        tapStartedAtMs = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
        phase = Phase.TAP_START

        builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_MODE, CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_MODE_AUTO)
        builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_TRIGGER, CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_TRIGGER_START)
        builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_REGIONS, regions)
        // AE follows the tap ROI (stage3 §2.6 C()); skipped under manual exposure to protect the
        // stealth manual-exposure law (AE_MODE_OFF must not be flipped by a focus tap).
        if (!settings.manualExposure) {
            builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_MODE, CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_MODE_ON)
            builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_LOCK, false)
            builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_PRECAPTURE_TRIGGER, CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_PRECAPTURE_TRIGGER_START)
            builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_REGIONS, regions)
        }
        builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.NOISE_REDUCTION_MODE, CaptureRequest.NOISE_REDUCTION_MODE_FAST)
    }

    /**
     * Explicit reset to continuous autofocus: AF_MODE_CONTINUOUS_PICTURE, AF_TRIGGER_IDLE and the
     * zero-weight sentinel region. Internal state is reset too, so the next [apply] keeps CAF.
     */
    fun restoreContinuous(builder: CaptureRequest.Builder) {
        phase = Phase.CONTINUOUS
        tapRegions = null
        builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_MODE, CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_MODE_CONTINUOUS_PICTURE)
        builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_TRIGGER, CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_TRIGGER_IDLE)
        builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_REGIONS, ZERO_REGION)
        builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_PRECAPTURE_TRIGGER, CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_PRECAPTURE_TRIGGER_IDLE)
    }

    /**
     * Base application for every rebuilt repeating request.
     *  - NOISE_REDUCTION_MODE_FAST always (M14 hard rule).
     *  - TAP_START (fresh tap, <150 ms): re-asserts AF_MODE_AUTO + AF_TRIGGER_START + ROI.
     *  - TAP_ACTIVE: AF_MODE_AUTO + AF_TRIGGER_IDLE + ROI (HAL converges and locks).
     *  - CONTINUOUS: AF_MODE_CONTINUOUS_PICTURE + AF regions from pending F-markers, else the
     *    zero-weight sentinel region.
     */
    fun apply(builder: CaptureRequest.Builder, activeArray: Rect?, sensorOrientation: Int) {
        builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.NOISE_REDUCTION_MODE, CaptureRequest.NOISE_REDUCTION_MODE_FAST)

        if (phase == Phase.TAP_START && SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() - tapStartedAtMs >= AF_TRIGGER_IDLE_DELAY_MS) {
            phase = Phase.TAP_ACTIVE
        }

        when (phase) {
            Phase.TAP_START -> {
                builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_MODE, CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_MODE_AUTO)
                builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_TRIGGER, CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_TRIGGER_START)
                tapRegions?.let {
                    builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_REGIONS, it)
                    if (!settings.manualExposure) builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_REGIONS, it)
                }
                if (!settings.manualExposure) {
                    builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_MODE, CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_MODE_ON)
                    builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_LOCK, false)
                    builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_PRECAPTURE_TRIGGER, CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_PRECAPTURE_TRIGGER_START)
                }
            }
            Phase.TAP_ACTIVE -> {
                if (SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() - tapStartedAtMs >= AF_TAP_HOLD_MS) {
                    // Tap hold expired — never wedge CAF off indefinitely.
                    phase = Phase.CONTINUOUS
                    tapRegions = null
                    applyContinuous(builder, activeArray, sensorOrientation)
                } else {
                    builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_MODE, CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_MODE_AUTO)
                    builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_TRIGGER, CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_TRIGGER_IDLE)
                    builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_PRECAPTURE_TRIGGER, CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_PRECAPTURE_TRIGGER_IDLE)
                    tapRegions?.let {
                        builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_REGIONS, it)
                        if (!settings.manualExposure) builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_REGIONS, it)
                    }
                }
            }
            Phase.CONTINUOUS -> applyContinuous(builder, activeArray, sensorOrientation)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Feed CaptureResult.CONTROL_AF_STATE (called by CameraEngine from its capture callback).
     *
     * @return true when the controller changed phase and the caller must refresh the repeating
     *         request (converged during START → flip trigger to IDLE; failed lock → restore CAF).
     */
    fun onAfResult(afState: Int): Boolean {
        return when (afState) {
            CaptureResult.CONTROL_AF_STATE_FOCUSED_LOCKED ->
                if (phase == Phase.TAP_START) {
                    phase = Phase.TAP_ACTIVE
                    true
                } else {
                    false
                }
            CaptureResult.CONTROL_AF_STATE_NOT_FOCUSED_LOCKED ->
                if (phase != Phase.CONTINUOUS) {
                    phase = Phase.CONTINUOUS
                    tapRegions = null
                    true
                } else {
                    false
                }
            else -> false
        }
    }

    private fun applyContinuous(builder: CaptureRequest.Builder, activeArray: Rect?, sensorOrientation: Int) {
        builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_MODE, CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_MODE_CONTINUOUS_PICTURE)
        builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_TRIGGER, CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_TRIGGER_IDLE)
        builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_PRECAPTURE_TRIGGER, CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_PRECAPTURE_TRIGGER_IDLE)
        val regions = markerRegions(activeArray, sensorOrientation)
        if (regions != null) {
            builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_REGIONS, regions)
            if (!settings.manualExposure) builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_REGIONS, regions)
        } else {
            builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AF_REGIONS, ZERO_REGION)
        }
    }

    /** Metering rectangles for every set F-marker (settings.focusMarkers), or null when none. */
    private fun markerRegions(activeArray: Rect?, sensorOrientation: Int): Array<MeteringRectangle>? {
        if (activeArray == null) return null
        val points = ArrayList<FocusPoint>(3)
        for (marker in settings.focusMarkers) {
            if (marker != null && marker.size >= 2) points.add(FocusPoint(marker[0], marker[1]))
        }
        if (points.isEmpty()) return null
        val out = ArrayList<MeteringRectangle>(points.size)
        for (p in points) {
            out.addAll(
                meteringFor(p, activeArray, sensorOrientation, Models.VIEWFINDER_WIDTH, Models.VIEWFINDER_HEIGHT)
            )
        }
        return out.toTypedArray()
    }
}

/** Sanitized request-key law: a rejected key must never crash request construction. */
private fun <T> CaptureRequest.Builder.guardedSet(key: CaptureRequest.Key<T>, value: T) {
    try {
        set(key, value)
    } catch (e: IllegalArgumentException) {
        Log.w(TAG, "request key rejected by platform: ${key.name}", e)
    }
}

==> m14_cinepro/app/src/main/java/com/m14/cinepro/camera/WhiteBalanceController.kt <==
package com.m14.cinepro.camera

import android.hardware.camera2.CaptureRequest
import android.hardware.camera2.CaptureResult
import android.hardware.camera2.TotalCaptureResult
import android.hardware.camera2.params.ColorSpaceTransform
import android.util.Log
import android.util.Rational
import com.m14.cinepro.color.ColorScience
import com.m14.cinepro.core.Models
import com.m14.cinepro.core.SettingsStore

private const val TAG = "M14Cam"

/**
 * M14 CinePro — white-balance control (stage3_camera2.md §2.5 + §3).
 *
 * Preset → CONTROL_AWB_MODE map:
 *   AUTO→1, DAYLIGHT→5, CLOUDY_DAYLIGHT→6, INCANDESCENT→2, FLUORESCENT→3, KELVIN→0 (AWB OFF).
 *
 * KELVIN path (§2.5 s() + §3 dt0 math): AWB_MODE_OFF, AWB_LOCK=false,
 *   COLOR_CORRECTION_MODE_CANCEL(0), then gains = ColorScience.kelvinToRggb(kelvin, tint, ccm);
 *   when non-null: COLOR_CORRECTION_GAINS = gains and COLOR_CORRECTION_TRANSFORM = the CCM as a
 *   ColorSpaceTransform built from Rational(round(f*1000), 1000). When the gain computation
 *   overflows (null result) the previously applied gains stay untouched — ground-truth law.
 *
 * Auto path (§2.5 l()): AWB_MODE = preset value, AWB_LOCK = settings.awbLock,
 *   COLOR_CORRECTION_MODE_FAST(1).
 *
 * The measured CCM (§3.7 rh0) is cached from TotalCaptureResult.COLOR_CORRECTION_TRANSFORM while
 * the device AWB is running (CONTROL_AWB_MODE != OFF), so manual Kelvin gain generation always
 * uses the freshest per-lens color matrix.
 */
class WhiteBalanceController(private val settings: SettingsStore) {

    private val ccmLock = Any()

    /** Last AWB-measured camera color matrix (row-major 3x3 floats); identity until measured. */
    private var measuredCcm: FloatArray = ColorScience.identityCcm()

    /** Preset → Camera2 CONTROL_AWB_MODE value. */
    fun awbCamera2Mode(): Int = when (settings.wbPreset) {
        Models.WbPreset.AUTO -> CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AWB_MODE_AUTO                     // 1
        Models.WbPreset.DAYLIGHT -> CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AWB_MODE_DAYLIGHT            // 5
        Models.WbPreset.CLOUDY_DAYLIGHT -> CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AWB_MODE_CLOUDY_DAYLIGHT // 6
        Models.WbPreset.INCANDESCENT -> CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AWB_MODE_INCANDESCENT    // 2
        Models.WbPreset.FLUORESCENT -> CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AWB_MODE_FLUORESCENT      // 3
        Models.WbPreset.KELVIN -> CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AWB_MODE_OFF                   // 0
    }

    /**
     * Apply the WB state onto [builder]. [ccm] is the CCM used for the Kelvin gain math — pass
     * [currentCcm] (CameraEngine forwards its cached value each rebuild).
     */
    fun apply(builder: CaptureRequest.Builder, ccm: FloatArray) {
        if (settings.wbPreset == Models.WbPreset.KELVIN) {
            builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AWB_MODE, CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AWB_MODE_OFF)
            builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AWB_LOCK, false)
            // stage3 §2.5 s(): COLOR_CORRECTION_MODE = 0 ("CANCEL" in the BMD smali; the public
            // API-34 constant for value 0 is COLOR_CORRECTION_MODE_TRANSFORM_MATRIX — the raw
            // metadata value 0 is the operative law, so it is written literally).
            builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.COLOR_CORRECTION_MODE, 0)

            val matrix = if (ccm.size == 9) ccm else ColorScience.identityCcm()
            val gains = try {
                ColorScience.kelvinToRggb(
                    settings.kelvin.coerceIn(Models.KELVIN_MIN, Models.KELVIN_MAX),
                    settings.tint.coerceIn(Models.TINT_MIN, Models.TINT_MAX),
                    matrix
                )
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.w(TAG, "kelvin->gains computation failed", e)
                null
            }
            if (gains != null) {
                builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.COLOR_CORRECTION_GAINS, gains)
                ccmToTransform(matrix)?.let {
                    builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.COLOR_CORRECTION_TRANSFORM, it)
                }
            }
            // gains == null → leave COLOR_CORRECTION_GAINS untouched (keep last applied gains).
        } else {
            builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AWB_MODE, awbCamera2Mode())
            builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AWB_LOCK, settings.awbLock)
            builder.guardedSet(CaptureRequest.COLOR_CORRECTION_MODE, CaptureRequest.COLOR_CORRECTION_MODE_FAST)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Cache the AWB-measured CCM from a capture result (stage3 §3.7): only while the device AWB is
     * running (CONTROL_AWB_MODE != OFF), so Kelvin-mode injections never pollute the cache.
     */
    fun onCaptureResult(result: TotalCaptureResult) {
        val awbMode = try {
            result.get(CaptureResult.CONTROL_AWB_MODE)
        } catch (t: Throwable) {
            null
        } ?: return
        if (awbMode == CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AWB_MODE_OFF) return

        val transform = try {
            result.get(CaptureResult.COLOR_CORRECTION_TRANSFORM)
        } catch (t: Throwable) {
            null
        } ?: return

        val floats = transformToFloats(transform) ?: return
        synchronized(ccmLock) {
            if (!floats.contentEquals(measuredCcm)) measuredCcm = floats
        }
    }

    /** Copy of the currently cached measured CCM (identity until the HAL publishes one). */
    fun currentCcm(): FloatArray = synchronized(ccmLock) { measuredCcm.copyOf() }

    /**
     * Normalized [r, 1, b] shader gains for the GL viewfinder WB trim: identity while any AWB
     * preset runs (the HAL has already white-balanced the preview), ColorScience-derived
     * green-normalized gains for the Kelvin path.
     */
    fun normalizedShaderGains(): FloatArray {
        if (settings.wbPreset != Models.WbPreset.KELVIN) return floatArrayOf(1f, 1f, 1f)
        return ColorScience.kelvinToNormalizedRgbGains(
            settings.kelvin.coerceIn(Models.KELVIN_MIN, Models.KELVIN_MAX),
            settings.tint.coerceIn(Models.TINT_MIN, Models.TINT_MAX),
            currentCcm()
        )
    }

    /**
     * ColorSpaceTransform from a row-major 3x3 float matrix via Rational(round(f*1000), 1000)
     * (equivalent to the 18-int num/den packed form). Null when the matrix is malformed.
     */
    private fun ccmToTransform(ccm: FloatArray): ColorSpaceTransform? {
        if (ccm.size != 9) return null
        return try {
            ColorSpaceTransform(Array(9) { i -> Rational(Math.round(ccm[i] * 1000f), 1000) })
        } catch (e: IllegalArgumentException) {
            Log.w(TAG, "CCM transform build failed", e)
            null
        }
    }

    /** Extract 9 floats from a ColorSpaceTransform (Rational num/den pairs → float). */
    private fun transformToFloats(transform: ColorSpaceTransform): FloatArray? {
        return try {
            val packed = IntArray(18)
            transform.copyElements(packed, 0)
            FloatArray(9) { i ->
                val den = packed[i * 2 + 1]
                if (den == 0) 0f else packed[i * 2].toFloat() / den.toFloat()
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.w(TAG, "CCM result read failed", e)
            null
        }
    }
}

/** Sanitized request-key law: a rejected key must never crash request construction. */
private fun <T> CaptureRequest.Builder.guardedSet(key: CaptureRequest.Key<T>, value: T) {
    try {
        set(key, value)
    } catch (e: IllegalArgumentException) {
        Log.w(TAG, "request key rejected by platform: ${key.name}", e)
    }
}

==> m14_cinepro/app/src/main/java/com/m14/cinepro/color/ColorScience.kt <==
package com.m14.cinepro.color

import android.hardware.camera2.params.RggbChannelVector
import kotlin.math.abs
import kotlin.math.exp
import kotlin.math.ln
import kotlin.math.sqrt

/**
 * M14 CinePro — exact clean-room port of the Blackmagic color-science math
 * (`dt0.java` + `ColorScienceUtils` + `libcolor_science.so`, reconstructed verbatim in
 * docs/stage3_camera2.md section 3).
 *
 * Law set (do not "improve" any constant — these are the verified ground truth):
 *  - Planckian locus (CIE 1960 UCS), rational polynomials in T:
 *      u_p(T) = (1.28641212e-7·T² + 1.54118254e-4·T + 0.860117757) / (7.08145163e-7·T² + 8.42420235e-4·T + 1.0)
 *      v_p(T) = (4.20481691e-8·T² + 4.22806245e-5·T + 0.317398726) / (1.61456053e-7·T² − 2.89741816e-5·T + 1.0)
 *  - CIE 1960 -> CIE 1931: x = 3u/(2u−8v+4), y = 2v/(2u−8v+4); white X = x/y, Z = (1−x−y)/y.
 *  - XYZ -> linear Rec.709 via the exact sRGB/Rec.709 matrix (Y normalized to 1).
 *  - Tint = green-gain offset around the geometric mean: gG = sqrt(gR·gB)·e^(−(tint+130)/200),
 *    deviceTintOffset = −130 (hard-coded JNI arg in the ground truth).
 *  - Gains normalized so min(gR,gG,gB) >= 1.
 *  - 10-iteration mired refinement loop (98% error correction, mired clamped [40,1000]).
 *  - Reverse CCT: golden-section search, 30 iterations, invphi = 0.618033988749895, mired [40,1000],
 *    minimizing squared uv distance to the locus; degenerate chromaticity falls back to 5000 K.
 *  - Reverse tint: tint = clamp(−200·ln(gG / sqrt(gR·gB)) − 130, −50, +50); the 0.72 green sensor
 *    baseline factor applies ONLY to the CCT estimate input, never to the tint formula.
 */
object ColorScience {

    private const val INVPHI = 0.618033988749895
    private const val MIRED_MIN = 40.0
    private const val MIRED_MAX = 1000.0
    private const val GREEN_BASELINE = 0.72f
    const val DEVICE_TINT_OFFSET = -130.0f

    /** Identity 3x3 CCM (row-major), used when the HAL has not published a measured matrix. */
    fun identityCcm(): FloatArray = floatArrayOf(
        1f, 0f, 0f,
        0f, 1f, 0f,
        0f, 0f, 1f
    )

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Planckian locus — CIE 1960 UCS rational polynomials (kz2.p(T2, a, b, c) = c / (a·T2 + b))
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    private fun planckianU(T: Double): Double {
        val T2 = T * T
        val num = (1.28641212e-7 * T2) + (1.54118254e-4 * T) + 0.860117757
        val den = (7.08145163e-7 * T2) + (8.42420235e-4 * T) + 1.0
        return num / den
    }

    private fun planckianV(T: Double): Double {
        val T2 = T * T
        val num = (4.20481691e-8 * T2) + (4.22806245e-5 * T) + 0.317398726
        val den = (1.61456053e-7 * T2) - (2.89741816e-5 * T) + 1.0
        return num / den
    }

    /** Squared uv distance to the Planckian locus at the given mired (dt0.f). */
    private fun locusDistance(u: Double, v: Double, mired: Double): Double {
        val T = 1_000_000.0 / mired
        val du = u - planckianU(T)
        val dv = v - planckianV(T)
        return du * du + dv * dv
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // FORWARD: (kelvin, tint, ccm) -> RggbChannelVector   (dt0.d)
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    /**
     * Compute camera white-balance gains for a Kelvin/Tint pair.
     *
     * @param kelvin target correlated color temperature, 2500..10000 K (UI law, stage3 §7d)
     * @param tint   green-magenta axis, −50..+50
     * @param ccm    row-major 3x3 camera color matrix (measured CCM preferred, else identity)
     * @return gains with both greens equal, normalized so the minimum gain >= 1,
     *         or null when a gain overflows (the caller keeps its previous gains — ground-truth law).
     */
    fun kelvinToRggb(kelvin: Int, tint: Int, ccm: FloatArray): RggbChannelVector? {
        val inv = inverse3x3(ccm)
        val fExp = exp(-((tint - DEVICE_TINT_OFFSET) / 200.0f)).toFloat()
        val targetMired = 1_000_000.0 / kelvin
        var mired = targetMired
        var gR = 1f
        var gG = 1f
        var gB = 1f
        for (i in 0 until 10) {
            val T = 1_000_000.0 / mired
            val uP = planckianU(T)
            val vP = planckianV(T)
            val den = uP * 2.0 - vP * 8.0 + 4.0
            val x = ((uP * 3.0) / den).toFloat()
            val y = ((vP * 2.0) / den).toFloat()
            val X = x / y
            val Z = (1f - x - y) / y
            // XYZ -> linear Rec.709 RGB, rows of the exact matrix with Y normalized to 1.
            val wr = 3.2406f * X + (-1.5372f) + (-0.4986f * Z)
            val wg = (-0.9689f) * X + 1.8758f + (0.0415f * Z)
            val wb = 0.0557f * X + (-0.2040f) + (1.0570f * Z)
            // CCM^-1 applied to the white RGB: the sensor raw values that map to neutral white.
            val rReq = 1.0f / ((inv[2] * wb) + (inv[1] * wg) + (inv[0] * wr))
            val bReq = 1.0f / ((inv[8] * wb) + (inv[7] * wg) + (inv[6] * wr))
            val gGM = sqrt(rReq * bReq) * fExp
            val minGain = minOf(rReq, gGM, bReq)
            val k = if (minGain < 1.0f) 1.0f / minGain else 1.0f
            gR = rReq * k
            gG = gGM * k
            gB = bReq * k
            val kelvinAchieved = cctEstimate(1.0f / gR, (1.0f / gG) * GREEN_BASELINE, 1.0f / gB, ccm)
            if (abs(kelvin - kelvinAchieved) < 0.1) break
            val correction = mired + ((targetMired - (1_000_000.0 / kelvinAchieved)) * 0.98)
            mired = correction.coerceIn(MIRED_MIN, MIRED_MAX)
        }
        if (abs(gR) > Float.MAX_VALUE || abs(gG) > Float.MAX_VALUE || abs(gB) > Float.MAX_VALUE ||
            gR.isNaN() || gG.isNaN() || gB.isNaN()) {
            return null
        }
        return RggbChannelVector(gR, gG, gG, gB)
    }

    /**
     * Same forward math, returned as green-normalized RGB multipliers for the viewfinder
     * shader trim (wbGains uniform) and for the live Kelvin/Tint HUD readout.
     */
    fun kelvinToNormalizedRgbGains(kelvin: Int, tint: Int, ccm: FloatArray): FloatArray {
        val v = kelvinToRggb(kelvin, tint, ccm)
        return if (v == null) {
            floatArrayOf(1f, 1f, 1f)
        } else {
            floatArrayOf(v.red / v.greenEven, 1f, v.blue / v.greenEven)
        }
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // REVERSE: (gains, ccm) -> (kelvin, tint)   (dt0.e + dt0.b)
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    /**
     * CCT estimator (dt0.e): CCM applied to reciprocal gains, linear RGB -> XYZ (Rec.709,
     * 0.4124/0.3576/0.1805 | 0.2126/0.7152/0.0722 | 0.0193/0.1192/0.9505), CIE 1960 UCS
     * u = 4X/(X+15Y+3Z), v = 6Y/(X+15Y+3Z), then a golden-section minimization of the squared
     * uv distance to the Planckian locus over mired [40,1000] with 30 iterations.
     */
    fun cctEstimate(r: Float, g: Float, b: Float, ccm: FloatArray): Int {
        val rr = ccm[0] * r + ccm[1] * g + ccm[2] * b
        val gg = ccm[3] * r + ccm[4] * g + ccm[5] * b
        val bb = ccm[6] * r + ccm[7] * g + ccm[8] * b
        val X = 0.4124f * rr + 0.3576f * gg + 0.1805f * bb
        val Y = 0.2126f * rr + 0.7152f * gg + 0.0722f * bb
        val d = 3f * (0.0193f * rr + 0.1192f * gg + 0.9505f * bb) + 15f * Y + X
        if (d == 0f) return 5000 // DNSConstants.PROBE_THROTTLE_COUNT_INTERVAL sentinel
        val u = (4f * X).toDouble() / d.toDouble()
        val v = (6f * Y).toDouble() / d.toDouble()
        var lo = MIRED_MIN
        var hi = MIRED_MAX
        for (i in 0 until 30) {
            val c = hi - INVPHI * (hi - lo)
            val dd = lo + INVPHI * (hi - lo)
            val fc = locusDistance(u, v, c)
            val fd = locusDistance(u, v, dd)
            if (fc < fd) hi = dd else lo = c
        }
        return (1_000_000.0 / ((lo + hi) / 2.0)).toInt()
    }

    /**
     * Read-back (dt0.b): gains + CCM -> (kelvin, tint).
     * green input to CCT = (1 / mean(green gains)) * 0.72 sensor baseline;
     * tint = clamp(−200·ln(gG / sqrt(gR·gB)) − 130, −50, +50) on the RAW gains.
     */
    fun rggbToKelvinTint(gains: RggbChannelVector, ccm: FloatArray): Pair<Int, Int> {
        val red = 1.0f / gains.red
        val blue = 1.0f / gains.blue
        val greenMean = (gains.greenOdd + gains.greenEven) * 0.5f
        val green = if (gains.greenEven > 0f) (1.0f / greenMean) * GREEN_BASELINE else 0.0f
        val kelvin = cctEstimate(red, green, blue, ccm)
        var tint = 0
        if (gains.red > 0f && gains.blue > 0f && gains.greenEven > 0f) {
            val ratio = greenMean / sqrt(gains.blue * gains.red).toDouble()
            // Exact ground-truth formula: tint = clamp((int)(−ln(gG/√(gR·gB))·200) − 130, −50, +50)
            tint = ((-ln(ratio).toFloat() * 200.0f).toInt() - 130)
                .coerceIn(-50, 50)
        }
        return Pair(kelvin, tint)
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 3x3 inverse (dt0.java:61-96) — standard adjugate/determinant, identity on det==0.
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    fun inverse3x3(m: FloatArray): FloatArray {
        val a = m[0]; val b = m[1]; val c = m[2]
        val d = m[3]; val e = m[4]; val f = m[5]
        val g = m[6]; val h = m[7]; val i = m[8]
        val A = e * i - f * h
        val B = -(d * i - f * g)
        val C = d * h - e * g
        val det = a * A + b * B + c * C
        if (det == 0f || det.isNaN() || det.isInfinite()) return identityCcm()
        val invDet = 1.0f / det
        return floatArrayOf(
            A * invDet, B * invDet, C * invDet,
            -(b * i - c * h) * invDet, (a * i - c * g) * invDet, -(a * h - b * g) * invDet,
            (b * f - c * e) * invDet, -(a * f - c * d) * invDet, (a * e - b * d) * invDet
        )
    }
}

==> m14_cinepro/app/src/main/java/com/m14/cinepro/core/EngineListener.kt <==
package com.m14.cinepro.core

import java.nio.ByteBuffer

/**
 * Callback surface from the engine subsystems up to the HUD / MainActivity.
 * All methods are invoked on main-thread-safe paths unless noted otherwise.
 */
interface EngineListener {

    /** Recording started. [clipBaseName] is the file base name (no extension) of this take. */
    fun onRecordingStarted(clipBaseName: String)

    /** Recording stopped; [durationMs] is wall-clock take length, [frameCount] encoded frames. */
    fun onRecordingStopped(clipBaseName: String, durationMs: Long, frameCount: Long)

    /** Hard engine failure. The HUD shows a toast; recording is stopped safely if active. */
    fun onError(stage: String, throwable: Throwable)

    /** Transient info line (e.g. "AF locked", "LUT imported", "50 Hz flicker detected"). */
    fun onInfo(message: String)

    /** Per-frame encoded frame counter for the timecode pill (called ~10 Hz from engine). */
    fun onFrameCount(frameIndex: Long, fps: Int)

    /** Stereo audio levels in dBFS, one call per audio buffer (~93 Hz at 512-frame reads). */
    fun onAudioLevels(peakLeftDb: Float, rmsLeftDb: Float, peakRightDb: Float, rmsRightDb: Float)

    /**
     * 64-bin RGB histogram from the GLES 3.1 compute pass at 10 fps.
     * Buffer holds 192 little-endian uint32 counts: R=[0,64) G=[64,128) B=[128,192).
     * The buffer is a fresh copy owned by the receiver.
     */
    fun onHistogram(bins: ByteBuffer)

    /** Camera2 CaptureResult telemetry (called at >= 3 Hz). */
    fun onCameraTelemetry(aeState: Int, afState: Int, sceneFlicker: Int)

    /** Battery %, SoC/battery temperature in °C, thermal headroom, free GB on the record volume. */
    fun onSystemStatus(batteryPct: Int, tempC: Float, thermalHeadroom: Float, freeGb: Float)
}

==> m14_cinepro/app/src/main/java/com/m14/cinepro/core/Models.kt <==
package com.m14.cinepro.core

import android.content.Context
import android.content.SharedPreferences

/**
 * M14 CinePro — central domain model + persistent settings store.
 *
 * This file is the shared contract between the camera engine, the GL viewfinder, the recording
 * pipeline and the HUD. All values follow the empirical law of:
 *   docs/M14_VERIFIED_TRUTH.md, docs/M14_SYSTEM_STRESS_REPORT.md,
 *   docs/stage3_camera2.md, docs/stage4_shaders.md, docs/stage5_media.md
 *
 * Thread-safety: every setter writes through to SharedPreferences on a background executor and
 * mutates the in-memory field synchronously. UI + engine threads read the in-memory field.
 */
object Models {

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Enums
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    /** Recording formats verified on SM-M146B (M14_VERIFIED_TRUTH.md §4). */
    enum class ResPreset(val width: Int, val height: Int, val fps: Int, val label: String, val highSpeed: Boolean) {
        UHD_4K_24(3840, 2160, 24, "4K 24", false),
        UHD_4K_30(3840, 2160, 30, "4K 30", false),
        QHD_25K_24(2560, 1440, 24, "2.5K 24", false),
        QHD_25K_30(2560, 1440, 30, "2.5K 30", false),
        FHD_1080_24(1920, 1080, 24, "1080p 24", false),
        FHD_1080_30(1920, 1080, 30, "1080p 30", false),
        HD_720_60_HS(1280, 720, 60, "720p 60 Slow-Mo", true)
    }

    /** Bitrate selector (stage5_media.md §2.4; Exynos MFC max verified 81 Mbps at 4K). */
    enum class BitratePreset(val bps: Int, val label: String) {
        M80(80_000_000, "80M"),
        M48(48_000_000, "48M"),
        M36(36_000_000, "36M"),
        M24(24_000_000, "24M")
    }

    enum class ShutterMode { ANGLE, SPEED }

    enum class AntiBandingMode(val camera2Value: Int) {
        OFF(android.hardware.camera2.CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_ANTIBANDING_MODE_OFF),
        FIFTY_HZ(android.hardware.camera2.CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_ANTIBANDING_MODE_50HZ),
        SIXTY_HZ(android.hardware.camera2.CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_ANTIBANDING_MODE_60HZ),
        AUTO(android.hardware.camera2.CaptureRequest.CONTROL_AE_ANTIBANDING_MODE_AUTO)
    }

    /** Focus peaking render mode (stage4_shaders.md §2). */
    enum class PeakingMode { OFF, COLORED_LINES, PEAK }

    enum class PeakingColor(val rgba: FloatArray) {
        RED(floatArrayOf(1f, 0f, 0f, 1f)),
        GREEN(floatArrayOf(0f, 1f, 0.2f, 1f)),
        BLUE(floatArrayOf(0.2f, 0.6f, 1f, 1f)),
        YELLOW(floatArrayOf(1f, 0.9f, 0.1f, 1f)),
        WHITE(floatArrayOf(1f, 1f, 1f, 1f));
        companion object { fun fromName(n: String?): PeakingColor = entries.firstOrNull { it.name == n } ?: RED }
    }

    /** Zebra display mode (stage4_shaders.md §4; mid-grey bands are 10-bit Y code values /1024). */
    enum class ZebraMode { OFF, Z95, Z90, MID_GREY, MID_GREY_PLUS1 }

    /** Built-in 33^3 LUTs + user .cube import (stage4_shaders.md §7). */
    enum class LutPreset { OFF, NEUTRAL_709, TEAL_ORANGE, WARM_FADE, MONO_CONTRAST, IMPORTED }

    enum class WbPreset { AUTO, DAYLIGHT, CLOUDY_DAYLIGHT, INCANDESCENT, FLUORESCENT, KELVIN }

    /** Aspect frame guides. */
    enum class GuidePreset(val ratio: Float, val label: String) {
        OFF(0f, "Off"),
        R_2_39(2.39f, "2.39:1"),
        R_2_00(2.00f, "2.00:1"),
        R_1_85(1.85f, "1.85:1"),
        R_4_3(4f / 3f, "4:3"),
        R_9_16(9f / 16f, "9:16")
    }

    enum class GridOverlay { NONE, THIRDS, CROSSHAIR, HORIZON }

    /** Anamorphic de-squeeze factors (stage4_shaders.md §9). */
    enum class DesqueezePreset(val factor: Float, val label: String) {
        X100(1.0f, "1.0x"),
        X133(1.33f, "1.33x"),
        X155(1.55f, "1.55x"),
        X200(2.0f, "2.0x")
    }

    /** VU/PPM meter reference (stage5_media.md §5.6 — AudioMetering enum). */
    enum class AudioMeteringMode(val refCentiDb: Int, val label: String) {
        VU_18DBFS(-1800, "VU -18"),
        VU_20DBFS(-2000, "VU -20"),
        PPM_18DBFS(-1800, "PPM -18"),
        PPM_20DBFS(-2000, "PPM -20")
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Numeric law (verified constants — do not tune)
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    /** 110 µs headroom snap: exposure must end 110 µs before the frame duration ends (stage3 §2.1). */
    const val EXPOSURE_HEADROOM_NS = 110_000L
    const val EXPOSURE_MIN_NS = 30_000L
    const val EXPOSURE_MAX_NS = 100_000_000L
    const val ISO_MIN = 100
    const val ISO_MAX = 3200
    const val KELVIN_MIN = 2500
    const val KELVIN_MAX = 10000
    const val TINT_MIN = -50
    const val TINT_MAX = 50
    const val EV_MIN = -2.0f
    const val EV_MAX = 2.0f

    /** Zebra threshold display-profile scale (stage4 §10.3: x0.93 for Rec.709 display). */
    const val ZEBRA_THRESHOLD_SCALE_709 = 0.93f
    /** Peaking strength UI -> shader scale (stage4 §10.3: peakingThreshold = UI * 0.04). */
    const val PEAKING_UI_SCALE = 0.04f
    const val PEAKING_DEFAULT_UI = 0.85f
    const val ZEBRA_WIDTH_PX = 8.0f
    const val ZEBRA_DEFAULT_UI_THRESHOLD = 0.75f

    /** Viewfinder is always 1920x1080 (Architecture A law). */
    const val VIEWFINDER_WIDTH = 1920
    const val VIEWFINDER_HEIGHT = 1080

    /** Gyro-EIS crop: 10% in (0.9 scale) for counter-rotation headroom. */
    const val EIS_CROP_SCALE = 0.9f

    /** Histogram: 64 bins x RGB, compute dispatch cadence 10 fps. */
    const val HISTOGRAM_BINS = 64
    const val HISTOGRAM_INTERVAL_MS = 100L

    /** Audio: 48 kHz stereo 16-bit PCM, AAC-LC 256 kbps (stage5_media.md §5). */
    const val AUDIO_SAMPLE_RATE = 48000
    const val AUDIO_BITRATE = 256_000

    /** Full-scale dB reference for 16-bit PCM: 20*log10(32767) = 90.308998 dB. */
    const val DBFS_REF_16BIT = 90.308998f

    fun shutterAnglePresets(): FloatArray = floatArrayOf(45f, 90f, 144f, 172.8f, 180f, 270f, 360f)
    fun shutterSpeedDenominators(): IntArray = intArrayOf(24, 48, 50, 60, 100, 120, 250, 500, 1000, 4000, 8000)

    /** Angle-invariant exposure law (stage3 §2.2): exposureNs = 1e9 * angle / (360 * fps). */
    fun exposureNsForAngle(angleDegrees: Float, fps: Int): Long =
        Math.round(1_000_000_000.0 * angleDegrees / (360.0 * fps))

    /** exposureNs for shutter speed 1/x s. */
    fun exposureNsForSpeed(denominator: Int): Long = 1_000_000_000L / denominator

    /**
     * Clamp + 110 µs headroom snap against the frame duration (stage3_camera2.md §2.1).
     * If (1e9/fps) <= exposure + 110_000 then exposure = (1e9/fps) - 110_000.
     */
    fun applyHeadroomSnap(exposureNs: Long, fps: Int): Long {
        val frameNs = 1_000_000_000L / fps
        val clamped = exposureNs.coerceIn(EXPOSURE_MIN_NS, EXPOSURE_MAX_NS)
        return if (frameNs <= clamped + EXPOSURE_HEADROOM_NS) {
            (frameNs - EXPOSURE_HEADROOM_NS).coerceAtLeast(EXPOSURE_MIN_NS)
        } else clamped
    }

    fun formatTimecode(totalFrames: Long, fps: Int): String {
        val f = (totalFrames % fps).toInt()
        val totalSeconds = totalFrames / fps
        val s = (totalSeconds % 60).toInt()
        val m = ((totalSeconds / 60) % 60).toInt()
        val h = totalSeconds / 3600
        return String.format("%02d:%02d:%02d:%02d", h, m, s, f)
    }
}

/**
 * Persistent + runtime settings holder. One instance owned by [com.m14.cinepro.MainActivity].
 */
class SettingsStore(context: Context) {

    private val prefs: SharedPreferences =
        context.getSharedPreferences("m14_cinepro_settings", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

    // ---- Recording format ----
    @Volatile var resolution: Models.ResPreset = Models.ResPreset.UHD_4K_24
        set(v) { field = v; persist("resolution", v.name) }
    @Volatile var bitrate: Models.BitratePreset = Models.BitratePreset.M80
        set(v) { field = v; persist("bitrate", v.name) }

    // ---- Exposure (angle-invariant law) ----
    @Volatile var shutterMode: Models.ShutterMode = Models.ShutterMode.ANGLE
        set(v) { field = v; persist("shutterMode", v.name) }
    @Volatile var shutterAngleDeg: Float = 180f
        set(v) { field = v; persistFloat("shutterAngleDeg", v) }
    @Volatile var shutterSpeedDenominator: Int = 48
        set(v) { field = v; persistInt("shutterSpeedDenominator", v) }
    @Volatile var iso: Int = 100
        set(v) { field = v; persistInt("iso", v) }
    @Volatile var manualExposure: Boolean = false
        set(v) { field = v; persist("manualExposure", v) }
    @Volatile var evCompensation: Float = 0f
        set(v) { field = v; persistFloat("ev", v) }
    @Volatile var aeLock: Boolean = false
        set(v) { field = v; persist("aeLock", v) }
    @Volatile var antiBanding: Models.AntiBandingMode = Models.AntiBandingMode.AUTO
        set(v) { field = v; persist("antiBanding", v.name) }
    /** Last detected STATISTICS_SCENE_FLICKER (0 none, 1 = 50 Hz, 2 = 60 Hz). */
    @Volatile var sceneFlicker: Int = 0

    // ---- White balance ----
    @Volatile var wbPreset: Models.WbPreset = Models.WbPreset.AUTO
        set(v) { field = v; persist("wbPreset", v.name) }
    @Volatile var kelvin: Int = 5600
        set(v) { field = v; persistInt("kelvin", v) }
    @Volatile var tint: Int = 0
        set(v) { field = v; persistInt("tint", v) }
    @Volatile var awbLock: Boolean = false
        set(v) { field = v; persist("awbLock", v) }

    // ---- Focus markers F1/F2/F3 (normalized viewfinder coords; null = unset) ----
    val focusMarkers: Array<FloatArray?> = arrayOfNulls(3)

    // ---- Viewfinder effects ----
    @Volatile var peakingMode: Models.PeakingMode = Models.PeakingMode.OFF
        set(v) { field = v; persist("peakingMode", v.name) }
    @Volatile var peakingStrength: Float = Models.PEAKING_DEFAULT_UI
        set(v) { field = v; persistFloat("peakingStrength", v) }
    @Volatile var peakingColor: Models.PeakingColor = Models.PeakingColor.RED
        set(v) { field = v; persist("peakingColor", v.name) }
    @Volatile var zebraMode: Models.ZebraMode = Models.ZebraMode.OFF
        set(v) { field = v; persist("zebraMode", v.name) }
    @Volatile var zebraThresholdUi: Float = Models.ZEBRA_DEFAULT_UI_THRESHOLD
        set(v) { field = v; persistFloat("zebraThresholdUi", v) }
    @Volatile var falseColorOn: Boolean = false
        set(v) { field = v; persist("falseColorOn", v) }
    @Volatile var lutPreset: Models.LutPreset = Models.LutPreset.OFF
        set(v) { field = v; persist("lutPreset", v.name) }
    @Volatile var importedLutPath: String? = null
        set(v) { field = v; persist("importedLutPath", v ?: "") }
    @Volatile var guide: Models.GuidePreset = Models.GuidePreset.OFF
        set(v) { field = v; persist("guide", v.name) }
    @Volatile var grid: Models.GridOverlay = Models.GridOverlay.NONE
        set(v) { field = v; persist("grid", v.name) }
    @Volatile var desqueeze: Models.DesqueezePreset = Models.DesqueezePreset.X100
        set(v) { field = v; persist("desqueeze", v.name) }

    // ---- Transport ----
    @Volatile var eisOn: Boolean = false
        set(v) { field = v; persist("eisOn", v) }
    @Volatile var torchOn: Boolean = false
        set(v) { field = v; persist("torchOn", v) }
    @Volatile var audioMetering: Models.AudioMeteringMode = Models.AudioMeteringMode.VU_18DBFS
        set(v) { field = v; persist("audioMetering", v.name) }

    // ---- Slate (stage5_media.md BmdMetadata registry) ----
    @Volatile var reel: Int = 1
        set(v) { field = v; persistInt("reel", v) }
    @Volatile var scene: String = "1"
