        set(v) { field = v; persist("scene", v) }
    @Volatile var take: Int = 1
        set(v) { field = v; persistInt("take", v) }
    @Volatile var goodTake: Boolean = false
        set(v) { field = v; persist("goodTake", v) }
    @Volatile var director: String = ""
        set(v) { field = v; persist("director", v) }
    @Volatile var operator: String = ""
        set(v) { field = v; persist("operator", v) }

    // ---- Convenience derivations (angle-invariant shutter law) ----

    val fps: Int get() = resolution.fps

    /** Current requested exposure in ns, per the active shutter mode. */
    fun requestedExposureNs(): Long = when (shutterMode) {
        Models.ShutterMode.ANGLE -> Models.exposureNsForAngle(shutterAngleDeg, fps)
        Models.ShutterMode.SPEED -> Models.exposureNsForSpeed(shutterSpeedDenominator)
    }

    /** Clamped + headroom-snapped exposure actually injected into the HAL. */
    fun effectiveExposureNs(): Long = Models.applyHeadroomSnap(requestedExposureNs(), fps)

    /** Shutter display string for the HUD pill. */
    fun shutterLabel(): String = when (shutterMode) {
        Models.ShutterMode.ANGLE -> String.format("%.1f°", shutterAngleDeg)
        Models.ShutterMode.SPEED -> "1/$shutterSpeedDenominator s"
    }

    fun isoLabel(): String = if (manualExposure) "ISO $iso" else "ISO AUTO"

    fun wbLabel(): String = when (wbPreset) {
        Models.WbPreset.AUTO -> "AWB"
        Models.WbPreset.KELVIN -> "${kelvin}K"
        Models.WbPreset.DAYLIGHT -> "Daylight"
        Models.WbPreset.CLOUDY_DAYLIGHT -> "Cloudy"
        Models.WbPreset.INCANDESCENT -> "Tungsten"
        Models.WbPreset.FLUORESCENT -> "Fluorescent"
    }

    /** Snapshot of the settings relevant to clip metadata / slate. */
    fun describeForMetadata(): String =
        "${resolution.label} ${fps}fps ${shutterLabel()} ${isoLabel()} ${wbLabel()} LUT=${lutPreset.name}"

    // ---- Persistence helpers ----

    private fun persist(key: String, value: String) = prefs.edit().putString(key, value).apply()
    private fun persistInt(key: String, value: Int) = prefs.edit().putInt(key, value).apply()
    private fun persistFloat(key: String, value: Float) = prefs.edit().putFloat(key, value).apply()
    private fun persist(key: String, value: Boolean) = prefs.edit().putBoolean(key, value).apply()

    init {
        fun str(k: String, d: String) = prefs.getString(k, d) ?: d
        resolution = runCatching { Models.ResPreset.valueOf(str("resolution", "UHD_4K_24")) }
            .getOrDefault(Models.ResPreset.UHD_4K_24)
        bitrate = runCatching { Models.BitratePreset.valueOf(str("bitrate", "M80")) }
            .getOrDefault(Models.BitratePreset.M80)
        shutterMode = runCatching { Models.ShutterMode.valueOf(str("shutterMode", "ANGLE")) }
            .getOrDefault(Models.ShutterMode.ANGLE)
        shutterAngleDeg = prefs.getFloat("shutterAngleDeg", 180f)
        shutterSpeedDenominator = prefs.getInt("shutterSpeedDenominator", 48)
        iso = prefs.getInt("iso", 100)
        manualExposure = prefs.getBoolean("manualExposure", false)
        evCompensation = prefs.getFloat("ev", 0f)
        aeLock = prefs.getBoolean("aeLock", false)
        antiBanding = runCatching { Models.AntiBandingMode.valueOf(str("antiBanding", "AUTO")) }
            .getOrDefault(Models.AntiBandingMode.AUTO)
        wbPreset = runCatching { Models.WbPreset.valueOf(str("wbPreset", "AUTO")) }
            .getOrDefault(Models.WbPreset.AUTO)
        kelvin = prefs.getInt("kelvin", 5600)
        tint = prefs.getInt("tint", 0)
        peakingMode = runCatching { Models.PeakingMode.valueOf(str("peakingMode", "OFF")) }
            .getOrDefault(Models.PeakingMode.OFF)
        peakingStrength = prefs.getFloat("peakingStrength", Models.PEAKING_DEFAULT_UI)
        peakingColor = Models.PeakingColor.fromName(str("peakingColor", "RED"))
        zebraMode = runCatching { Models.ZebraMode.valueOf(str("zebraMode", "OFF")) }
            .getOrDefault(Models.ZebraMode.OFF)
        zebraThresholdUi = prefs.getFloat("zebraThresholdUi", Models.ZEBRA_DEFAULT_UI_THRESHOLD)
        falseColorOn = prefs.getBoolean("falseColorOn", false)
        lutPreset = runCatching { Models.LutPreset.valueOf(str("lutPreset", "OFF")) }
            .getOrDefault(Models.LutPreset.OFF)
        importedLutPath = prefs.getString("importedLutPath", null)
        guide = runCatching { Models.GuidePreset.valueOf(str("guide", "OFF")) }
            .getOrDefault(Models.GuidePreset.OFF)
        grid = runCatching { Models.GridOverlay.valueOf(str("grid", "NONE")) }
            .getOrDefault(Models.GridOverlay.NONE)
        desqueeze = runCatching { Models.DesqueezePreset.valueOf(str("desqueeze", "X100")) }
            .getOrDefault(Models.DesqueezePreset.X100)
        eisOn = prefs.getBoolean("eisOn", false)
        audioMetering = runCatching {
            Models.AudioMeteringMode.valueOf(str("audioMetering", "VU_18DBFS"))
        }.getOrDefault(Models.AudioMeteringMode.VU_18DBFS)
        reel = prefs.getInt("reel", 1)
        scene = str("scene", "1")
        take = prefs.getInt("take", 1)
        director = str("director", "")
        operator = str("operator", "")
    }

    /** Persist F-marker positions (normalized 0..1 x/y, or null to clear). */
    fun saveMarker(index: Int, x: Float?, y: Float?) {
        val e = prefs.edit()
        if (x == null || y == null) {
            focusMarkers[index] = null
            e.remove("marker${index}x").remove("marker${index}y")
        } else {
            focusMarkers[index] = floatArrayOf(x, y)
            e.putFloat("marker${index}x", x).putFloat("marker${index}y", y)
        }
        e.apply()
    }

    init {
        for (i in 0..2) {
            if (prefs.contains("marker${i}x")) {
                focusMarkers[i] = floatArrayOf(
                    prefs.getFloat("marker${i}x", 0.5f),
                    prefs.getFloat("marker${i}y", 0.5f)
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

==> m14_cinepro/app/src/main/java/com/m14/cinepro/gl/GlViewfinder.kt <==
package com.m14.cinepro.gl

import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.SurfaceTexture
import android.opengl.GLSurfaceView
import com.m14.cinepro.core.EngineListener
import com.m14.cinepro.core.Models
import com.m14.cinepro.core.SettingsStore

/**
 * M14 CinePro — GL viewfinder surface (Architecture A: 1080p GL monitoring pipeline).
 *
 * Setup law: EGL context client version 3 (ES 3.2 device: Mali-G68 MP2), RGBA8888 +
 * 24-bit depth config chooser, EGL context preserved across pause, continuous render mode.
 * The camera frame arrives via the renderer-owned SurfaceTexture (GL_TEXTURE_EXTERNAL_OES);
 * [onTextureReady] is invoked exactly once per created SurfaceTexture from the GL thread so
 * MainActivity can hand it to CameraEngine (it must call SurfaceTexture.setDefaultBufferSize
 * to the viewfinder size before attaching the capture session).
 *
 * Wiring (MainActivity):
 *   viewfinder.renderer.eisMatrixProvider = { gyroEis.matrixIfEnabled() }        // null = EIS off
 *   viewfinder.renderer.wbGainsProvider  = { wbController.normalizedShaderGains() }
 *   viewfinder.applyLut(preset, importedPath)                               // LUT changes
 *   viewfinder.refreshLuminanceLut()                                        // rare re-bake
 */
class GlViewfinder(
    context: Context,
    settings: SettingsStore,
    listener: EngineListener,
    onTextureReady: (SurfaceTexture) -> Unit
) : GLSurfaceView(context) {

    /** 3D LUT engine (generators, .cube parser, luminance bake, half-float codec). */
    val lutEngine = LutEngine()

    /** The GLSurfaceView.Renderer; expose for provider wiring (eisMatrixProvider/wbGainsProvider). */
    val renderer: ViewfinderRenderer =
        ViewfinderRenderer(context, settings, listener, lutEngine, onTextureReady)

    init {
        renderer.host = this
        setEGLContextClientVersion(3)
        setEGLConfigChooser(8, 8, 8, 8, 24, 0)
        setPreserveEGLContextOnPause(true)
        setRenderer(renderer)
        renderMode = RENDERMODE_CONTINUOUSLY
    }

    /**
     * Apply a LUT preset (posts to the GL thread; resolved + uploaded at the next frame
     * boundary). For Models.LutPreset.IMPORTED pass the .cube file path — non-null paths are
     * stored on [LutEngine.importedPath] before the request is queued.
     */
    fun applyLut(preset: Models.LutPreset, importedPath: String?) {
        if (!importedPath.isNullOrEmpty()) lutEngine.importedPath = importedPath
        queueEvent { renderer.requestLut(preset) }
    }

    /** Re-bake + re-upload the 33^3 luminance L* 3D texture on the GL thread. */
    fun refreshLuminanceLut() {
        queueEvent { renderer.refreshLuminanceLut() }
    }

    /** Deterministic teardown: release GL objects + SurfaceTexture, then pause the render thread. */
    fun releaseViewfinder() {
        queueEvent { renderer.releaseGl() }
        onPause()
    }
}

==> m14_cinepro/app/src/main/java/com/m14/cinepro/gl/HistogramEngine.kt <==
package com.m14.cinepro.gl

import android.content.Context
import android.opengl.GLES31
import android.opengl.GLES32
import android.util.Log
import com.m14.cinepro.core.Models
import java.nio.ByteBuffer
import java.nio.ByteOrder

/**
 * M14 CinePro — GPU histogram engine (stage4_shaders.md §1.8 / §5 / §10.4).
 *
 * Renders the graded (LUT-applied, overlay-free) analysis frame into a downscaled RGBA8 FBO
 * (floor(viewport/16), even, min 1) and accumulates a 64-bin x RGB histogram with the
 * GLES 3.1 compute kernel from assets (%%BINS%% injected textually). Two-level accumulation
 * (shared-memory workgroup histogram -> one atomicAdd per bin into a std430 SSBO at binding 0);
 * the global buffer is zeroed on the CPU each dispatch. Readback maps the SSBO and hands a
 * fresh 768-byte little-endian ByteBuffer (192 uint32: R=[0,64) G=[64,128) B=[128,192)) to the
 * renderer, which forwards it to EngineListener.onHistogram.
 *
 * Compute capability guard: if the compute program cannot be built the engine disables itself
 * (isReady() == false) and logs — the viewfinder keeps rendering, only histogram/scene
 * detection are disabled (stage4 §10.4 law string).
 */
class HistogramEngine(private val context: Context) {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "M14GL"

        /** 64 bins x 3 channels x 4 bytes = 768. */
        private const val SSBO_BYTES = Models.HISTOGRAM_BINS * 3 * 4
        private const val WORK_GROUP = 16
    }

    private var program = 0
    private var uSrc = -1
    private var fbo = 0
    private var tex = 0
    private var ssbo = 0
    private var width = 0
    private var height = 0
    private var computeReady = false

    /** Analysis FBO dimensions (floor(viewport/16), even, min 1). */
    fun analysisWidth(): Int = width
    fun analysisHeight(): Int = height

    /** True when FBO + SSBO + compute program are live and usable. */
    fun isReady(): Boolean = computeReady && program != 0 && fbo != 0 && tex != 0 && ssbo != 0

    /**
     * Create / resize the analysis resources. Called from onSurfaceChanged with the viewport
     * size; recreates the FBO texture + SSBO when the derived analysis size changes, keeps the
     * compiled compute program across sizes.
     */
    fun setup(viewportW: Int, viewportH: Int) {
        val aw = analysisSize(viewportW)
        val ah = analysisSize(viewportH)
        if (isReady() && aw == width && ah == height) return

        releaseTargets(keepProgram = true)
        width = aw
        height = ah

        // Analysis color texture: GL_RGBA8, LINEAR, CLAMP_TO_EDGE (stage4 §10.4).
        val texArr = IntArray(1)
        GLES32.glGenTextures(1, texArr, 0)
        tex = texArr[0]
        GLES32.glBindTexture(GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_2D, tex)
        GLES32.glTexParameteri(GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_2D, GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, GLES32.GL_LINEAR)
        GLES32.glTexParameteri(GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_2D, GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, GLES32.GL_LINEAR)
        GLES32.glTexParameteri(GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_2D, GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_S, GLES32.GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE)
        GLES32.glTexParameteri(GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_2D, GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_T, GLES32.GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE)
        GLES32.glTexImage2D(
            GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0, GLES32.GL_RGBA8, width, height, 0,
            GLES32.GL_RGBA, GLES32.GL_UNSIGNED_BYTE, null
        )

        val fboArr = IntArray(1)
        GLES32.glGenFramebuffers(1, fboArr, 0)
        fbo = fboArr[0]
        GLES32.glBindFramebuffer(GLES32.GL_FRAMEBUFFER, fbo)
        GLES32.glFramebufferTexture2D(
            GLES32.GL_FRAMEBUFFER, GLES32.GL_COLOR_ATTACHMENT0, GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_2D, tex, 0
        )
        val status = GLES32.glCheckFramebufferStatus(GLES32.GL_FRAMEBUFFER)
        if (status != GLES32.GL_FRAMEBUFFER_COMPLETE) {
            Log.w(TAG, "M14GL histogram FBO incomplete: 0x${Integer.toHexString(status)}")
            releaseTargets(keepProgram = true)
            return
        }

        if (program == 0) buildComputeProgram()
        if (program == 0) {
            releaseTargets(keepProgram = true)
            return
        }

        // SSBO: 768 bytes, GL_DYNAMIC_READ, bound at binding 0 (std430 layout in the kernel).
        val ssboArr = IntArray(1)
        GLES31.glGenBuffers(1, ssboArr, 0)
        ssbo = ssboArr[0]
        GLES31.glBindBuffer(GLES31.GL_SHADER_STORAGE_BUFFER, ssbo)
        GLES31.glBufferData(GLES31.GL_SHADER_STORAGE_BUFFER, SSBO_BYTES, null, GLES31.GL_DYNAMIC_READ)
        GLES31.glBindBufferBase(GLES31.GL_SHADER_STORAGE_BUFFER, 0, ssbo)
        GLES31.glBindBuffer(GLES31.GL_SHADER_STORAGE_BUFFER, 0)

        GLES32.glBindFramebuffer(GLES32.GL_FRAMEBUFFER, 0)
        computeReady = true
        GlslSupport.checkGlErrors(TAG, "histogram setup")
        Log.i(TAG, "M14GL histogram ready: analysis ${width}x${height}, bins=${Models.HISTOGRAM_BINS}")
    }

    /** floor(viewport/16), forced even, min 1 — exactly stage4 §10.4. */
    private fun analysisSize(viewport: Int): Int {
        val q = viewport / WORK_GROUP
        val even = (q / 2) * 2
        return even.coerceAtLeast(1)
    }

    private fun buildComputeProgram() {
        try {
            val src = GlslSupport.readAsset(context, "glsl/histogram_compute.glsl")
                .replace("%%BINS%%", Models.HISTOGRAM_BINS.toString())
            val shader = GlslSupport.compile(GLES31.GL_COMPUTE_SHADER, src, "histogram_compute")
            val p = GLES32.glCreateProgram()
            GLES32.glAttachShader(p, shader)
            GLES32.glLinkProgram(p)
            val linked = IntArray(1)
            GLES32.glGetProgramiv(p, GLES32.GL_LINK_STATUS, linked, 0)
            GLES32.glDeleteShader(shader)
            if (linked[0] == 0) {
                val log = GLES32.glGetProgramInfoLog(p)
                GLES32.glDeleteProgram(p)
                throw IllegalStateException("histogram_compute link failed: $log")
            }
            program = p
            uSrc = GLES32.glGetUniformLocation(p, "uSrc")
        } catch (t: Throwable) {
            Log.w(
                TAG,
                "M14GL GPU histogram unavailable: compute shaders unsupported on this device. " +
                    "Histogram and scene detection are disabled.",
                t
            )
            program = 0
            uSrc = -1
        }
    }

    /**
     * Bind the analysis FBO + its viewport, run [drawFn] (the renderer's master-program quad
     * draw with areEffectsVisible=0), then unbind back to the default framebuffer.
     */
    fun drawGradedFrame(drawFn: () -> Unit) {
        if (!isReady()) return
        GLES32.glBindFramebuffer(GLES32.GL_FRAMEBUFFER, fbo)
        GLES32.glViewport(0, 0, width, height)
        drawFn()
        GLES32.glBindFramebuffer(GLES32.GL_FRAMEBUFFER, 0)
    }

    /**
     * Zero the SSBO, dispatch the compute kernel over ceil(size/16) work groups, barrier,
     * map the buffer read-only and copy the 192 uint32 counts into a fresh direct
     * LITTLE_ENDIAN ByteBuffer owned by the receiver. Returns null when disabled or the map
     * fails (renderer skips onHistogram that frame).
     */
    fun dispatchAndRead(): ByteBuffer? {
        if (!isReady()) return null

        // CPU-side zero each dispatch (kernel accumulates with atomicAdd).
        GLES31.glBindBuffer(GLES31.GL_SHADER_STORAGE_BUFFER, ssbo)
        GLES31.glBufferSubData(GLES31.GL_SHADER_STORAGE_BUFFER, 0, SSBO_BYTES, ByteBuffer.allocateDirect(SSBO_BYTES))

        GLES32.glUseProgram(program)
        GLES32.glActiveTexture(GLES32.GL_TEXTURE0)
        GLES32.glBindTexture(GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_2D, tex)
        if (uSrc >= 0) GLES32.glUniform1i(uSrc, 0)
        GLES31.glBindBufferBase(GLES31.GL_SHADER_STORAGE_BUFFER, 0, ssbo)

        val groupsX = (width + WORK_GROUP - 1) / WORK_GROUP
        val groupsY = (height + WORK_GROUP - 1) / WORK_GROUP
        GLES31.glDispatchCompute(groupsX, groupsY, 1)
        GLES31.glMemoryBarrier(GLES31.GL_SHADER_STORAGE_BARRIER_BIT or GLES31.GL_BUFFER_UPDATE_BARRIER_BIT)

        GLES31.glBindBuffer(GLES31.GL_SHADER_STORAGE_BUFFER, ssbo)
        val mapped = GLES31.glMapBufferRange(GLES31.GL_SHADER_STORAGE_BUFFER, 0, SSBO_BYTES, GLES31.GL_MAP_READ_BIT) as? ByteBuffer
        var result: ByteBuffer? = null
        if (mapped != null) {
            mapped.order(ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN)
            val outBuf = ByteBuffer.allocateDirect(SSBO_BYTES).order(ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN)
            val count = Models.HISTOGRAM_BINS * 3
            for (i in 0 until count) outBuf.putInt(mapped.int)
            outBuf.rewind()
            result = outBuf
        }
        GLES31.glUnmapBuffer(GLES31.GL_SHADER_STORAGE_BUFFER)
        GLES31.glBindBuffer(GLES31.GL_SHADER_STORAGE_BUFFER, 0)
        return result
    }

    /** Drop FBO/texture/SSBO (and optionally the program). Safe to call repeatedly. */
    private fun releaseTargets(keepProgram: Boolean) {
        computeReady = false
        if (fbo != 0) {
            GLES32.glDeleteFramebuffers(1, intArrayOf(fbo), 0)
            fbo = 0
        }
        if (tex != 0) {
            GLES32.glDeleteTextures(1, intArrayOf(tex), 0)
            tex = 0
        }
        if (ssbo != 0) {
            GLES31.glDeleteBuffers(1, intArrayOf(ssbo), 0)
            ssbo = 0
        }
        if (!keepProgram && program != 0) {
            GLES32.glDeleteProgram(program)
            program = 0
            uSrc = -1
        }
    }

    /** Full teardown (GL thread). */
    fun release() {
        releaseTargets(keepProgram = false)
    }
}

==> m14_cinepro/app/src/main/java/com/m14/cinepro/gl/LutEngine.kt <==
package com.m14.cinepro.gl

import android.util.Log
import com.m14.cinepro.core.Models
import java.io.BufferedReader
import java.io.File
import java.io.FileInputStream
import java.io.InputStream
import java.io.InputStreamReader
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets

/**
 * M14 CinePro — 3D LUT engine (stage4_shaders.md §7 + §10.3).
 *
 * Responsibilities:
 *  - Generate the four built-in 33^3 display-referred LUTs (NEUTRAL_709 / TEAL_ORANGE /
 *    WARM_FADE / MONO_CONTRAST) as half-float RGBA voxels.
 *  - Parse user-imported .cube files (full grammar: TITLE, LUT_3D_SIZE, LUT_1D_SIZE (ignored
 *    data skipped), DOMAIN_MIN/MAX inverse re-gridding, comments) into the same representation.
 *  - Bake the 33^3 luminance 3D LUT (RGB -> CIE L* normalized) consumed by
 *    luminance_function.glsl (stage4 §1.9) for zebras + fast false color.
 *  - IEEE 754 binary16 conversion with the full bit algorithm (denormals, ties-to-even,
 *    overflow clamps to the inf range).
 *
 * Voxel layout law: index i = (b*size^2 + g*size + r)*4 — red fastest, matching the .cube
 * file convention and the RGBA16F GL_TEXTURE_3D upload of stage4 §7.4.
 *
 * All LUTs are display-referred Rec.709/sRGB-ish [0,1] transforms with alpha = 1.0
 * (the shader `lut3D()` returns the fetched texel verbatim, including alpha).
 */
class LutEngine {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "M14GL"

        /** Built-in grid resolution (stage4 law: 33^3 everywhere). */
        const val LUT_SIZE = 33

        // CIE L* constants (stage4 §1.5 / §1.9).
        private const val LSTAR_C1 = 0.008856451679036f   // (6/29)^3
        private const val LSTAR_C2 = 903.2962962962963f   // (29/3)^3

        // Rec.709 transfer function constants (stage4 §1.5).
        private const val REC709_LINEAR_THRESHOLD = 0.081f
        private const val REC709_LINEAR_SLOPE = 4.5f
        private const val REC709_OFFSET = 0.099f
        private const val REC709_SCALE_FACTOR = 1.099f
        private const val REC709_GAMMA_RECIPROCAL = 0.45f
    }

    /** A ready-to-upload 3D LUT: size^3 voxels x 4 half floats (RGBA), red fastest. */
    class Lut3D(val size: Int, val rgbaHalf: ShortArray, val title: String)

    /** Path of the user-imported .cube file, set by [GlViewfinder.applyLut]. */
    @Volatile
    var importedPath: String? = null

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Generation
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    /**
     * Resolve a preset to a LUT. OFF -> null (identity fallback, lut3DVisible = 0).
     * IMPORTED -> parse [importedPath]; null when absent/broken (caller falls back).
     */
    fun generate(preset: Models.LutPreset): Lut3D? = when (preset) {
        Models.LutPreset.OFF -> null
        Models.LutPreset.IMPORTED -> importedPath?.let { loadImported(it) }
        Models.LutPreset.NEUTRAL_709 -> buildLut("Neutral Rec.709") { r, g, b, out ->
            out[0] = r; out[1] = g; out[2] = b
        }
        Models.LutPreset.TEAL_ORANGE -> buildLut("Cinema Teal & Orange", voxel = ::tealOrangeVoxel)
        Models.LutPreset.WARM_FADE -> buildLut("Warm Fade", voxel = ::warmFadeVoxel)
        Models.LutPreset.MONO_CONTRAST -> buildLut("Mono Contrast", voxel = ::monoContrastVoxel)
    }

    /** Generic 33^3 voxel builder; voxel() writes sRGB-ish display-referred RGB into out[0..2]. */
    private fun buildLut(
        title: String,
        size: Int = LUT_SIZE,
        voxel: (Float, Float, Float, FloatArray) -> Unit
    ): Lut3D {
        val half = ShortArray(size * size * size * 4)
        val out = FloatArray(3)
        val n = (size - 1).toFloat()
        var i = 0
        for (b in 0 until size) for (g in 0 until size) for (r in 0 until size) {
            voxel(r / n, g / n, b / n, out)
            half[i++] = toHalf(out[0].coerceIn(0f, 1f))
            half[i++] = toHalf(out[1].coerceIn(0f, 1f))
            half[i++] = toHalf(out[2].coerceIn(0f, 1f))
            half[i++] = toHalf(1f)
        }
        return Lut3D(size, half, title)
    }

    /**
     * NEUTRAL_709 support / TEAL_ORANGE: per-channel film S-curve (smoothstep contrast,
     * pivot 0.435), shadows pushed teal (b +0.06, r -0.04), highlights warmed
     * (r +0.05, b -0.05), classic shoulder from the smoothstep contrast curve.
     */
    private fun tealOrangeVoxel(r: Float, g: Float, b: Float, out: FloatArray) {
        var cr = smoothContrast(r, 0.435f, 1.15f)
        var cg = smoothContrast(g, 0.435f, 1.15f)
        var cb = smoothContrast(b, 0.435f, 1.15f)
        val luma = 0.2126f * cr + 0.7152f * cg + 0.0722f * cb
        val shadow = 1f - smoothstep01(luma / 0.5f)
        val high = smoothstep01((luma - 0.5f) / 0.5f)
        cr += -0.04f * shadow + 0.05f * high
        cb += 0.06f * shadow - 0.05f * high
        out[0] = cr; out[1] = cg; out[2] = cb
    }

    /**
     * WARM_FADE: lifted blacks (+0.05 at 0, white preserved), warm gain (r *1.04, b *0.94),
     * gentle highlight roll-off above a 0.8 knee (smoothstep shoulder, 1 -> 1).
     */
    private fun warmFadeVoxel(r: Float, g: Float, b: Float, out: FloatArray) {
        var cr = 0.05f + 0.95f * r
        var cg = 0.05f + 0.95f * g
        var cb = 0.05f + 0.95f * b
        cr *= 1.04f
        cb *= 0.94f
        out[0] = highlightRollOff(cr)
        out[1] = highlightRollOff(cg)
        out[2] = highlightRollOff(cb)
    }

    /**
     * MONO_CONTRAST: Rec.709 luma then a strong smoothstep S-curve
     * (contrast ~1.25 around 0.45).
     */
    private fun monoContrastVoxel(r: Float, g: Float, b: Float, out: FloatArray) {
        val luma = 0.2126f * r + 0.7152f * g + 0.0722f * b
        val c = smoothContrast(luma, 0.45f, 1.25f)
        out[0] = c; out[1] = c; out[2] = c
    }

    /** Standard smoothstep on a t already normalized to [0,1] (clamped). */
    private fun smoothstep01(t: Float): Float {
        val x = t.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
        return x * x * (3f - 2f * x)
    }

    /**
     * Smoothstep contrast curve centered at [center]: 0 -> 0, center -> center, 1 -> 1,
     * mid-slope multiplied by [strength] (values > 1 crush/clip the tails after clamp,
     * which is the intended filmic shoulder/toe behavior).
     */
    private fun smoothContrast(x: Float, center: Float, strength: Float): Float =
        if (x < center) {
            center - smoothstep01((center - x) / center) * center * strength
        } else {
            center + smoothstep01((x - center) / (1f - center)) * (1f - center) * strength
        }

    /** Gentle highlight roll-off above the 0.8 knee; identity below the knee, 1 -> 1. */
    private fun highlightRollOff(x: Float): Float {
        val knee = 0.8f
        return if (x <= knee) x else knee + (1f - knee) * smoothstep01((x - knee) / (1f - knee))
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // .cube import (stage4 §7.3 user-import path)
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    /**
     * Full .cube grammar: TITLE, LUT_3D_SIZE (required), LUT_1D_SIZE (1D data lines are
     * skipped — they precede the 3D table per the Adobe spec), DOMAIN_MIN/MAX (inverse
     * domain scaling is baked in via trilinear re-gridding so the uploaded texture keeps
     * the [0,1]^3 domain the shader samples), '#' comments, blank lines, "r g b" floats.
     * Returns null (and logs) on any malformed/incomplete input — caller shows import-failed.
     */
    fun parseCube(inputStream: InputStream): Lut3D? {
        try {
            inputStream.use { stream ->
                BufferedReader(InputStreamReader(stream, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)).use { reader ->
                    return parseCubeReader(reader)
                }
            }
        } catch (t: Throwable) {
            Log.w(TAG, "M14GL .cube parse failed: ${t.message}")
            return null
        }
    }

    private fun parseCubeReader(reader: BufferedReader): Lut3D? {
        var title = "Imported LUT"
        var size = -1
        var size1d = -1
        var dmin = floatArrayOf(0f, 0f, 0f)
        var dmax = floatArrayOf(1f, 1f, 1f)
        var table = FloatArray(0)
        var fill = 0
        var skipped1d = 0

        var line = reader.readLine()
        while (line != null) {
            val t = line.trim()
            if (t.isEmpty() || t.startsWith("#")) {
                line = reader.readLine()
                continue
            }
            when {
                t.startsWith("TITLE") -> title = parseTitle(t)

                t.startsWith("LUT_3D_SIZE") -> {
                    val parsed = firstInt(t.substring("LUT_3D_SIZE".length))
                    size = parsed
                    table = if (size >= 2) FloatArray(size * size * size * 3) else FloatArray(0)
                    fill = 0
                }

                t.startsWith("LUT_1D_SIZE") -> size1d = firstInt(t.substring("LUT_1D_SIZE".length))

                t.startsWith("DOMAIN_MIN") -> parse3(t.substring("DOMAIN_MIN".length))?.let { dmin = it }
                t.startsWith("DOMAIN_MAX") -> parse3(t.substring("DOMAIN_MAX".length))?.let { dmax = it }

                else -> {
                    val v = parse3(t)
                    if (v != null) {
                        if (size1d > 0 && skipped1d < size1d) {
                            // Adobe spec: 1D table data lines come before the 3D table; skip them.
                            skipped1d++
                        } else if (size >= 2 && fill < size * size * size) {
                            val o = fill * 3
                            table[o] = v[0]; table[o + 1] = v[1]; table[o + 2] = v[2]
                            fill++
                        }
                        // Extra data lines beyond the declared grid are ignored (grammar-tolerant).
                    }
                }
            }
            line = reader.readLine()
        }

        if (size < 2) {
            Log.w(TAG, "M14GL .cube parse: LUT_3D_SIZE missing/invalid")
            return null
        }
        val need = size * size * size
        if (fill < need) {
            Log.w(TAG, "M14GL .cube parse: incomplete 3D table (size=$size need=$need got=$fill)")
            return null
        }

        val src = if (domainIsIdentity(dmin, dmax)) table else regridToUnitDomain(table, size, dmin, dmax)

        val half = ShortArray(need * 4)
        var i = 0
        for (v in 0 until need) {
            half[i++] = toHalf(src[v * 3].coerceIn(0f, 1f))
            half[i++] = toHalf(src[v * 3 + 1].coerceIn(0f, 1f))
            half[i++] = toHalf(src[v * 3 + 2].coerceIn(0f, 1f))
            half[i++] = toHalf(1f)
        }
        return Lut3D(size, half, title)
    }

    private fun parseTitle(t: String): String {
        val rest = t.substring("TITLE".length).trim()
        return if (rest.isEmpty()) "Imported LUT" else rest.removeSurrounding("\"")
    }

    private fun firstInt(rest: String): Int =
        Regex("\\d+").find(rest)?.value?.toIntOrNull() ?: -1

    private fun parse3(rest: String): FloatArray? {
        val parts = rest.trim().split(Regex("\\s+"))
        if (parts.size < 3) return null
        val a = parts[0].toFloatOrNull() ?: return null
        val b = parts[1].toFloatOrNull() ?: return null
        val c = parts[2].toFloatOrNull() ?: return null
        return floatArrayOf(a, b, c)
    }

    private fun domainIsIdentity(dmin: FloatArray, dmax: FloatArray): Boolean {
        for (axis in 0 until 3) {
            if (Math.abs(dmin[axis]) > 1e-9f) return false
            if (Math.abs(dmax[axis] - 1f) > 1e-9f) return false
        }
        return true
    }

    /**
     * DOMAIN_MIN/MAX law: input coordinate v maps to domain position dmin + v*(dmax-dmin),
     * so the stored table G[p] = f(dmin + (dmax-dmin)*p). The shader samples with the fixed
     * half-voxel correction on [0,1], therefore bake T[p] = f(p) = G[domainInv(p)] here via
     * trilinear sampling of the file grid at the inverse-domain coordinates.
     */
    private fun regridToUnitDomain(table: FloatArray, size: Int, dmin: FloatArray, dmax: FloatArray): FloatArray {
        val out = FloatArray(size * size * size * 3)
        val inv = FloatArray(3)
        for (axis in 0 until 3) {
            val span = dmax[axis] - dmin[axis]
            inv[axis] = if (Math.abs(span) < 1e-9f) 0f else 1f / span
        }
        val n = (size - 1).toFloat()
        var o = 0
        for (b in 0 until size) for (g in 0 until size) for (r in 0 until size) {
            val x = (r / n - dmin[0]) * inv[0]
            val y = (g / n - dmin[1]) * inv[1]
            val z = (b / n - dmin[2]) * inv[2]
            sampleTrilinear(table, size, x, y, z, out, o)
            o += 3
        }
        return out
    }

    /** Trilinear sample of a size^3 x 3 float table (voxel order: r fastest, then g, then b). */
    private fun sampleTrilinear(table: FloatArray, size: Int, x: Float, y: Float, z: Float, out: FloatArray, outOffset: Int) {
        val max = size - 1
        val fx = x.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * max
        val fy = y.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * max
        val fz = z.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * max
        val x0 = fx.toInt().coerceIn(0, max - 1)
        val y0 = fy.toInt().coerceIn(0, max - 1)
        val z0 = fz.toInt().coerceIn(0, max - 1)
        val tx = fx - x0
        val ty = fy - y0
        val tz = fz - z0
        val x1 = x0 + 1
        val y1 = y0 + 1
        val z1 = z0 + 1
        val s2 = size * size
        var c = outOffset
        for (ch in 0 until 3) {
            val c000 = table[(z0 * s2 + y0 * size + x0) * 3 + ch]
            val c100 = table[(z0 * s2 + y0 * size + x1) * 3 + ch]
            val c010 = table[(z0 * s2 + y1 * size + x0) * 3 + ch]
            val c110 = table[(z0 * s2 + y1 * size + x1) * 3 + ch]
            val c001 = table[(z1 * s2 + y0 * size + x0) * 3 + ch]
            val c101 = table[(z1 * s2 + y0 * size + x1) * 3 + ch]
            val c011 = table[(z1 * s2 + y1 * size + x0) * 3 + ch]
            val c111 = table[(z1 * s2 + y1 * size + x1) * 3 + ch]
            val c00 = c000 + (c100 - c000) * tx
            val c10 = c010 + (c110 - c010) * tx
            val c01 = c001 + (c101 - c001) * tx
            val c11 = c011 + (c111 - c011) * tx
            val c0 = c00 + (c10 - c00) * ty
            val c1 = c01 + (c11 - c01) * ty
            out[c++] = c0 + (c1 - c0) * tz
        }
    }

    /** Load a user .cube from a filesystem path (FileInputStream -> parseCube). */
    fun loadImported(path: String): Lut3D? = try {
        FileInputStream(File(path)).use { parseCube(it) }
    } catch (t: Throwable) {
        Log.w(TAG, "M14GL LUT import failed for $path: ${t.message}")
        null
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Luminance LUT bake (stage4 §1.9)
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    /**
     * 33^3 bake of RGB -> normalized CIE L*: per channel rec709DecodeGamma, Y = 709 dot,
     * L* = Y<=C1 ? Y*C2 : cbrt(Y)*116-16, normalized /100. All four half-float channels
     * carry L* (the shader reads .r).
     */
    fun bakeLuminanceLut(): Lut3D {
        val size = LUT_SIZE
        val half = ShortArray(size * size * size * 4)
        val n = (size - 1).toFloat()
        var i = 0
        for (b in 0 until size) for (g in 0 until size) for (r in 0 until size) {
            val lr = rec709DecodeGamma(r / n)
            val lg = rec709DecodeGamma(g / n)
            val lb = rec709DecodeGamma(b / n)
            val y = 0.2126f * lr + 0.7152f * lg + 0.0722f * lb
            val l = y2Lstar(y)
            val h = toHalf(l)
            half[i++] = h
            half[i++] = h
            half[i++] = h
            half[i++] = h
        }
        return Lut3D(size, half, "Luminance L* Rec.709")
    }

    /** Rec.709 OETF inverse (stage4 §1.5 constants). */
    private fun rec709DecodeGamma(v: Float): Float =
        if (v <= REC709_LINEAR_THRESHOLD) {
            v / REC709_LINEAR_SLOPE
        } else {
            Math.pow(((v + REC709_OFFSET) / REC709_SCALE_FACTOR).toDouble(), 1.0 / REC709_GAMMA_RECIPROCAL).toFloat()
        }

    /** CIE Y -> L* normalized to [0,1] (stage4 §1.5). */
    private fun y2Lstar(y: Float): Float {
        val raw = if (y <= LSTAR_C1) y * LSTAR_C2 else Math.cbrt(y.toDouble()).toFloat() * 116.0f - 16.0f
        return raw / 100.0f
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // IEEE 754 binary16 conversion (full bit algorithm)
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    /**
     * Float -> half (IEEE 754 binary16), round-to-nearest-even, denormals supported,
     * overflow clamps to the inf range, NaN -> canonical 0x7E00 (sign preserved), -0 kept.
     */
    fun toHalf(v: Float): Short {
        val bits = java.lang.Float.floatToIntBits(v)
        val sign = (bits ushr 16) and 0x8000
        if (v.isNaN()) return (sign or 0x7E00).toShort()
        if (v.isInfinite()) return (sign or 0x7C00).toShort()
        if ((bits and 0x7FFFFFFF) == 0) return (bits ushr 16).toShort()

        val exp = ((bits ushr 23) and 0xFF) - 127 + 15
        var mant = bits and 0x7FFFFF
        var h: Int
        if (exp >= 0x1F) {
            // Overflow: clamp to the inf range.
            h = 0x7C00
        } else if (exp <= 0) {
            if (exp < -10) {
                h = 0
            } else {
                // Half subnormal: shift the implicit-bit-extended mantissa, ties-to-even.
                mant = mant or 0x800000
                val shift = 14 - exp
                h = mant ushr shift
                val roundBit = 1 shl (shift - 1)
                if ((mant and roundBit) != 0 && ((mant and (roundBit - 1)) != 0 || (h and 1) != 0)) h += 1
            }
        } else {
            val halfMant = mant ushr 13
            h = (exp shl 10) or halfMant
            if ((mant and 0x1000) != 0 && ((mant and 0x0FFF) != 0 || (halfMant and 1) != 0)) h += 1
        }
        return (sign or h).toShort()
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Half-voxel-center sampling law (stage4 §1.10 / §7.4 / §10.3)
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    /** lut3DScale / luminanceScale = 1 - 1/N. */
    fun halfVoxelScale(size: Int): Float = 1f - 1f / size

    /** lut3DOffset / luminanceOffset = (1/N)/2. */
    fun halfVoxelOffset(size: Int): Float = (1f / size) / 2f
}

==> m14_cinepro/app/src/main/java/com/m14/cinepro/gl/ViewfinderRenderer.kt <==
package com.m14.cinepro.gl

import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.SurfaceTexture
import android.opengl.GLES32
import android.opengl.GLES11Ext
import android.opengl.GLSurfaceView
import android.opengl.Matrix
import android.os.Process
import android.os.SystemClock
import android.util.Log
import com.m14.cinepro.core.EngineListener
import com.m14.cinepro.core.Models
import com.m14.cinepro.core.SettingsStore
import java.nio.FloatBuffer
import java.nio.ShortBuffer
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets
import javax.microedition.khronos.egl.EGLConfig
import javax.microedition.khronos.opengles.GL10

/**
 * M14 CinePro — the GL viewfinder renderer (stage4_shaders.md §1, §7.4, §10.2, §10.3, §10.5).
 *
 * Per frame: SurfaceTexture.updateTexImage() -> compose texMatrix = ST * EIS (EIS right
 * operand, applied to texcoords first) -> master program full-screen quad ->
 * every Models.HISTOGRAM_INTERVAL_MS draw the graded overlay-free frame into the analysis FBO
 * and dispatch the 64-bin RGB histogram compute + readback -> EngineListener.onHistogram.
 *
 * Master fragment assembly (§10.2 EXACT): assets/glsl/fragment_header.glsl + wb_trim_effect +
 * luminance_function + lut_3d_effect + false_color_effect_fast + zebra_effect +
 * focus_assist_effect + generated main(). All shader source is loaded from assets at runtime —
 * never duplicated in Kotlin.
 *
 * Uniform laws (§10.3): zebraThreshold = UI * Models.ZEBRA_THRESHOLD_SCALE_709 (0.93,
 * Rec.709 display profile), peakingThreshold = UI * Models.PEAKING_UI_SCALE (0.04),
 * zebraShift marches +1.0/frame wrapping at 2*zebraWidth, luminance LUT always visible
 * (33^3, unit 3), creative LUT on unit 2 with half-voxel scale/offset, colorSpace = 0
 * (Rec.709 SDR).
 *
 * Renderer holds no sensor and no camera-package dependencies: EIS matrices and WB gains
 * arrive via provider lambdas wired by MainActivity. Renderer thread priority -19 (ground
 * truth law), applied once on the first onDrawFrame.
 */
class ViewfinderRenderer(
    private val context: Context,
    private val settings: SettingsStore,
    private val listener: EngineListener,
    private val lutEngine: LutEngine,
    private val onTextureReady: (SurfaceTexture) -> Unit
) : GLSurfaceView.Renderer {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "M14GL"

        /** Full-screen quad, TRIANGLE_STRIP order (-1,-1),(1,-1),(-1,1),(1,1), vec4 attribute. */
        private val QUAD_COORDS = floatArrayOf(
            -1f, -1f, 0f, 1f,
            1f, -1f, 0f, 1f,
            -1f, 1f, 0f, 1f,
            1f, 1f, 0f, 1f
        )

        /** Generated main() — stage4 §10.2 exact statement order (pre-LUT luminance law). */
        private const val MASTER_MAIN =
            "void main() {\n" +
                "    vec4 inColor = texture(cameraTexture, cameraTexCoordinates);\n" +
                "    vec4 outColor = inColor;\n" +
                "    if (wbTrimVisible == 1) outColor = wbTrim(outColor);\n" +
                "    float inLuminance = getLuminance(inColor);\n" +
                "    if (lut3DVisible == 1) outColor = lut3D(outColor);\n" +
                "    if (areEffectsVisible == 1) {\n" +
                "        if (falseColorVisible == 1) outColor = falseColor(inLuminance);\n" +
                "        if (zebrasVisible == 1) outColor = zebras(outColor, inLuminance);\n" +
                "        if (focusAssistVisible == 1) outColor = focusAssist(outColor);\n" +
                "    }\n" +
                "    fragColor = outColor;\n" +
                "}\n"

        private val UNIFORM_NAMES = arrayOf(
            "cameraTexture", "texMatrix", "areEffectsVisible",
            "wbTrimVisible", "wbGains",
            "luminanceVisible", "luminanceScale", "luminanceOffset", "luminanceTexture",
            "lut3DVisible", "lut3DScale", "lut3DOffset", "lut3DTexture",
            "falseColorVisible", "colorSpace",
            "zebrasVisible", "midGreyMode", "zebraThreshold", "zebraShift", "zebraWidth",
            "zebraColor", "outWidth", "outHeight",
            "focusAssistVisible", "enableColoredLines", "lineColor", "peakingThreshold"
        )
    }

    /** Back-reference to the hosting GLSurfaceView (for requestRender on frame available). */
    @Volatile
    internal var host: GLSurfaceView? = null

    /**
     * EIS counter-rotation matrix provider, set by MainActivity (GyroEisController owns the
     * sensor math). Return null when EIS is off. Column-major 4x4 like SurfaceTexture's ST
     * matrix; composed as combined = ST * EIS so EIS is the right operand (applied to
     * texcoords first).
     */
    @Volatile
    var eisMatrixProvider: (() -> FloatArray?)? = null

    /**
     * White-balance shader gains provider, set by MainActivity (wired to
     * WhiteBalanceController.normalizedShaderGains(), green == 1.0). Null -> unity trim.
     */
    @Volatile
    var wbGainsProvider: (() -> FloatArray)? = null

    // GL objects / program state
    private var program = 0
    private val uniformCache = HashMap<String, Int>()
    private var positionAttrib = -1
    private var quadVbo = 0
    private var cameraOesTex = 0
    private var lutTex3D = 0
    private var lumTex3D = 0
    private var uploadedLutSize = 0
    private var uploadedLumSize = 0
    private var surfaceTexture: SurfaceTexture? = null

    // Frame state
    private val stMatrix = FloatArray(16)
    private val combinedMatrix = FloatArray(16)
    private var outW = 0
    private var outH = 0
    private val histogramEngine = HistogramEngine(context)
    private var lastHistogramMs = 0L
    private var zebraShift = 0f
    private var frameCounter = 0L
    private var priorityApplied = false

    // LUT state
    @Volatile
    private var pendingLutPreset: Models.LutPreset? = null
    private var currentLut: LutEngine.Lut3D? = null
    private var currentLutPreset: Models.LutPreset? = null
    private var currentLutPathStamp: String? = null

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // GLSurfaceView.Renderer
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    override fun onSurfaceCreated(gl: GL10?, config: EGLConfig?) {
        Log.i(TAG, "M14GL onSurfaceCreated: ${GLES32.glGetString(GLES32.GL_RENDERER)}")

        GLES32.glDisable(GLES32.GL_DEPTH_TEST)
        GLES32.glDisable(GLES32.GL_BLEND)
        GLES32.glClearColor(0f, 0f, 0f, 1f)

        buildProgram()

        // Full-viewport quad VBO.
        val vboArr = IntArray(1)
        GLES32.glGenBuffers(1, vboArr, 0)
        quadVbo = vboArr[0]
        GLES32.glBindBuffer(GLES32.GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, quadVbo)
        GLES32.glBufferData(
            GLES32.GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, QUAD_COORDS.size * 4,
            FloatBuffer.wrap(QUAD_COORDS), GLES32.GL_STATIC_DRAW
        )
        GLES32.glBindBuffer(GLES32.GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, 0)

        // Camera OES texture + SurfaceTexture (callback handed to CameraEngine by MainActivity).
        val texArr = IntArray(1)
        GLES32.glGenTextures(1, texArr, 0)
        cameraOesTex = texArr[0]
        GLES32.glBindTexture(GLES11Ext.GL_TEXTURE_EXTERNAL_OES, cameraOesTex)
        GLES32.glTexParameteri(GLES11Ext.GL_TEXTURE_EXTERNAL_OES, GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, GLES32.GL_LINEAR)
        GLES32.glTexParameteri(GLES11Ext.GL_TEXTURE_EXTERNAL_OES, GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, GLES32.GL_LINEAR)
        GLES32.glTexParameteri(GLES11Ext.GL_TEXTURE_EXTERNAL_OES, GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_S, GLES32.GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE)
        GLES32.glTexParameteri(GLES11Ext.GL_TEXTURE_EXTERNAL_OES, GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_T, GLES32.GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE)
        GLES32.glBindTexture(GLES11Ext.GL_TEXTURE_EXTERNAL_OES, 0)

        val st = SurfaceTexture(cameraOesTex)
        surfaceTexture = st
        st.setOnFrameAvailableListener { host?.requestRender() }

        // 3D LUT textures: 2^3 identity fallback (LUT-off path never changes the shader path,
        // stage4 §12.6) + the always-visible 33^3 luminance bake.
        val lutTexArr = IntArray(1)
        GLES32.glGenTextures(1, lutTexArr, 0)
        lutTex3D = lutTexArr[0]
        uploadedLutSize = 0
        uploadLutTexture(lutTex3D, identityVoxel2x3(), 2)
        currentLut = null
        currentLutPreset = null
        currentLutPathStamp = null

        val lumTexArr = IntArray(1)
        GLES32.glGenTextures(1, lumTexArr, 0)
        lumTex3D = lumTexArr[0]
        uploadedLumSize = 0
        refreshLuminanceLut()

        // Seed the creative LUT from the persisted settings for the very first frame.
        pendingLutPreset = settings.lutPreset

        // MUST fire once per created SurfaceTexture, from the GL thread.
        onTextureReady(st)
        GlslSupport.checkGlErrors(TAG, "onSurfaceCreated")
    }

    override fun onSurfaceChanged(gl: GL10?, width: Int, height: Int) {
        outW = width
        outH = height
        GLES32.glViewport(0, 0, width, height)
        histogramEngine.setup(width, height)
        Log.i(TAG, "M14GL onSurfaceChanged: ${width}x${height}")
    }

    override fun onDrawFrame(gl: GL10?) {
        if (!priorityApplied) {
            // Ground-truth law: renderer thread prio -19 (stage4 §10.1 ax0 HandlerThread).
            priorityApplied = try {
                Process.setThreadPriority(Process.myTid(), -19)
                true
            } catch (t: Throwable) {
                try {
                    Process.setThreadPriority(Process.myTid(), Process.THREAD_PRIORITY_URGENT_DISPLAY)
                } catch (ignored: Throwable) {
                }
                Log.w(TAG, "M14GL setThreadPriority(-19) failed: ${t.message}")
                true
            }
        }

        resolvePendingLut()

        val st = surfaceTexture
        if (st == null || outW <= 0 || outH <= 0 || program == 0 || positionAttrib < 0) {
            GLES32.glBindFramebuffer(GLES32.GL_FRAMEBUFFER, 0)
            GLES32.glClear(GLES32.GL_COLOR_BUFFER_BIT)
            return
        }

        st.updateTexImage()
        st.getTransformMatrix(stMatrix)
        val eis = eisMatrixProvider?.invoke()
        if (eis != null && eis.size >= 16) {
            Matrix.multiplyMM(combinedMatrix, 0, stMatrix, 0, eis, 0)
        } else {
            System.arraycopy(stMatrix, 0, combinedMatrix, 0, 16)
        }

        // ---- Screen pass: master program, all effects per settings ----
        GLES32.glBindFramebuffer(GLES32.GL_FRAMEBUFFER, 0)
        GLES32.glViewport(0, 0, outW, outH)
        GLES32.glUseProgram(program)

        GLES32.glActiveTexture(GLES32.GL_TEXTURE0)
        GLES32.glBindTexture(GLES11Ext.GL_TEXTURE_EXTERNAL_OES, cameraOesTex)
        GLES32.glActiveTexture(GLES32.GL_TEXTURE2)
        GLES32.glBindTexture(GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_3D, lutTex3D)
        GLES32.glActiveTexture(GLES32.GL_TEXTURE3)
        GLES32.glBindTexture(GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_3D, lumTex3D)
        GLES32.glActiveTexture(GLES32.GL_TEXTURE0)

        uMat4("texMatrix", combinedMatrix)
        applyUniforms(effectsVisible = true)
        drawQuad()

        // ---- Histogram pass: graded (LUT-applied), overlay-free, at 10 fps ----
        val now = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
        if (histogramEngine.isReady() && now - lastHistogramMs >= Models.HISTOGRAM_INTERVAL_MS) {
            lastHistogramMs = now
            GLES32.glUseProgram(program)
            applyUniforms(effectsVisible = false)
            histogramEngine.drawGradedFrame { drawQuad() }
            val bins = histogramEngine.dispatchAndRead()
            if (bins != null) listener.onHistogram(bins)
        }

        // Zebra marching law: +1.0 px per frame, wrap at 2 * zebraWidth (16.0).
        zebraShift += 1.0f
        if (zebraShift >= 2f * Models.ZEBRA_WIDTH_PX) zebraShift -= 2f * Models.ZEBRA_WIDTH_PX

        frameCounter++
        if (frameCounter % 90L == 0L) GlslSupport.checkGlErrors(TAG, "frame $frameCounter")
    }

    // Note: GLSurfaceView.Renderer has no onSurfaceDestroyed hook; GL objects are owned by the
    // EGL context which is preserved via setPreserveEGLContextOnPause, and full teardown happens
    // in releaseViewfinder() called from GlViewfinder.releaseViewfinder().

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Public control surface (GlViewfinder forwards, may arrive queued on the GL thread)
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    /** Queue a LUT preset change; resolved + uploaded at the start of the next onDrawFrame. */
    fun requestLut(preset: Models.LutPreset) {
        pendingLutPreset = preset
    }

    /** Re-bake + re-upload the luminance 3D LUT (glTexSubImage3D, same 33^3 size). */
    fun refreshLuminanceLut() {
        if (lumTex3D == 0) return
        val lut = lutEngine.bakeLuminanceLut()
        uploadLutTexture(lumTex3D, lut.rgbaHalf, lut.size)
        Log.i(TAG, "M14GL luminance LUT baked (${lut.size}^3)")
    }

    /** Full GL teardown (GL thread; called via GlViewfinder.releaseViewfinder). */
    fun releaseGl() {
        surfaceTexture?.release()
        surfaceTexture = null
        if (cameraOesTex != 0) {
            GLES32.glDeleteTextures(1, intArrayOf(cameraOesTex), 0)
            cameraOesTex = 0
        }
        if (quadVbo != 0) {
            GLES32.glDeleteBuffers(1, intArrayOf(quadVbo), 0)
            quadVbo = 0
        }
        if (lutTex3D != 0) {
            GLES32.glDeleteTextures(1, intArrayOf(lutTex3D), 0)
            lutTex3D = 0
        }
        if (lumTex3D != 0) {
            GLES32.glDeleteTextures(1, intArrayOf(lumTex3D), 0)
            lumTex3D = 0
        }
        if (program != 0) {
            GLES32.glDeleteProgram(program)
            program = 0
        }
        uniformCache.clear()
        positionAttrib = -1
        currentLut = null
        currentLutPreset = null
        currentLutPathStamp = null
        histogramEngine.release()
        Log.i(TAG, "M14GL renderer released")
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Master program assembly (stage4 §10.2 EXACT include order + generated main)
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    private fun buildProgram() {
        val vertexSrc = GlslSupport.readAsset(context, "glsl/transform_vertex.glsl")
        val fragmentSrc = StringBuilder()
            .append(GlslSupport.readAsset(context, "glsl/fragment_header.glsl"))
            .append(GlslSupport.readAsset(context, "glsl/wb_trim_effect.glsl"))
            .append(GlslSupport.readAsset(context, "glsl/luminance_function.glsl"))
            .append(GlslSupport.readAsset(context, "glsl/lut_3d_effect.glsl"))
            .append(GlslSupport.readAsset(context, "glsl/false_color_effect_fast.glsl"))
            .append(GlslSupport.readAsset(context, "glsl/zebra_effect.glsl"))
            .append(GlslSupport.readAsset(context, "glsl/focus_assist_effect.glsl"))
            .append(MASTER_MAIN)
            .toString()

        val vs = GlslSupport.compile(GLES32.GL_VERTEX_SHADER, vertexSrc, "transform_vertex")
        val fs = GlslSupport.compile(GLES32.GL_FRAGMENT_SHADER, fragmentSrc, "master_fragment")
        val p = GLES32.glCreateProgram()
        GLES32.glAttachShader(p, vs)
        GLES32.glAttachShader(p, fs)
        GLES32.glLinkProgram(p)
        val linked = IntArray(1)
        GLES32.glGetProgramiv(p, GLES32.GL_LINK_STATUS, linked, 0)
        GLES32.glDeleteShader(vs)
        GLES32.glDeleteShader(fs)
        if (linked[0] == 0) {
            val log = GLES32.glGetProgramInfoLog(p)
            GLES32.glDeleteProgram(p)
            // Fail fast — no silent black screen (task law).
            throw IllegalStateException("M14GL master program link failed: $log")
        }
        program = p
        positionAttrib = GLES32.glGetAttribLocation(p, "vPosition")

        uniformCache.clear()
        GLES32.glUseProgram(p)
        for (name in UNIFORM_NAMES) uniformCache[name] = GLES32.glGetUniformLocation(p, name)
        // Sampler bindings (fixed units: 0 = camera OES, 2 = creative LUT, 3 = luminance LUT).
        u1i("cameraTexture", 0)
        u1i("lut3DTexture", 2)
        u1i("luminanceTexture", 3)
        GlslSupport.checkGlErrors(TAG, "buildProgram")
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Uniform uploads (stage4 §10.3 exact setters)
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    /**
     * Upload every uniform from SettingsStore (volatile reads, every frame).
     * [effectsVisible] = true for the screen pass, false for the histogram FBO pass
     * (areEffectsVisible=0 -> "LUT-applied, overlay-free" law; wbTrim + 3D LUT stay live).
     */
    private fun applyUniforms(effectsVisible: Boolean) {
        val anyOverlay = settings.peakingMode != Models.PeakingMode.OFF ||
            settings.zebraMode != Models.ZebraMode.OFF ||
            settings.falseColorOn
        u1i("areEffectsVisible", if (effectsVisible && anyOverlay) 1 else 0)

        // White balance trim (first in chain; not gated by areEffectsVisible).
        u1i("wbTrimVisible", if (settings.wbPreset == Models.WbPreset.KELVIN) 1 else 0)
        val gains = wbGainsProvider?.invoke()
        if (gains != null && gains.size >= 3) {
            u3f("wbGains", gains[0], gains[1], gains[2])
        } else {
            u3f("wbGains", 1f, 1f, 1f)
        }

        // Luminance LUT: always visible, fixed 33^3 half-voxel correction.
        u1i("luminanceVisible", 1)
        u1f("luminanceScale", lutEngine.halfVoxelScale(LutEngine.LUT_SIZE))
        u1f("luminanceOffset", lutEngine.halfVoxelOffset(LutEngine.LUT_SIZE))

        // Creative 3D LUT.
        val lut = currentLut
        u1i("lut3DVisible", if (lut != null) 1 else 0)
        if (lut != null) {
            u1f("lut3DScale", lutEngine.halfVoxelScale(lut.size))
            u1f("lut3DOffset", lutEngine.halfVoxelOffset(lut.size))
        }

        // False color: fast variant consumes the PRE-LUT luminance (inLuminance in main()).
        u1i("falseColorVisible", if (settings.falseColorOn) 1 else 0)
        u1i("colorSpace", 0) // Rec.709 SDR

        // Zebras.
        u1i("zebrasVisible", if (settings.zebraMode != Models.ZebraMode.OFF) 1 else 0)
        val zebra = zebraUniformParams()
        u1i("midGreyMode", zebra.first)
        u1f("zebraThreshold", zebra.second)
        u1f("zebraShift", zebraShift)
        u1f("zebraWidth", Models.ZEBRA_WIDTH_PX)
        u4f("zebraColor", 1.0f, 0.85f, 0.27f, 1.0f) // YELLOW (task law)
        u1i("outWidth", outW)
        u1i("outHeight", outH)

        // Focus peaking.
        u1i("focusAssistVisible", if (settings.peakingMode != Models.PeakingMode.OFF) 1 else 0)
        u1i("enableColoredLines", if (settings.peakingMode == Models.PeakingMode.COLORED_LINES) 1 else 0)
        val pc = settings.peakingColor.rgba
        u4f("lineColor", pc[0], pc[1], pc[2], pc[3])
        u1f("peakingThreshold", settings.peakingStrength * Models.PEAKING_UI_SCALE)
    }

    /**
     * Zebra mode -> (midGreyMode, zebraThreshold).
     *  Z95/Z90: midGreyMode 0, anchored thresholds 0.95 / 0.90 scaled by the Rec.709 display
     *  profile factor (Models.ZEBRA_THRESHOLD_SCALE_709 = 0.93, stage4 §10.3).
     *  MID_GREY / MID_GREY_PLUS1: midGreyMode 1 / 2 with zebraThreshold = 0.0 (threshold
     *  irrelevant when the mid-grey band drives — task law; NOTE the include ORs both
     *  conditions, flagged in the worklog).
     *  OFF / custom: the persisted UI threshold * 0.93 (values unused while visible = 0).
     */
    private fun zebraUniformParams(): Pair<Int, Float> = when (settings.zebraMode) {
        Models.ZebraMode.Z95 -> 0 to (0.95f * Models.ZEBRA_THRESHOLD_SCALE_709)
        Models.ZebraMode.Z90 -> 0 to (0.90f * Models.ZEBRA_THRESHOLD_SCALE_709)
        Models.ZebraMode.MID_GREY -> 1 to 0.0f
        Models.ZebraMode.MID_GREY_PLUS1 -> 2 to 0.0f
        Models.ZebraMode.OFF -> 0 to (settings.zebraThresholdUi * Models.ZEBRA_THRESHOLD_SCALE_709)
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // LUT resolution + upload (stage4 §7.4)
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    private fun resolvePendingLut() {
        val preset = pendingLutPreset ?: return
        pendingLutPreset = null
        val pathStamp = lutEngine.importedPath
        if (currentLutPreset == preset && currentLutPathStamp == pathStamp && lutTex3D != 0) return
        currentLutPreset = preset
        currentLutPathStamp = pathStamp

        val lut = if (lutTex3D != 0) lutEngine.generate(preset) else null
        if (lut != null) {
            uploadLutTexture(lutTex3D, lut.rgbaHalf, lut.size)
            currentLut = lut
            Log.i(TAG, "M14GL LUT '${lut.title}' active (${lut.size}^3)")
        } else {
            // LUT off: 2^3 identity data stays in the texture, lut3DVisible = 0.
            uploadLutTexture(lutTex3D, identityVoxel2x3(), 2)
            currentLut = null
        }
    }

    /**
     * RGBA16F GL_TEXTURE_3D upload: GL_TEXTURE_3D / GL_RGBA16F / GL_RGBA / GL_HALF_FLOAT,
     * LINEAR filters, CLAMP_TO_EDGE on S/T/R; glTexSubImage3D when the size is unchanged
     * (LUT swap law, stage4 §7.4 ef2.i).
     */
    private fun uploadLutTexture(texId: Int, rgbaHalf: ShortArray, size: Int) {
        if (texId == 0) return
        GLES32.glActiveTexture(GLES32.GL_TEXTURE2)
        GLES32.glBindTexture(GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_3D, texId)
        GLES32.glTexParameteri(GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_3D, GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, GLES32.GL_LINEAR)
        GLES32.glTexParameteri(GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_3D, GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, GLES32.GL_LINEAR)
        GLES32.glTexParameteri(GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_3D, GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_S, GLES32.GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE)
        GLES32.glTexParameteri(GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_3D, GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_T, GLES32.GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE)
        GLES32.glTexParameteri(GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_3D, GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_R, GLES32.GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE)
        val buffer = ShortBuffer.wrap(rgbaHalf)
        // glTexSubImage3D when the size is unchanged (LUT swap law, stage4 §7.4).
        val isSubUpload = (texId == lutTex3D && uploadedLutSize == size) ||
            (texId == lumTex3D && uploadedLumSize == size)
        if (isSubUpload) {
            GLES32.glTexSubImage3D(
                GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_3D, 0, 0, 0, 0, size, size, size,
                GLES32.GL_RGBA, GLES32.GL_HALF_FLOAT, buffer
            )
        } else {
            GLES32.glTexImage3D(
                GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_3D, 0, GLES32.GL_RGBA16F, size, size, size, 0,
                GLES32.GL_RGBA, GLES32.GL_HALF_FLOAT, buffer
            )
            if (texId == lutTex3D) uploadedLutSize = size
            if (texId == lumTex3D) uploadedLumSize = size
        }
        GLES32.glBindTexture(GLES32.GL_TEXTURE_3D, 0)
        GLES32.glActiveTexture(GLES32.GL_TEXTURE0)
        GlslSupport.checkGlErrors(TAG, "uploadLutTexture size=$size")
    }

    /** 2^3 identity LUT payload (LUT-off fallback, stage4 §12.6). */
    private fun identityVoxel2x3(): ShortArray {
        val data = ShortArray(2 * 2 * 2 * 4)
        var i = 0
        for (b in 0..1) for (g in 0..1) for (r in 0..1) {
            data[i++] = lutEngine.toHalf(r.toFloat())
            data[i++] = lutEngine.toHalf(g.toFloat())
            data[i++] = lutEngine.toHalf(b.toFloat())
            data[i++] = lutEngine.toHalf(1f)
        }
        return data
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Draw helpers
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    private fun drawQuad() {
        GLES32.glBindBuffer(GLES32.GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, quadVbo)
        GLES32.glVertexAttribPointer(positionAttrib, 4, GLES32.GL_FLOAT, false, 0, 0)
        GLES32.glEnableVertexAttribArray(positionAttrib)
        GLES32.glDrawArrays(GLES32.GL_TRIANGLE_STRIP, 0, 4)
        GLES32.glBindBuffer(GLES32.GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, 0)
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Uniform cache helpers
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    private fun loc(name: String): Int = uniformCache.getOrPut(name) {
        if (program != 0) GLES32.glGetUniformLocation(program, name) else -1
    }

    private fun u1i(name: String, v: Int) {
        val l = loc(name)
        if (l >= 0) GLES32.glUniform1i(l, v)
    }

    private fun u1f(name: String, v: Float) {
        val l = loc(name)
        if (l >= 0) GLES32.glUniform1f(l, v)
    }

    private fun u3f(name: String, a: Float, b: Float, c: Float) {
        val l = loc(name)
        if (l >= 0) GLES32.glUniform3f(l, a, b, c)
    }

    private fun u4f(name: String, a: Float, b: Float, c: Float, d: Float) {
        val l = loc(name)
        if (l >= 0) GLES32.glUniform4f(l, a, b, c, d)
    }

    private fun uMat4(name: String, m: FloatArray) {
        val l = loc(name)
        if (l >= 0) GLES32.glUniformMatrix4fv(l, 1, false, m, 0)
    }
}

/**
 * GLSL loader util (task law): asset read + compile/link with info-log checks; any failure
 * throws IllegalStateException carrying the GL log — fail fast, no silent black screen.
 */
internal object GlslSupport {

    fun readAsset(context: Context, path: String): String =
        context.assets.open(path).use { stream ->
            stream.readBytes().toString(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
        }

    fun compile(type: Int, source: String, label: String): Int {
        val shader = GLES32.glCreateShader(type)
        if (shader == 0) throw IllegalStateException("M14GL glCreateShader failed [$label]")
        GLES32.glShaderSource(shader, source)
        GLES32.glCompileShader(shader)
        val status = IntArray(1)
        GLES32.glGetShaderiv(shader, GLES32.GL_COMPILE_STATUS, status, 0)
        if (status[0] == 0) {
            val log = GLES32.glGetShaderInfoLog(shader)
            GLES32.glDeleteShader(shader)
            throw IllegalStateException("M14GL shader compile failed [$label]: $log")
        }
        return shader
    }

    /** Drain + log pending GL errors (assert-free; called occasionally). */
    fun checkGlErrors(tag: String, where: String) {
        var error = GLES32.glGetError()
        if (error == GLES32.GL_NO_ERROR) return
        while (error != GLES32.GL_NO_ERROR) {
            Log.w(tag, "M14GL GL error 0x${Integer.toHexString(error)} at $where")
            error = GLES32.glGetError()
        }
    }
}

==> m14_cinepro/app/src/main/java/com/m14/cinepro/hud/AudioMeterView.kt <==
package com.m14.cinepro.hud

import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.graphics.LinearGradient
import android.graphics.Paint
import android.graphics.Shader
import android.view.View

/**
 * M14 CinePro — stereo audio meter (viewfinder scopes, bottom-right of the HUD).
 *
 * Two horizontal bars (L top, R bottom) on a -60..0 dBFS scale:
 *  - RMS fill with a green -> yellow -> red level gradient.
 *  - White peak-hold tick at the peak position (decay is already applied upstream by the
 *    AudioEngine, this view is deliberately dumb).
 *  - Thin white reference marks with tiny labels at -18 and -20 dBFS (VU/PPM reference,
 *    see Models.AudioMeteringMode / stage5_media.md §5.6).
 */
class AudioMeterView(context: Context) : View(context) {

    private var peakL = -60f
    private var rmsL = -60f
    private var peakR = -60f
    private var rmsR = -60f

    private val bgPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply { color = 0xB314181F.toInt() }
    private val borderPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        style = Paint.Style.STROKE
        strokeWidth = resources.displayMetrics.density
        color = 0x33F2F4F7
    }
    private val refPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        strokeWidth = resources.displayMetrics.density * 0.8f
        color = 0xB3FFFFFF.toInt()
    }
    private val peakPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        strokeWidth = resources.displayMetrics.density * 2f
        color = 0xFFFFFFFF.toInt()
    }
    private val labelPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        color = 0xE6F2F4F7.toInt()
        textSize = 8f * resources.displayMetrics.density
        isFakeBoldText = true
    }
    private val dimLabelPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        color = 0x80F2F4F7.toInt()
        textSize = 8f * resources.displayMetrics.density
    }

    private val rmsPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG)
    private var gradientShader: LinearGradient? = null
    private var gradientWidth = 0f

    init {
        contentDescription = "Audio meters"
    }

    /** dBFS levels, expected in -60..0. Values outside are clamped. */
    fun setLevels(peakLeftDb: Float, rmsLeftDb: Float, peakRightDb: Float, rmsRightDb: Float) {
        peakL = peakLeftDb.coerceIn(-60f, 0f)
        rmsL = rmsLeftDb.coerceIn(-60f, 0f)
        peakR = peakRightDb.coerceIn(-60f, 0f)
        rmsR = rmsRightDb.coerceIn(-60f, 0f)
        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
    }

    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
        super.onDraw(canvas)
        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), bgPaint)
        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), borderPaint)

        val d = resources.displayMetrics.density
        val pad = 6f * d
        val gutter = 12f * d // left gutter for the L / R channel letters
        val barTopPad = 4f * d

        val barX0 = pad + gutter
        val barX1 = width - pad
        val barW = barX1 - barX0
        if (barW <= 0f) return

        val barH = (height - pad * 2f - barTopPad * 2f) / 2f
        if (barH <= 0f) return

        if (gradientShader == null || gradientWidth != barW) {
            gradientShader = LinearGradient(
                barX0, 0f, barX1, 0f,
                intArrayOf(0xFF2ECC71.toInt(), 0xFFF1C40F.toInt(), 0xFFE74C3C.toInt()),
                floatArrayOf(0f, 0.72f, 1f),
                Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
            )
            rmsPaint.shader = gradientShader
            gradientWidth = barW
        }

        drawChannel(canvas, barX0, barX1, pad, barH, "L", rmsL, peakL, showRefLabels = true)
        drawChannel(
            canvas, barX0, barX1, pad + barH + barTopPad * 2f, barH,
            "R", rmsR, peakR, showRefLabels = false
        )

        // Scale endpoints (-60 .. 0 dBFS).
        canvas.drawText("-60", barX0, height - pad * 0.2f, dimLabelPaint)
        val zeroW = labelPaint.measureText("0")
        canvas.drawText("0", barX1 - zeroW, height - pad * 0.2f, labelPaint)
    }

    private fun drawChannel(
        canvas: Canvas,
        x0: Float,
        x1: Float,
        top: Float,
        barH: Float,
        letter: String,
        rmsDb: Float,
        peakDb: Float,
        showRefLabels: Boolean
    ) {
        val bottom = top + barH

        // Channel letter.
        canvas.drawText(letter, x0 - resources.displayMetrics.density * 9f, top + barH * 0.78f, labelPaint)

        // RMS fill.
        val rmsX = dbToX(x0, x1, rmsDb)
        canvas.drawRect(x0, top, rmsX, bottom, rmsPaint)

        // Peak-hold tick.
        val peakX = dbToX(x0, x1, peakDb)
        canvas.drawLine(peakX, top, peakX, bottom, peakPaint)

        // Reference marks at -18 and -20 dBFS.
        for (ref in intArrayOf(-18, -20)) {
            val rx = dbToX(x0, x1, ref.toFloat())
            canvas.drawLine(rx, top, rx, bottom, refPaint)
            if (showRefLabels) {
                val label = "$ref"
                val w = labelPaint.measureText(label)
                val lx = (rx - w / 2f).coerceIn(x0, x1 - w)
                canvas.drawText(label, lx, top - 1f, labelPaint)
            }
        }

        // 0 dBFS end marker.
        canvas.drawLine(x1, top, x1, bottom, refPaint)
    }

    private fun dbToX(x0: Float, x1: Float, db: Float): Float {
        val t = ((db + 60f) / 60f).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
        return x0 + t * (x1 - x0)
    }

    override fun onMeasure(widthMeasureSpec: Int, heightMeasureSpec: Int) {
        val d = resources.displayMetrics.density
        val w = resolve((160f * d).toInt(), widthMeasureSpec)
        val h = resolve((110f * d).toInt(), heightMeasureSpec)
        setMeasuredDimension(w, h)
    }

    private fun resolve(wanted: Int, spec: Int): Int {
        val mode = View.MeasureSpec.getMode(spec)
        val size = View.MeasureSpec.getSize(spec)
        return when (mode) {
            View.MeasureSpec.EXACTLY -> size
            View.MeasureSpec.AT_MOST -> minOf(wanted, size)
            else -> wanted
        }
    }
}

==> m14_cinepro/app/src/main/java/com/m14/cinepro/hud/FocusOverlayView.kt <==
package com.m14.cinepro.hud

import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.graphics.Color
import android.graphics.Paint
import android.os.SystemClock
import android.view.GestureDetector
import android.view.MotionEvent
import android.view.View
import com.m14.cinepro.core.SettingsStore
import kotlin.math.abs

/**
 * M14 CinePro — full-size focus/touch overlay drawn UNDER the HUD bars, ABOVE the guides.
 *
 * Responsibilities:
 *  - Owns ALL viewfinder-area gestures: single tap -> focus tap (normalized coords),
 *    double tap + fast vertical swipe (> 80 dp) -> HUD chrome toggle, forwarded via the
 *    [onTap] / [onDoubleTap] / [onVerticalSwipe] callbacks wired by [HudController].
 *  - Draws the animated white corner-bracket tap box.
 *  - Draws persistent F1/F2/F3 focus markers from [SettingsStore.focusMarkers].
 */
class FocusOverlayView(context: Context, private val settings: SettingsStore) : View(context) {

    companion object {
        private const val TAP_BOX_ANIM_MS = 700L
    }

    /** Single tap on the picture area, normalized 0..1 x / y. */
    var onTap: ((Float, Float) -> Unit)? = null

    /** Double tap anywhere on the picture area — HUD chrome toggle. */
    var onDoubleTap: (() -> Unit)? = null

    /** Fast vertical swipe > 80 dp — HUD chrome toggle. */
    var onVerticalSwipe: (() -> Unit)? = null

    private var tapNx = -1f
    private var tapNy = -1f
    private var tapStartMs = 0L

    private val density = resources.displayMetrics.density

    private val bracketPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        color = Color.WHITE
        strokeWidth = 2.5f * density
        strokeCap = Paint.Cap.ROUND
    }
    private val markerPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        style = Paint.Style.STROKE
        color = 0xFF3DDC84.toInt()
        strokeWidth = 2.2f * density
    }
    private val markerFillPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        style = Paint.Style.FILL
        color = 0x663DDC84.toInt()
    }
    private val markerTextPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        color = Color.WHITE
        textSize = 11f * density
        isFakeBoldText = true
        setShadowLayer(3f, 0f, 0f, 0xB3000000.toInt())
    }

    private val gestures = GestureDetector(context, object : GestureDetector.SimpleOnGestureListener() {
        override fun onDown(e: MotionEvent): Boolean = true

        override fun onSingleTapConfirmed(e: MotionEvent): Boolean {
            val nx = (e.x / width.coerceAtLeast(1)).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
            val ny = (e.y / height.coerceAtLeast(1)).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
            showTapBox(nx, ny)
            onTap?.invoke(nx, ny)
            return true
        }

        override fun onDoubleTap(e: MotionEvent): Boolean {
            onDoubleTap?.invoke()
            return true
        }

        override fun onFling(e1: MotionEvent?, e2: MotionEvent, velocityX: Float, velocityY: Float): Boolean {
            val dy = e2.y - (e1?.y ?: e2.y)
            val dx = e2.x - (e1?.x ?: e2.x)
            if (abs(dy) > 80f * density && abs(dy) > abs(dx) && abs(velocityY) > 400f * density) {
                onVerticalSwipe?.invoke()
                return true
            }
            return false
        }
    })

    init {
        isClickable = true
        isFocusable = false
        contentDescription = "Viewfinder touch area"
        setOnTouchListener { _, event -> gestures.onTouchEvent(event) }
    }

    /** Show the animated focus box at the given normalized position. */
    fun showTapBox(nx: Float, ny: Float) {
        tapNx = nx.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
        tapNy = ny.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
        tapStartMs = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
    }

    /** Re-read focus markers from settings and redraw. */
    fun refreshMarkers() {
        invalidate()
    }

    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
        super.onDraw(canvas)
        val w = width.toFloat()
        val h = height.toFloat()
        if (w <= 0f || h <= 0f) return

        drawMarkers(canvas, w, h)

        if (tapStartMs > 0L) {
            val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - tapStartMs
            if (elapsed >= TAP_BOX_ANIM_MS) {
                tapStartMs = 0L
            } else {
                drawTapBox(canvas, w, h, elapsed / TAP_BOX_ANIM_MS.toFloat())
                postInvalidateOnAnimation()
            }
        }
    }

    private fun drawTapBox(canvas: Canvas, w: Float, h: Float, progress: Float) {
        // Box settles from a large bracket ring down to the target size while fading out.
        val targetHalf = 34f * density
        val half = targetHalf + (1f - progress) * 22f * density
        val cx = tapNx * w
        val cy = tapNy * h
        val len = half * 0.42f
        val prevAlpha = bracketPaint.alpha
        bracketPaint.alpha = ((1f - progress) * 255f).toInt().coerceIn(0, 255)

        val x0 = cx - half
        val x1 = cx + half
        val y0 = cy - half
        val y1 = cy + half

        // Four corner brackets.
        canvas.drawLine(x0, y0, x0 + len, y0, bracketPaint)
        canvas.drawLine(x0, y0, x0, y0 + len, bracketPaint)
        canvas.drawLine(x1 - len, y0, x1, y0, bracketPaint)
        canvas.drawLine(x1, y0, x1, y0 + len, bracketPaint)
        canvas.drawLine(x0, y1, x0 + len, y1, bracketPaint)
        canvas.drawLine(x0, y1 - len, x0, y1, bracketPaint)
        canvas.drawLine(x1 - len, y1, x1, y1, bracketPaint)
        canvas.drawLine(x1, y1 - len, x1, y1, bracketPaint)

        // Center dot.
        canvas.drawPoint(cx, cy, bracketPaint)
        bracketPaint.alpha = prevAlpha
    }

    private fun drawMarkers(canvas: Canvas, w: Float, h: Float) {
        for (i in 0..2) {
            val m = settings.focusMarkers.getOrNull(i) ?: continue
            val cx = m[0] * w
            val cy = m[1] * h
            val r = 9f * density
            canvas.drawCircle(cx, cy, r, markerFillPaint)
            canvas.drawCircle(cx, cy, r, markerPaint)
            val label = "F${i + 1}"
            canvas.drawText(label, cx + r + 3f * density, cy + markerTextPaint.textSize * 0.35f, markerTextPaint)
        }
    }
}

==> m14_cinepro/app/src/main/java/com/m14/cinepro/hud/GuidesOverlayView.kt <==
package com.m14.cinepro.hud

import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.graphics.Color
import android.graphics.Paint
import android.graphics.RectF
import android.view.View
import com.m14.cinepro.core.Models
import com.m14.cinepro.core.SettingsStore
import java.util.Locale

/**
 * M14 CinePro — full-size framing overlay drawn UNDER the HUD bars, ABOVE the viewfinder.
 *
 * Renders (all driven straight from [SettingsStore] at draw time):
 *  - Aspect mask bars for [Models.GuidePreset] (cinema letterboxing, 2.39 / 2.00 / 1.85 / 4:3 / 9:16).
 *  - Grid overlays per [Models.GridOverlay]: THIRDS / CROSSHAIR / HORIZON.
 *  - De-squeeze readout per [Models.DesqueezePreset]: with the GL preview horizontally
 *    stretched by the anamorphic factor (stage4_shaders.md §9, matrix.setScale(f, 1.0f)),
 *    the full frame already represents the projected aspect — the overlay annotates it with
 *    the effective delivery ratio and, when no aspect guide is active, draws subtle corner
 *    ticks so the operator can see de-squeeze is armed.
 */
class GuidesOverlayView(context: Context, private val settings: SettingsStore) : View(context) {

    private val maskPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        color = Color.BLACK
        alpha = 235
    }
    private val linePaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        color = Color.WHITE
        alpha = 85
        strokeWidth = resources.displayMetrics.density
    }
    private val accentPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        color = 0xFF3DDC84.toInt()
        alpha = 190
        strokeWidth = resources.displayMetrics.density * 1.6f
    }
    private val framePaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        style = Paint.Style.STROKE
        color = Color.WHITE
        alpha = 70
        strokeWidth = resources.displayMetrics.density
    }
    private val textPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        color = Color.WHITE
        alpha = 190
        textSize = 11f * resources.displayMetrics.density
        isFakeBoldText = true
    }

    private val tmpRect = RectF()

    init {
        importantForAccessibility = IMPORTANT_FOR_ACCESSIBILITY_NO
        contentDescription = "Framing guides"
    }

    /** Re-read settings and redraw (called by HudController.refreshAll). */
    fun refresh() {
        invalidate()
    }

    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
        super.onDraw(canvas)
        val w = width.toFloat()
        val h = height.toFloat()
        if (w <= 0f || h <= 0f) return

        val guide = settings.guide
        var frame: RectF? = null

        if (guide != Models.GuidePreset.OFF) {
            frame = fitRatio(w, h, guide.ratio)
            drawMask(canvas, w, h, frame)
            canvas.drawRect(frame, framePaint)
            canvas.drawText(guide.label, frame.centerX() - textPaint.measureText(guide.label) / 2f,
                frame.bottom - 8f * resources.displayMetrics.density, textPaint)
        }

        when (settings.grid) {
            Models.GridOverlay.THIRDS -> drawThirds(canvas, frame ?: RectF(0f, 0f, w, h))
            Models.GridOverlay.CROSSHAIR -> drawCrosshair(canvas, w, h)
