            Models.GridOverlay.HORIZON -> drawHorizon(canvas, w, h)
            Models.GridOverlay.NONE -> Unit
        }

        val dz = settings.desqueeze
        if (dz != Models.DesqueezePreset.X100) {
            drawDesqueeze(canvas, w, h, dz, guide)
        }
    }

    private fun fitRatio(w: Float, h: Float, ratio: Float): RectF {
        val viewRatio = w / h
        return if (ratio >= viewRatio) {
            val fh = w / ratio
            RectF(0f, (h - fh) / 2f, w, (h + fh) / 2f)
        } else {
            val fw = h * ratio
            RectF((w - fw) / 2f, 0f, (w + fw) / 2f, h)
        }
    }

    private fun drawMask(canvas: Canvas, w: Float, h: Float, frame: RectF) {
        // Top band.
        if (frame.top > 0f) canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, w, frame.top, maskPaint)
        // Bottom band.
        if (frame.bottom < h) canvas.drawRect(0f, frame.bottom, w, h, maskPaint)
        // Left pillar.
        if (frame.left > 0f) canvas.drawRect(0f, frame.top, frame.left, frame.bottom, maskPaint)
        // Right pillar.
        if (frame.right < w) canvas.drawRect(frame.right, frame.top, w, frame.bottom, maskPaint)
    }

    private fun drawThirds(canvas: Canvas, area: RectF) {
        val w3 = area.width() / 3f
        val h3 = area.height() / 3f
        for (i in 1..2) {
            val x = area.left + w3 * i
            val y = area.top + h3 * i
            canvas.drawLine(x, area.top, x, area.bottom, linePaint)
            canvas.drawLine(area.left, y, area.right, y, linePaint)
        }
    }

    private fun drawCrosshair(canvas: Canvas, w: Float, h: Float) {
        val cx = w / 2f
        val cy = h / 2f
        val len = 22f * resources.displayMetrics.density
        val gap = 6f * resources.displayMetrics.density
        canvas.drawLine(cx - len - gap, cy, cx - gap, cy, linePaint)
        canvas.drawLine(cx + gap, cy, cx + len + gap, cy, linePaint)
        canvas.drawLine(cx, cy - len - gap, cx, cy - gap, linePaint)
        canvas.drawLine(cx, cy + gap, cx, cy + len + gap, linePaint)
        canvas.drawPoint(cx, cy, accentPaint)
    }

    private fun drawHorizon(canvas: Canvas, w: Float, h: Float) {
        val cy = h / 2f
        canvas.drawLine(0f, cy, w, cy, linePaint)
        val tick = 10f * resources.displayMetrics.density
        canvas.drawLine(0f, cy - tick, 0f, cy + tick, accentPaint)
        canvas.drawLine(w, cy - tick, w, cy + tick, accentPaint)
    }

    private fun drawDesqueeze(
        canvas: Canvas,
        w: Float,
        h: Float,
        dz: Models.DesqueezePreset,
        guide: Models.GuidePreset
    ) {
        val d = resources.displayMetrics.density
        val finalRatio = (w / h) * dz.factor
        val label = String.format(Locale.US, "%s -> %.2f:1", dz.label, finalRatio)
        val tw = textPaint.measureText(label)
        val baseline = h - 12f * d

        if (guide == Models.GuidePreset.OFF) {
            // Corner ticks so the operator sees the de-squeeze frame is armed.
            val tick = 16f * d
            val inset = 8f * d
            val corners = floatArrayOf(
                inset, inset, inset + tick, inset,                                    // TL h
                inset, inset, inset, inset + tick,                                    // TL v
                w - inset - tick, inset, w - inset, inset,                            // TR h
                w - inset, inset, w - inset, inset + tick,                            // TR v
                inset, h - inset, inset + tick, h - inset,                            // BL h
                inset, h - inset - tick, inset, h - inset,                            // BL v
                w - inset - tick, h - inset, w - inset, h - inset,                    // BR h
                w - inset, h - inset - tick, w - inset, h - inset                     // BR v
            )
            var i = 0
            while (i < corners.size) {
                canvas.drawLine(corners[i], corners[i + 1], corners[i + 2], corners[i + 3], accentPaint)
                i += 4
            }
        }

        tmpRect.set(w / 2f - tw / 2f - 6f * d, baseline - textPaint.textSize - 4f * d,
            w / 2f + tw / 2f + 6f * d, baseline + 4f * d)
        canvas.drawText(label, tmpRect.centerX() - tw / 2f, baseline, textPaint)
    }
}

==> m14_cinepro/app/src/main/java/com/m14/cinepro/hud/HistogramView.kt <==
package com.m14.cinepro.hud

import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.graphics.Paint
import android.view.View
import com.m14.cinepro.core.Models
import java.nio.ByteBuffer
import java.nio.ByteOrder
import kotlin.math.min

/**
 * M14 CinePro — 64-bin RGB display histogram (viewfinder scopes, bottom-left of the HUD).
 *
 * Input: the fresh ByteBuffer copy delivered by [com.m14.cinepro.core.EngineListener.onHistogram]
 * — 192 little-endian uint32 counts laid out as R=[0,64) G=[64,128) B=[128,192)
 * (see Models.HISTOGRAM_BINS, computed at 10 fps by histogram_compute.glsl).
 *
 * Render: 64 vertical bars with 2 px gaps; the R, G and B channel columns are overlaid with
 * alpha (screen-style additive look), each normalized against the global max so channel
 * relationships stay readable. Backing is semi-transparent dark cinema panels.
 */
class HistogramView(context: Context) : View(context) {

    private val bins = IntArray(Models.HISTOGRAM_BINS * 3)

    private val bgPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        color = 0xB314181F.toInt()
    }
    private val borderPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        style = Paint.Style.STROKE
        strokeWidth = resources.displayMetrics.density
        color = 0x33F2F4F7
    }
    private val redPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply { color = 0xB0FF3B30.toInt() }
    private val greenPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply { color = 0xB032D74B.toInt() }
    private val bluePaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply { color = 0xB00A84FF.toInt() }

    private val padPx: Float
    private val gapPx: Float = 2f * resources.displayMetrics.density

    init {
        val d = resources.displayMetrics.density
        padPx = 5f * d
        contentDescription = "RGB histogram"
    }

    /** Store a 192-int copy of the incoming histogram buffer and schedule a redraw. */
    fun onHistogram(buffer: ByteBuffer) {
        synchronized(bins) {
            val dup = buffer.duplicate().order(ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN)
            var i = 0
            val n = min(bins.size, dup.remaining() / 4)
            while (i < n) {
                bins[i] = dup.int.coerceAtLeast(0)
                i++
            }
            while (i < bins.size) {
                bins[i] = 0
                i++
            }
        }
        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
    }

    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
        super.onDraw(canvas)
        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), bgPaint)
        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), borderPaint)

        val innerW = width - padPx * 2f
        val innerH = height - padPx * 2f
        if (innerW <= 0f || innerH <= 0f) return

        val barCount = Models.HISTOGRAM_BINS
        val barW = (innerW - gapPx * (barCount - 1)) / barCount
        if (barW <= 0f) return

        val maxCount: Int = synchronized(bins) {
            var m = 1
            for (v in bins) if (v > m) m = v
            m
        }.coerceAtLeast(1)

        val bottom = height - padPx
        val maxF = maxCount.toFloat()

        synchronized(bins) {
            for (i in 0 until barCount) {
                val x = padPx + i * (barW + gapPx)
                val r = bins[i] / maxF
                val g = bins[barCount + i] / maxF
                val b = bins[barCount * 2 + i] / maxF
                val hr = r * innerH
                val hg = g * innerH
                val hb = b * innerH
                // Overlaid channels: blue first so red/green sit on top (classic RGB parade mix).
                if (hb > 0f) canvas.drawRect(x, bottom - hb, x + barW, bottom, bluePaint)
                if (hg > 0f) canvas.drawRect(x, bottom - hg, x + barW, bottom, greenPaint)
                if (hr > 0f) canvas.drawRect(x, bottom - hr, x + barW, bottom, redPaint)
            }
        }
    }

    override fun onMeasure(widthMeasureSpec: Int, heightMeasureSpec: Int) {
        val d = resources.displayMetrics.density
        val w = View.resolveSize((200f * d).toInt(), widthMeasureSpec)
        val h = View.resolveSize((110f * d).toInt(), heightMeasureSpec)
        setMeasuredDimension(w, h)
    }
}

==> m14_cinepro/app/src/main/java/com/m14/cinepro/hud/HudController.kt <==
package com.m14.cinepro.hud

import android.app.Activity
import android.content.Context
import android.content.res.ColorStateList
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.graphics.Color
import android.graphics.Paint
import android.graphics.Typeface
import android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.Looper
import android.os.SystemClock
import android.text.TextUtils
import android.view.GestureDetector
import android.view.Gravity
import android.view.MotionEvent
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import android.widget.FrameLayout
import android.widget.HorizontalScrollView
import android.widget.LinearLayout
import android.widget.PopupWindow
import android.widget.SeekBar
import android.widget.Space
import android.widget.TextView
import com.m14.cinepro.R
import com.m14.cinepro.core.Models
import com.m14.cinepro.core.SettingsStore
import java.nio.ByteBuffer
import java.util.Locale
import kotlin.math.abs
import kotlin.math.min
import kotlin.math.roundToInt

/**
 * M14 CinePro — programmatic landscape cinema HUD.
 *
 * Layered into the activity's [R.id.hud_root] FrameLayout (the GL viewfinder sits at index 0,
 * added by MainActivity):
 *
 *   [1] GuidesOverlayView   — aspect masks / grids / de-squeeze annotation
 *   [2] FocusOverlayView    — tap box animation + F1/F2/F3 markers, owns viewfinder gestures
 *   [3] Top bar             — FORMAT BITRATE SHUTTER ISO WB TIMECODE STATUS pills
 *   [4] Bottom container    — histogram + audio meter row, then toggle pill bar
 *   [5] Transport strip     — REC / SLATE / EIS / TORCH
 *
 * Every pill opens a popup panel (PopupWindow, rounded dark cinema style). All chrome hides
 * with a fast vertical swipe or a double tap on the picture. TAG "M14Hud".
 */
class HudController(
    private val activity: Activity,
    val settings: SettingsStore,
    private val hooks: Hooks
) {

    /** Decisions delegated back to MainActivity. */
    interface Hooks {
        fun onRecordToggle()
        fun onSlateOpen()
        fun onEisToggled(on: Boolean)
        fun onTorchToggled(on: Boolean)
        fun onFormatChanged(preset: Models.ResPreset)      // MainActivity rebuilds the session
        fun onBitrateChanged(p: Models.BitratePreset)
        fun onFocusTap(nx: Float, ny: Float)               // normalized viewfinder coords
        fun onMarkerTap(index: Int)                        // 0..2 short tap -> AF at marker
        fun onMarkerLongPress(index: Int)                  // save last tap point as marker
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Optional engine wiring (installed by MainActivity; HUD never touches engine classes)
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    /** MainActivity opens the framework document picker for .cube import. */
    var lutImportRequester: (() -> Unit)? = null

    /** MainActivity forwards to GlViewfinder.applyLut(preset, path). */
    var lutApplier: ((Models.LutPreset, String?) -> Unit)? = null

    /** MainActivity pushes changed exposure/WB settings into the camera engine. */
    var onSettingsDirty: (() -> Unit)? = null

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // State + views
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    private val mainHandler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())
    private val density = activity.resources.displayMetrics.density

    private val root: FrameLayout =
        (activity.findViewById(R.id.hud_root) as? FrameLayout) ?: createFallbackRoot()

    private lateinit var guidesOverlay: GuidesOverlayView
    private lateinit var focusOverlay: FocusOverlayView
    private lateinit var topBar: LinearLayout
    private lateinit var bottomContainer: LinearLayout
    private lateinit var transportStrip: LinearLayout
    private lateinit var histogramView: HistogramView
    private lateinit var audioMeter: AudioMeterView
    private lateinit var recButton: RecButton

    private lateinit var formatPill: TextView
    private lateinit var bitratePill: TextView
    private lateinit var shutterPill: TextView
    private lateinit var isoPill: TextView
    private lateinit var wbPill: TextView
    private lateinit var tcPill: TextView
    private lateinit var statusPill: TextView
    private lateinit var peakPill: TextView
    private lateinit var zebraPill: TextView
    private lateinit var fcPill: TextView
    private lateinit var lutPill: TextView
    private lateinit var guidesPill: TextView
    private lateinit var squeezePill: TextView
    private lateinit var eisPill: TextView
    private lateinit var torchPill: TextView
    private val markerPills = arrayOfNulls<TextView>(3)

    private var popup: PopupWindow? = null
    private val panelChips = ArrayList<ChipRef>()
    private var chromeVisible = true
    private var lastTap: FloatArray? = null

    private var actionStatus = "—"
    private var systemStatus = ""
    private var telemetryHint = ""

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Construction
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    init {
        buildOverlays()
        buildTopBar()
        buildBottomContainer()
        buildTransportStrip()
        installRootGestures()
        refreshAll()
    }

    private fun createFallbackRoot(): FrameLayout {
        val f = FrameLayout(activity)
        f.id = R.id.hud_root
        activity.addContentView(
            f,
            ViewGroup.LayoutParams(
                ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT
            )
        )
        return f
    }

    private fun buildOverlays() {
        guidesOverlay = GuidesOverlayView(activity, settings)
        root.addView(
            guidesOverlay,
            FrameLayout.LayoutParams(
                ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT
            )
        )
        focusOverlay = FocusOverlayView(activity, settings)
        root.addView(
            focusOverlay,
            FrameLayout.LayoutParams(
                ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT
            )
        )
        focusOverlay.onTap = { nx, ny ->
            lastTap = floatArrayOf(nx, ny)
            hooks.onFocusTap(nx, ny)
        }
        focusOverlay.onDoubleTap = { toggleChrome() }
        focusOverlay.onVerticalSwipe = { toggleChrome() }
    }

    private fun buildTopBar() {
        topBar = LinearLayout(activity).apply {
            orientation = LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL
            gravity = Gravity.CENTER_VERTICAL
            background = barBg()
            val pad = dp(6f)
            setPadding(pad, pad, pad, pad)
        }

        formatPill = makePill(settings.resolution.label) { panelFormat(it) }
        bitratePill = makePill(settings.bitrate.label) { panelBitrate(it) }
        shutterPill = makePill(settings.shutterLabel()) { panelShutter(it) }
        isoPill = makePill(settings.isoLabel()) { panelIso(it) }
        wbPill = makePill(settings.wbLabel()) { panelWb(it) }
        tcPill = makePill("00:00:00:00", mono = true, sizeSp = 14f, onClick = null)
        statusPill = makePill("—", onClick = null).apply {
            gravity = Gravity.CENTER or Gravity.CENTER_VERTICAL
            ellipsize = TextUtils.TruncateAt.END
        }

        topBar.addView(formatPill, pillLp())
        topBar.addView(bitratePill, pillLp())
        topBar.addView(shutterPill, pillLp())
        topBar.addView(isoPill, pillLp())
        topBar.addView(wbPill, pillLp())
        topBar.addView(tcPill, pillLp())
        topBar.addView(
            statusPill,
            LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, 1f)
        )

        root.addView(
            topBar,
            FrameLayout.LayoutParams(
                ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, Gravity.TOP
            )
        )
    }

    private fun buildBottomContainer() {
        bottomContainer = LinearLayout(activity).apply {
            orientation = LinearLayout.VERTICAL
        }

        // Scopes row: histogram bottom-left, audio meter bottom-right, above the toggle bar.
        val scopesRow = LinearLayout(activity).apply {
            orientation = LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL
            gravity = Gravity.BOTTOM
        }
        histogramView = HistogramView(activity)
        val histLp = LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
            ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT
        )
        histLp.marginStart = dp(10f)
        histLp.bottomMargin = dp(10f)
        scopesRow.addView(histogramView, histLp)

        scopesRow.addView(
            Space(activity),
            LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0, 1, 1f)
        )

        audioMeter = AudioMeterView(activity)
        val meterLp = LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
            ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT
        )
        meterLp.marginEnd = dp(10f)
        meterLp.bottomMargin = dp(10f)
        scopesRow.addView(audioMeter, meterLp)

        bottomContainer.addView(
            scopesRow,
            LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
                ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT
            )
        )

        // Toggle pill bar.
        val pillsRow = LinearLayout(activity).apply {
            orientation = LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL
            gravity = Gravity.CENTER_VERTICAL
            background = barBg()
            val pad = dp(6f)
            setPadding(pad, pad, pad, pad)
        }

        peakPill = makePill(activity.getString(R.string.btn_peak)) { panelPeak(it) }
        zebraPill = makePill(activity.getString(R.string.btn_zebra)) { panelZebra(it) }
        fcPill = makePill(activity.getString(R.string.btn_false_color)).apply {
            setOnClickListener {
                settings.falseColorOn = !settings.falseColorOn
                refreshAll()
            }
        }
        lutPill = makePill(activity.getString(R.string.btn_lut)) { panelLut(it) }
        guidesPill = makePill(activity.getString(R.string.btn_guides)) { panelGuides(it) }
        squeezePill = makePill(activity.getString(R.string.btn_desqueeze)) { panelSqueeze(it) }

        pillsRow.addView(peakPill, pillLp())
        pillsRow.addView(zebraPill, pillLp())
        pillsRow.addView(fcPill, pillLp())
        pillsRow.addView(lutPill, pillLp())
        pillsRow.addView(guidesPill, pillLp())
        pillsRow.addView(squeezePill, pillLp())

        for (i in 0..2) {
            val pill = makePill(activity.getString(markerLabelRes(i))).apply {
                setOnClickListener { hooks.onMarkerTap(i) }
                setOnLongClickListener {
                    hooks.onMarkerLongPress(i)
                    true
                }
            }
            markerPills[i] = pill
            pillsRow.addView(pill, pillLp())
        }

        bottomContainer.addView(
            pillsRow,
            LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
                ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT
            )
        )

        root.addView(
            bottomContainer,
            FrameLayout.LayoutParams(
                ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
                Gravity.BOTTOM
            )
        )
    }

    private fun buildTransportStrip() {
        transportStrip = LinearLayout(activity).apply {
            orientation = LinearLayout.VERTICAL
            gravity = Gravity.CENTER_HORIZONTAL
            val pad = dp(12f)
            setPadding(pad, pad, pad, pad)
        }

        recButton = RecButton(activity).apply {
            setOnClickListener { hooks.onRecordToggle() }
        }
        transportStrip.addView(
            recButton,
            verticalStripLp(dp(76f), dp(76f), 0)
        )

        val slateBtn = TextView(activity).apply {
            text = activity.getString(R.string.btn_slate)
            setTextColor(activity.getColor(R.color.hud_text_primary))
            setTextSize(android.util.TypedValue.COMPLEX_UNIT_SP, 12f)
            typeface = Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD
            gravity = Gravity.CENTER
            background = pillBg().apply { cornerRadius = dp(12f).toFloat() }
            maxLines = 1
            setOnClickListener { hooks.onSlateOpen() }
        }
        transportStrip.addView(slateBtn, verticalStripLp(dp(56f), dp(56f), dp(14f)))

        eisPill = makePill(activity.getString(R.string.btn_eis)).apply {
            setOnClickListener {
                settings.eisOn = !settings.eisOn
                refreshAll()
                hooks.onEisToggled(settings.eisOn)
            }
        }
        torchPill = makePill(activity.getString(R.string.btn_torch)).apply {
            setOnClickListener {
                settings.torchOn = !settings.torchOn
                refreshAll()
                hooks.onTorchToggled(settings.torchOn)
            }
        }
        transportStrip.addView(eisPill, verticalStripLp(dp(64f), ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, dp(14f)))
        transportStrip.addView(torchPill, verticalStripLp(dp(64f), ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, dp(12f)))

        val lp = FrameLayout.LayoutParams(
            ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
            Gravity.END or Gravity.CENTER_VERTICAL
        )
        lp.marginEnd = dp(24f)
        root.addView(transportStrip, lp)
    }

    private fun installRootGestures() {
        val rootGestures = GestureDetector(activity, object : GestureDetector.SimpleOnGestureListener() {
            override fun onDown(e: MotionEvent): Boolean = false

            override fun onDoubleTap(e: MotionEvent): Boolean {
                toggleChrome()
                return true
            }

            override fun onFling(e1: MotionEvent?, e2: MotionEvent, velocityX: Float, velocityY: Float): Boolean {
                val dy = e2.y - (e1?.y ?: e2.y)
                val dx = e2.x - (e1?.x ?: e2.x)
                if (abs(dy) > dp(80f) && abs(dy) > abs(dx) && abs(velocityY) > 400f * density) {
                    toggleChrome()
                    return true
                }
                return false
            }
        })
        // Fallback only: FocusOverlayView consumes picture-area touches, bars consume theirs.
        root.setOnTouchListener { _, event -> rootGestures.onTouchEvent(event) }
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Public API (MainActivity + engine callbacks)
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    /** Re-render every pill label + active state from settings. */
    fun refreshAll() {
        formatPill.text = settings.resolution.label
        bitratePill.text = settings.bitrate.label
        shutterPill.text = settings.shutterLabel()
        isoPill.text = settings.isoLabel()
        wbPill.text = settings.wbLabel()

        setActiveBg(peakPill, settings.peakingMode != Models.PeakingMode.OFF)
        setActiveBg(zebraPill, settings.zebraMode != Models.ZebraMode.OFF)
        setActiveBg(fcPill, settings.falseColorOn)
        setActiveBg(lutPill, settings.lutPreset != Models.LutPreset.OFF)
        setActiveBg(
            guidesPill,
            settings.guide != Models.GuidePreset.OFF || settings.grid != Models.GridOverlay.NONE
        )
        setActiveBg(squeezePill, settings.desqueeze != Models.DesqueezePreset.X100)
        for (i in 0..2) markerPills[i]?.let { setActiveBg(it, settings.focusMarkers[i] != null) }
        setActiveBg(eisPill, settings.eisOn)
        setActiveBg(torchPill, settings.torchOn)

        guidesOverlay.refresh()
        focusOverlay.refreshMarkers()
        composeStatus()
        syncPanelChips()
    }

    /** Timecode pill; called at 10 Hz from MainActivity and on engine frame counts. */
    fun updateTimecode(frameIndex: Long, fps: Int) {
        tcPill.text = Models.formatTimecode(frameIndex.coerceAtLeast(0L), fps.coerceAtLeast(1))
    }

    /** Primary status text ("READY", "REC A001C001", engine info lines). */
    fun setStatus(text: String) {
        actionStatus = text.uppercase(Locale.US).ifEmpty { "—" }
        composeStatus()
    }

    /** Tally: red dot compound drawable on the TC pill + REC button state. */
    fun setRecording(recording: Boolean, blink: Boolean) {
        setTally(recording)
        recButton.recording = recording
        recButton.blinkEnabled = blink
    }

    fun onLevels(peakL: Float, rmsL: Float, peakR: Float, rmsR: Float) {
        audioMeter.setLevels(peakL, rmsL, peakR, rmsR)
    }

    fun onHistogram(bins: ByteBuffer) {
        histogramView.onHistogram(bins)
    }

    fun onTelemetry(aeState: Int, afState: Int, flicker: Int) {
        val hints = ArrayList<String>(2)
        when (flicker) {
            1 -> hints.add("FLK 50Hz")
            2 -> hints.add("FLK 60Hz")
        }
        when (afState) {
            3, 4 -> hints.add("AF OK")
        }
        if (aeState == 4) hints.add("AE SRCH")
        telemetryHint = hints.joinToString(" ")
        composeStatus()
    }

    fun onSystemStatus(batteryPct: Int, tempC: Float, thermalHeadroom: Float, freeGb: Float) {
        systemStatus = String.format(
            Locale.US, "%d%% %.0f°C TH%.1f %.1fGB",
            batteryPct.coerceIn(0, 100), tempC, thermalHeadroom, freeGb
        )
        composeStatus()
    }

    /** Show the animated tap box at a normalized position (e.g. F-marker recall). */
    fun showTapBoxAt(nx: Float, ny: Float) {
        focusOverlay.showTapBox(nx, ny)
    }

    fun refreshMarkers() {
        focusOverlay.refreshMarkers()
    }

    /** Last viewfinder tap as [x, y] normalized, or null if the operator has not tapped yet. */
    fun lastTapPoint(): FloatArray? = lastTap?.copyOf()

    /** Called by MainActivity after a successful .cube import. */
    fun refreshAfterLut(path: String?) {
        settings.importedLutPath = path
        settings.lutPreset = Models.LutPreset.IMPORTED
        refreshAll()
    }

    fun destroy() {
        popup?.dismiss()
        popup = null
        panelChips.clear()
        mainHandler.removeCallbacksAndMessages(null)
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Status composition
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    private fun composeStatus() {
        val parts = ArrayList<String>(3)
        if (actionStatus.isNotEmpty()) parts.add(actionStatus)
        if (systemStatus.isNotEmpty()) parts.add(systemStatus)
        if (telemetryHint.isNotEmpty()) parts.add(telemetryHint)
        statusPill.text = parts.joinToString("  ")
    }

    private fun setTally(on: Boolean) {
        if (on) {
            val dot = GradientDrawable().apply {
                shape = GradientDrawable.OVAL
                setColor(activity.getColor(R.color.hud_record_red))
            }
            dot.setBounds(0, 0, dp(9f), dp(9f))
            tcPill.setCompoundDrawables(null, null, dot, null)
            tcPill.compoundDrawablePadding = dp(6f)
        } else {
            tcPill.setCompoundDrawables(null, null, null, null)
        }
    }

    private fun toggleChrome() {
        chromeVisible = !chromeVisible
        val target = if (chromeVisible) 1f else 0f
        val hide = !chromeVisible
        for (v in listOf(topBar, bottomContainer, transportStrip)) {
            v.animate().cancel()
            v.animate()
                .alpha(target)
                .setDuration(180L)
                .withStartAction { v.visibility = View.VISIBLE }
                .withEndAction { if (hide) v.visibility = View.INVISIBLE }
                .start()
        }
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // View factories
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    private fun dp(v: Float): Int = (v * density + 0.5f).toInt()

    private fun accentColor(): Int = activity.getColor(R.color.hud_accent)
    private fun textColor(): Int = activity.getColor(R.color.hud_text_primary)
    private fun dimColor(): Int = activity.getColor(R.color.hud_text_dim)

    private fun barBg(): GradientDrawable = GradientDrawable().apply {
        setColor(activity.getColor(R.color.hud_bg))
    }

    private fun panelBg(): GradientDrawable = GradientDrawable().apply {
        cornerRadius = dp(12f).toFloat()
        setColor(activity.getColor(R.color.panel_bg))
    }

    private fun pillBg(): GradientDrawable = GradientDrawable().apply {
        cornerRadius = dp(10f).toFloat()
        setColor(activity.getColor(R.color.hud_pill_bg))
    }

    private fun makePill(
        label: String,
        mono: Boolean = false,
        sizeSp: Float = 13f,
        onClick: ((View) -> Unit)? = null
    ): TextView = TextView(activity).apply {
        text = label.uppercase(Locale.US)
        setTextColor(textColor())
        setTextSize(android.util.TypedValue.COMPLEX_UNIT_SP, sizeSp)
        typeface = if (mono) Typeface.MONOSPACE else Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD
        background = pillBg()
        val padH = dp(10f)
        val padV = dp(6f)
        setPadding(padH, padV, padH, padV)
        minWidth = dp(54f)
        gravity = Gravity.CENTER
        maxLines = 1
        onClick?.let { setOnClickListener(it) }
    }

    private fun setActiveBg(v: TextView, on: Boolean) {
        (v.background as? GradientDrawable)?.setColor(
            activity.getColor(if (on) R.color.hud_pill_active else R.color.hud_pill_bg)
        )
    }

    private fun pillLp(): LinearLayout.LayoutParams =
        LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
            ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT
        ).apply { marginEnd = dp(6f) }

    private fun sectionLp(): LinearLayout.LayoutParams =
        LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
            ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT
        ).apply { bottomMargin = dp(10f) }

    private fun verticalStripLp(w: Int, h: Int, topMargin: Int): LinearLayout.LayoutParams =
        LinearLayout.LayoutParams(w, h).apply { this.topMargin = topMargin }

    private fun markerLabelRes(i: Int): Int = when (i) {
        0 -> R.string.marker_f1
        1 -> R.string.marker_f2
        else -> R.string.marker_f3
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Popup panel machinery
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    private class ChipRef(val view: TextView, val provider: () -> Boolean)

    private class SliderRowRef(val row: LinearLayout, val seek: SeekBar, val readout: TextView)

    private class ChipItem(val label: String, val active: () -> Boolean, val onClick: () -> Unit)

    private fun syncPanelChips() {
        for (c in panelChips) setActiveBg(c.view, c.provider())
    }

    private fun showPanel(anchor: View, build: LinearLayout.() -> Unit) {
        popup?.dismiss()
        panelChips.clear()

        val content = LinearLayout(activity).apply {
            orientation = LinearLayout.VERTICAL
            val pad = dp(14f)
            setPadding(pad, pad, pad, pad)
            build()
        }
        val wrapper = FrameLayout(activity)
        wrapper.addView(
            content,
            FrameLayout.LayoutParams(
                ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT
            )
        )

        val maxW = min(dp(680f), (root.width * 0.9f).toInt().coerceAtLeast(dp(280f)))
        val pw = PopupWindow(wrapper, maxW, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, true).apply {
            setBackgroundDrawable(panelBg())
            isOutsideTouchable = true
            elevation = dp(10f).toFloat()
        }
        popup = pw

        val widthSpec = View.MeasureSpec.makeMeasureSpec(maxW, View.MeasureSpec.EXACTLY)
        val heightSpec = View.MeasureSpec.makeMeasureSpec(0, View.MeasureSpec.UNSPECIFIED)
        wrapper.measure(widthSpec, heightSpec)

        val loc = IntArray(2)
        anchor.getLocationOnScreen(loc)
        val x = (loc[0] + anchor.width / 2 - maxW / 2)
            .coerceIn(dp(8f), (root.width - maxW - dp(8f)).coerceAtLeast(dp(8f)))
        val y = if (loc[1] < root.height / 2) {
            loc[1] + anchor.height + dp(6f)
        } else {
            (loc[1] - wrapper.measuredHeight - dp(6f)).coerceAtLeast(dp(8f))
        }
        pw.showAtLocation(root, Gravity.NO_GRAVITY, x, y)
    }

    /** Section caption inside a panel. */
    private fun LinearLayout.title(text: String) {
        val t = TextView(activity).apply {
            this.text = text.uppercase(Locale.US)
            setTextColor(dimColor())
            setTextSize(android.util.TypedValue.COMPLEX_UNIT_SP, 11f)
            typeface = Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD
            letterSpacing = 0.12f
        }
        val lp = LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
            ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT
        )
        lp.topMargin = dp(2f)
        lp.bottomMargin = dp(6f)
        addView(t, lp)
    }

    private fun makeChip(item: ChipItem): TextView = TextView(activity).apply {
        text = item.label
        setTextColor(textColor())
        setTextSize(android.util.TypedValue.COMPLEX_UNIT_SP, 12f)
        typeface = Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD
        background = pillBg()
        val padH = dp(12f)
        val padV = dp(8f)
        setPadding(padH, padV, padH, padV)
        gravity = Gravity.CENTER
        maxLines = 1
        minWidth = dp(48f)
        setActiveBg(this, item.active())
        setOnClickListener {
            item.onClick()
            syncPanelChips()
        }
    }

    /** Horizontally scrollable chip group (member extension on the panel content). */
    private fun LinearLayout.chipGroup(items: List<ChipItem>) {
        val scroll = HorizontalScrollView(activity).apply {
            isHorizontalScrollBarEnabled = false
            clipToPadding = false
        }
        val row = LinearLayout(activity).apply { orientation = LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL }
        for (item in items) row.addView(makeChip(item), chipLp())
        scroll.addView(row)
        addView(scroll, sectionLp())
    }

    private fun chipLp(): LinearLayout.LayoutParams =
        LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
            ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT
        ).apply { marginEnd = dp(8f) }

    /** Labelled seek row with live readout; integer domain [minVal, maxVal] with [step]. */
    private fun LinearLayout.slider(
        label: String,
        minVal: Int,
        maxVal: Int,
        step: Int,
        initVal: Int,
        format: (Int) -> String,
        onValue: (Int) -> Unit
    ): SliderRowRef {
        val row = LinearLayout(activity).apply {
            orientation = LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL
            gravity = Gravity.CENTER_VERTICAL
        }

        val lbl = TextView(activity).apply {
            text = label.uppercase(Locale.US)
            setTextColor(dimColor())
            setTextSize(android.util.TypedValue.COMPLEX_UNIT_SP, 11f)
            typeface = Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD
            width = dp(64f)
            maxLines = 1
        }
        row.addView(lbl)

        val seek = SeekBar(activity).apply {
            max = ((maxVal - minVal) / step.coerceAtLeast(1)).coerceAtLeast(1)
            progress = ((initVal - minVal) / step.coerceAtLeast(1)).coerceIn(0, max)
            progressTintList = ColorStateList.valueOf(accentColor())
            thumbTintList = ColorStateList.valueOf(accentColor())
        }
        val readout = TextView(activity).apply {
            setTextColor(textColor())
            setTextSize(android.util.TypedValue.COMPLEX_UNIT_SP, 12f)
            typeface = Typeface.MONOSPACE
            width = dp(112f)
            gravity = Gravity.END or Gravity.CENTER_VERTICAL
            maxLines = 1
        }
        val initV = (minVal + seek.progress * step).coerceIn(minVal, maxVal)
        readout.text = format(initV)

        seek.setOnSeekBarChangeListener(object : SeekBar.OnSeekBarChangeListener {
            override fun onProgressChanged(sb: SeekBar?, progress: Int, fromUser: Boolean) {
                val v = (minVal + progress * step).coerceIn(minVal, maxVal)
                readout.text = format(v)
                if (fromUser) onValue(v)
            }

            override fun onStartTrackingTouch(sb: SeekBar?) = Unit
            override fun onStopTrackingTouch(sb: SeekBar?) = Unit
        })

        row.addView(seek, LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, 1f))
        row.addView(readout)
        addView(row, sectionLp())
        return SliderRowRef(row, seek, readout)
    }

    private fun exposureReadout(): String {
        val eff = settings.effectiveExposureNs()
        return String.format(Locale.US, "≈ %.2f ms  (%.0f µs)", eff / 1_000_000.0, eff / 1_000.0)
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Panels
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    private fun panelFormat(anchor: View) = showPanel(anchor) {
        title("Recording format")
        chipGroup(
            Models.ResPreset.entries.map { p ->
                ChipItem(p.label, { settings.resolution == p }, {
                    settings.resolution = p
                    refreshAll()
                    hooks.onFormatChanged(p)
                    popup?.dismiss()
                })
            }
        )
    }

    private fun panelBitrate(anchor: View) = showPanel(anchor) {
        title("Bitrate")
        chipGroup(
            Models.BitratePreset.entries.map { p ->
                ChipItem(p.label, { settings.bitrate == p }, {
                    settings.bitrate = p
                    refreshAll()
                    hooks.onBitrateChanged(p)
                    popup?.dismiss()
                })
            }
        )
    }

    private fun panelShutter(anchor: View) = showPanel(anchor) {
        title("Shutter")
        val angleBox = LinearLayout(activity).apply { orientation = LinearLayout.VERTICAL }
        val speedBox = LinearLayout(activity).apply { orientation = LinearLayout.VERTICAL }
        val readout = TextView(activity).apply {
            setTextColor(accentColor())
            setTextSize(android.util.TypedValue.COMPLEX_UNIT_SP, 13f)
            typeface = Typeface.MONOSPACE
            gravity = Gravity.END
        }
        var modeBtn: TextView? = null

        fun syncMode() {
            val angleMode = settings.shutterMode == Models.ShutterMode.ANGLE
            modeBtn?.text = if (angleMode) {
                "MODE: ANGLE °  ⇄  SPEED 1/x"
            } else {
                "MODE: SPEED 1/x  ⇄  ANGLE °"
            }
            angleBox.visibility = if (angleMode) View.VISIBLE else View.GONE
            speedBox.visibility = if (angleMode) View.GONE else View.VISIBLE
            readout.text = exposureReadout()
        }

        val btn = TextView(activity).apply {
            setTextColor(textColor())
            setTextSize(android.util.TypedValue.COMPLEX_UNIT_SP, 12f)
            typeface = Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD
            background = pillBg()
            val padH = dp(12f)
            val padV = dp(8f)
            setPadding(padH, padV, padH, padV)
            gravity = Gravity.CENTER
            setOnClickListener {
                settings.shutterMode = if (settings.shutterMode == Models.ShutterMode.ANGLE) {
                    Models.ShutterMode.SPEED
                } else {
                    Models.ShutterMode.ANGLE
                }
                syncMode()
                onSettingsDirty?.invoke()
                refreshAll()
            }
        }
        modeBtn = btn

        // Angle mode: preset chips + continuous angle slider.
        angleBox.chipGroup(
            Models.shutterAnglePresets().map { a ->
                val label = if (a % 1f == 0f) "${a.toInt()}°" else "${a}°"
                ChipItem(label, {
                    settings.shutterMode == Models.ShutterMode.ANGLE &&
                        abs(settings.shutterAngleDeg - a) < 0.05f
                }, {
                    settings.shutterAngleDeg = a
                    onSettingsDirty?.invoke()
                    refreshAll()
                    readout.text = exposureReadout()
                })
            }
        )
        angleBox.slider(
            "Angle", 45, 360, 1, settings.shutterAngleDeg.roundToInt(),
            { "$it°" },
            { v ->
                settings.shutterAngleDeg = v.toFloat()
                onSettingsDirty?.invoke()
                refreshAll()
                readout.text = exposureReadout()
            }
        )

        // Speed mode: 1/x denominator chips.
        speedBox.chipGroup(
            Models.shutterSpeedDenominators().map { d ->
                ChipItem("1/$d", {
                    settings.shutterMode == Models.ShutterMode.SPEED &&
                        settings.shutterSpeedDenominator == d
                }, {
                    settings.shutterSpeedDenominator = d
                    onSettingsDirty?.invoke()
                    refreshAll()
                    readout.text = exposureReadout()
                })
            }
        )

        addView(btn, sectionLp())
        addView(angleBox, LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
            ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT
        ))
        addView(speedBox, LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
            ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT
        ))
        addView(readout, sectionLp())
        syncMode()
    }

    private fun panelIso(anchor: View) = showPanel(anchor) {
        title("Exposure — ISO / EV")
        var isoRow: SliderRowRef? = null
        var evRow: SliderRowRef? = null

        fun syncVis() {
            val manual = settings.manualExposure
            isoRow?.row?.visibility = if (manual) View.VISIBLE else View.GONE
            evRow?.row?.visibility = if (manual) View.GONE else View.VISIBLE
        }

        chipGroup(
            listOf(
                ChipItem("AUTO", { !settings.manualExposure }, {
                    settings.manualExposure = false
                    onSettingsDirty?.invoke()
                    refreshAll()
                    syncVis()
                }),
                ChipItem("MANUAL", { settings.manualExposure }, {
                    settings.manualExposure = true
                    onSettingsDirty?.invoke()
                    refreshAll()
                    syncVis()
                }),
                ChipItem("AE LOCK", { settings.aeLock }, {
                    settings.aeLock = !settings.aeLock
                    onSettingsDirty?.invoke()
                    refreshAll()
                })
            )
        )

        isoRow = slider(
            "ISO", Models.ISO_MIN, Models.ISO_MAX, 100, settings.iso,
            { "ISO $it" },
            { v ->
                settings.iso = v
                onSettingsDirty?.invoke()
                refreshAll()
            }
        )

        evRow = slider(
            "EV", (Models.EV_MIN * 10).roundToInt(), (Models.EV_MAX * 10).roundToInt(), 1,
            (settings.evCompensation * 10).roundToInt(),
            { String.format(Locale.US, "%+.1f EV", it / 10f) },
            { v ->
                settings.evCompensation = v / 10f
                onSettingsDirty?.invoke()
                refreshAll()
            }
        )

        syncVis()
    }

    private fun panelWb(anchor: View) = showPanel(anchor) {
        title("White balance")
        var kelvinRow: SliderRowRef? = null
        var tintRow: SliderRowRef? = null

            val kelvin = settings.wbPreset == Models.WbPreset.KELVIN

            tintRow?.row?.visibility = if (kelvin) View.VISIBLE else View.GONE

            "Kelvin", Models.KELVIN_MIN, Models.KELVIN_MAX, 50, settings.kelvin,
            { "$it K" },
            { v ->
                settings.kelvin = v
                if (settings.wbPreset != Models.WbPreset.KELVIN) {
                    settings.wbPreset = Models.WbPreset.KELVIN

                onSettingsDirty?.invoke()
                refreshAll()
            }
        )

        tintRow = slider(
            "Tint", Models.TINT_MIN, Models.TINT_MAX, 1, settings.tint,
            { String.format(Locale.US, "%+d", it) },
            { v ->
                settings.tint = v
                if (settings.wbPreset != Models.WbPreset.KELVIN) {
                    settings.wbPreset = Models.WbPreset.KELVIN
                }
                onSettingsDirty?.invoke()
                refreshAll()
            }
        )

        chipGroup(
            listOf(
                ChipItem("AWB LOCK", { settings.awbLock }, {
                    settings.awbLock = !settings.awbLock
                    onSettingsDirty?.invoke()
                    refreshAll()
                })
            )
        )

        syncVis()
    }

    private fun wbChipLabel(p: Models.WbPreset): String = when (p) {

    private fun panelZebra(anchor: View) = showPanel(anchor) {
        title("Zebras")
        var thresholdRow: SliderRowRef? = null

        fun syncVis() {

                if (z == Models.ZebraMode.Z95 || z == Models.ZebraMode.Z90) View.VISIBLE else View.GONE
        }

        chipGroup(
            listOf(

                    settings.zebraMode = Models.ZebraMode.Z95
                    refreshAll()

        popup?.dismiss()

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // REC button (idle: solid red circle / recording: white ring + blinking red core)
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    private class RecButton(context: Context) : View(context) {

        var recording: Boolean = false
            set(v) {
                field = v
                invalidate()
            }

        var blinkEnabled: Boolean = true

        private val density = resources.displayMetrics.density
        private val fillPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG)
        private val ringPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
            style = Paint.Style.STROKE
            strokeWidth = 3f * density
            strokeCap = Paint.Cap.ROUND
            color = Color.WHITE
        }

        init {
            contentDescription = context.getString(R.string.btn_record)
        }

        override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
            super.onDraw(canvas)
            val cx = width / 2f
            val cy = height / 2f
            val r = min(width, height) / 2f - 2f * density
            val red = context.getColor(R.color.hud_record_red)

            if (!recording) {
                fillPaint.color = red
                fillPaint.alpha = 255
                canvas.drawCircle(cx, cy, r, fillPaint)
            } else {
                canvas.drawCircle(cx, cy, r, ringPaint)
                val blinkOn = !blinkEnabled || (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() / 500L) % 2L == 0L
                fillPaint.color = red
                fillPaint.alpha = if (blinkOn) 255 else 70
                canvas.drawCircle(cx, cy, r - 5f * density, fillPaint)
                fillPaint.alpha = 255
                postInvalidateDelayed(500L)
            }
        }
    }
}

==> m14_cinepro/app/src/main/java/com/m14/cinepro/hud/SlateDialog.kt <==
package com.m14.cinepro.hud

import android.app.Activity
import android.app.AlertDialog
import android.graphics.Typeface
import android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable
import android.text.InputType
import android.view.Gravity
import android.view.View
import android.view.ViewGroup
import android.widget.CheckBox
import android.widget.EditText
import android.widget.LinearLayout
import android.widget.ScrollView
import android.widget.TextView
import com.m14.cinepro.R
import com.m14.cinepro.core.SettingsStore

/**
 * M14 CinePro — cinema slate dialog (reel / scene / take / director / operator / good-take).
 *
 * Pure framework [AlertDialog] with a fully programmatic dark form. On Save the fields are
 * written through [SettingsStore] (which persists them and feeds the clip metadata registry
 * via RecordingController's SlateManager); [onSave] lets the caller refresh the HUD.
 */
object SlateDialog {

    fun show(activity: Activity, settings: SettingsStore, onSave: () -> Unit) {
        val density = activity.resources.displayMetrics.density
        fun dp(v: Float): Int = (v * density + 0.5f).toInt()

        val textDark = activity.getColor(R.color.hud_text_primary)
        val textDim = activity.getColor(R.color.hud_text_dim)
        val fieldBgColor = 0xFF1A1E26.toInt()

        fun newFieldBg(): GradientDrawable = GradientDrawable().apply {
            shape = GradientDrawable.RECTANGLE
            cornerRadius = dp(10f).toFloat()
            setColor(fieldBgColor)
        }

        fun caption(text: String): TextView = TextView(activity).apply {
            this.text = text
            setTextColor(textDim)
            textSize = 11f
            typeface = Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD
            setAllCaps(true)
            val lp = LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
                ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT
            )
            lp.topMargin = dp(10f)
            lp.bottomMargin = dp(2f)
            layoutParams = lp
        }

        fun field(value: String, inputType: Int, hint: String): EditText =
            EditText(activity).apply {
                setText(value)
                this.hint = hint
                setHintTextColor(textDim)
                setTextColor(textDark)
                textSize = 15f
                this.inputType = inputType
                isSingleLine = true
                background = newFieldBg()
                setPadding(dp(10f), dp(8f), dp(10f), dp(8f))
            }

        val container = LinearLayout(activity).apply {
            orientation = LinearLayout.VERTICAL
            val pad = dp(20f)
            setPadding(pad, dp(8f), pad, 0)
        }

        val summary = TextView(activity).apply {
            text = settings.describeForMetadata()
            setTextColor(textDim)
            textSize = 12f
            typeface = Typeface.MONOSPACE
        }
        container.addView(summary)

        container.addView(caption(activity.getString(R.string.slate_reel)))
        val reelEt = field(settings.reel.toString(), InputType.TYPE_CLASS_NUMBER, "001")
        container.addView(reelEt)

        container.addView(caption(activity.getString(R.string.slate_scene)))
        val sceneEt = field(settings.scene, InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT, "12A")
        container.addView(sceneEt)

        container.addView(caption(activity.getString(R.string.slate_take)))
        val takeEt = field(settings.take.toString(), InputType.TYPE_CLASS_NUMBER, "1")
        container.addView(takeEt)

        container.addView(caption(activity.getString(R.string.slate_director)))
        val directorEt = field(
            settings.director,
            InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_FLAG_CAP_WORDS,
            activity.getString(R.string.slate_director)
        )
        container.addView(directorEt)

        container.addView(caption(activity.getString(R.string.slate_operator)))
        val operatorEt = field(
            settings.operator,
            InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_FLAG_CAP_WORDS,
            activity.getString(R.string.slate_operator)
        )
        container.addView(operatorEt)

        val goodTake = CheckBox(activity).apply {
            text = activity.getString(R.string.slate_good_take)
            setTextColor(textDark)
            textSize = 14f
            isChecked = settings.goodTake
            val lp = LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
                ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT
            )
            lp.topMargin = dp(14f)
            layoutParams = lp
        }
        container.addView(goodTake)

        val scroll = ScrollView(activity).apply {
            isVerticalScrollBarEnabled = false
            addView(
                container,
                ViewGroup.LayoutParams(
                    ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT
                )
            )
        }

        AlertDialog.Builder(activity, android.R.style.Theme_Material_Dialog)
            .setTitle(activity.getString(R.string.slate_title))
            .setView(scroll)
            .setPositiveButton(activity.getString(R.string.slate_ok)) { dialog, _ ->
                settings.reel = reelEt.text.toString().trim().toIntOrNull()?.coerceAtLeast(1)
                    ?: settings.reel
                val scene = sceneEt.text.toString().trim()
                if (scene.isNotEmpty()) settings.scene = scene
                settings.take = takeEt.text.toString().trim().toIntOrNull()?.coerceAtLeast(1)
                    ?: settings.take
                settings.goodTake = goodTake.isChecked
                settings.director = directorEt.text.toString().trim()
                settings.operator = operatorEt.text.toString().trim()
                onSave()
                dialog.dismiss()
            }
            .setNegativeButton(activity.getString(R.string.slate_cancel), null)
            .show()
            .window?.let { w ->
                w.setLayout(dp(420f), ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT)
                w.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER)
            }
    }
}

==> m14_cinepro/app/src/main/java/com/m14/cinepro/MainActivity.kt <==
package com.m14.cinepro

import android.Manifest
import android.app.Activity
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.pm.ActivityInfo
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.graphics.SurfaceTexture
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Bundle
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.Looper
import android.os.SystemClock
import android.util.Log
import android.view.View
import android.view.WindowInsets
import android.view.WindowInsetsController
import android.view.WindowManager
import android.widget.FrameLayout
import android.widget.Toast
import com.m14.cinepro.camera.CameraEngine
import com.m14.cinepro.core.EngineListener
import com.m14.cinepro.core.Models
import com.m14.cinepro.core.SettingsStore
import com.m14.cinepro.gl.GlViewfinder
import com.m14.cinepro.hud.HudController
import com.m14.cinepro.hud.SlateDialog
import com.m14.cinepro.recording.RecordingController
import com.m14.cinepro.recording.StorageHelper
import com.m14.cinepro.sensor.GyroEisController
import com.m14.cinepro.system.SystemMonitor
import java.util.Locale

/**
 * M14 CinePro — MainActivity. Landscape cinema camera wiring hub.
 *
 * Owns: permissions gate, engine/subsystem lifetimes, the [HudController] hook decisions,
 * session (re)builds, the 10 Hz timecode tick and LUT import (framework SAF, zero androidx).
 *
 * Public API for other modules: none — everything is driven through HudController.Hooks.
 */
class MainActivity : Activity(), HudController.Hooks {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "M14Hud"
        private const val REQ_PERMISSIONS = 1001
        private const val REQ_LUT_IMPORT = 1002
        private const val TICK_MS = 100L
        private const val BACK_EXIT_WINDOW_MS = 2000L
    }

    private lateinit var settings: SettingsStore
    private var listener: EngineListener? = null
    private var hud: HudController? = null
    private var glView: GlViewfinder? = null
    private var engine: CameraEngine? = null
    private var recController: RecordingController? = null
    private var gyroEis: GyroEisController? = null
    private var systemMonitor: SystemMonitor? = null
    private var storage: StorageHelper? = null

    @Volatile private var pendingPreviewTexture: SurfaceTexture? = null
    @Volatile private var sessionReady = false
    private var engineInitialized = false
    private var lastBackPressAt = 0L

    private val tickHandler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())
    private var tickRunnable: Runnable? = null

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Lifecycle
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        requestedOrientation = ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_SENSOR_LANDSCAPE
        window.addFlags(WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_KEEP_SCREEN_ON)
        setContentView(R.layout.activity_main)
        applyImmersive()

        val needed = arrayOf(Manifest.permission.CAMERA, Manifest.permission.RECORD_AUDIO)
        val missing = needed.filter {
            checkSelfPermission(it) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
        }
        if (missing.isEmpty()) {
            initEngine()
        } else {
            requestPermissions(missing.toTypedArray(), REQ_PERMISSIONS)
        }
    }

    override fun onResume() {
        super.onResume()
        applyImmersive()
        glView?.onResume()
    }

    override fun onPause() {
        // Graceful stop: never leave the encoder running while the activity is backgrounded.
        if (recController?.isRecording() == true) {
            runCatching { recController?.stopRecording() }
                .onFailure { Log.w(TAG, "stopRecording on pause failed", it) }
            hud?.setRecording(false, false)
        }
        glView?.onPause()
        super.onPause()
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        tickRunnable?.let { tickHandler.removeCallbacks(it) }
        tickRunnable = null
        systemMonitor?.stop()
        systemMonitor = null
        runCatching { engine?.close() }
        engine = null
        runCatching { recController?.releaseEncoder() }
        runCatching { recController?.audioEngine?.stopMeteringOnly() }
        recController = null
        gyroEis?.setEnabled(false)
        gyroEis = null
        hud?.destroy()
        hud = null
        sessionReady = false
        super.onDestroy()
    }

    @Deprecated("Deprecated in Java")
    override fun onBackPressed() {
        if (recController?.isRecording() == true) {
            toast("Stop recording first")
            return
        }
        val now = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
        if (now - lastBackPressAt < BACK_EXIT_WINDOW_MS) {
            finish()
        } else {
            lastBackPressAt = now
            toast("Press again to exit")
        }
    }

    override fun onWindowFocusChanged(hasFocus: Boolean) {
        super.onWindowFocusChanged(hasFocus)
        if (hasFocus) applyImmersive()
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Immersive sticky landscape
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    private fun applyImmersive() {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 30) {
            val ic = try { window.insetsController } catch (_: Throwable) { null }
            ic?.let {
                it.hide(WindowInsets.Type.systemBars())
                it.systemBarsBehavior = WindowInsetsController.BEHAVIOR_SHOW_TRANSIENT_BARS_BY_SWIPE
            }
        } else {
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            window.decorView.systemUiVisibility =
                View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_IMMERSIVE_STICKY or
                View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_FULLSCREEN or
                View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_HIDE_NAVIGATION or
                View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_LAYOUT_STABLE or
                View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_LAYOUT_FULLSCREEN or
                View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_LAYOUT_HIDE_NAVIGATION
        }
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Permissions
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    override fun onRequestPermissionsResult(
        requestCode: Int,
        permissions: Array<out String>,
        grantResults: IntArray
    ) {
        super.onRequestPermissionsResult(requestCode, permissions, grantResults)
        if (requestCode != REQ_PERMISSIONS) return
        if (grantResults.isNotEmpty() &&
            grantResults.all { it == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED }
        ) {
            initEngine()
        } else {
            toast("Camera and microphone permissions are required")
            Log.e(TAG, "Permissions denied — finishing")
            finish()
        }
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Engine bring-up
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    private fun initEngine() {
        if (engineInitialized) return
        engineInitialized = true

        settings = SettingsStore(this)
        listener = buildEngineListener()
        val engineListener = listener!!

        storage = StorageHelper(this)
        systemMonitor = SystemMonitor(this, engineListener).also { it.start() }
        recController = RecordingController(this, settings, engineListener)
        gyroEis = GyroEisController(this)

        hud = HudController(this, settings, this).also { h ->
            h.lutImportRequester = { requestLutImport() }
            h.lutApplier = { preset, path -> glView?.applyLut(preset, path) }
            h.onSettingsDirty = { engine?.applySettings() }
        }

        val root = findViewById<FrameLayout>(R.id.hud_root)
        glView = GlViewfinder(this, settings, engineListener) { st ->
            pendingPreviewTexture = st
            maybeStartCamera()
        }.also { gv ->
            root.addView(gv, 0)
        }
        glView?.renderer?.eisMatrixProvider = {
            if (settings.eisOn) gyroEis?.matrix() else null
        }
        glView?.renderer?.wbGainsProvider = {
            engine?.whiteBalanceController?.normalizedShaderGains() ?: floatArrayOf(1f, 1f, 1f)
        }

        // Metering-only audio after permissions (spec: monitoring starts without recording).
        runCatching { recController?.audioEngine?.startMeteringOnly() }
            .onFailure { Log.w(TAG, "audio metering-only start failed", it) }

        hud?.refreshAll()
        startTick()
        Log.i(TAG, "Engine initialized; waiting for preview texture + session")
    }

    private fun maybeStartCamera() {
        if (engine != null) return
        if (pendingPreviewTexture == null) return
        val permissionsOk = arrayOf(
            Manifest.permission.CAMERA, Manifest.permission.RECORD_AUDIO
        ).all { checkSelfPermission(it) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED }
        if (!permissionsOk) {
            Log.i(TAG, "Preview texture ready but permissions missing; waiting")
            return
        }

        engine = CameraEngine(this, settings, listener!!).also { e ->
            e.open {
                rebuildSession()
            }
        }
    }

    private fun rebuildSession() {
        val e = engine ?: return
        val tex = pendingPreviewTexture ?: run {
            Log.w(TAG, "rebuildSession: no preview texture yet")
            return
        }
        if (recController?.isRecording() == true) {
            runCatching { recController?.stopRecording() }
                .onFailure { Log.w(TAG, "stopRecording before rebuild failed", it) }
            hud?.setRecording(false, false)
        }
        sessionReady = false
        val p = settings.resolution
        // ALWAYS prepare the record surface up front (record-ready pipeline, Architecture A).
        val recordSurface = recController?.prepareEncoder(
            p.width, p.height, p.fps, settings.bitrate.bps, p.highSpeed
        ) ?: run {
            hud?.setStatus("ENCODER PREP FAILED")
            return
        }
        e.createSession(recordSurface, tex) { ok ->
            sessionReady = ok
            if (ok) {
                e.applySettings()
                hud?.setStatus("Ready")
            } else {
                hud?.setStatus("SESSION FAILED")
                Log.e(TAG, "Camera session configuration failed")
            }
        }
    }

    private fun startTick() {
        val r = object : Runnable {
            override fun run() {
                hud?.updateTimecode(recController?.frameIndex() ?: 0L, settings.fps)
                tickHandler.postDelayed(this, TICK_MS)
            }
        }
        tickRunnable = r
        tickHandler.post(r)
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // EngineListener forwards
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    private fun buildEngineListener(): EngineListener = object : EngineListener {
        override fun onRecordingStarted(clipBaseName: String) {
            hud?.setRecording(true, true)
            hud?.setStatus("REC $clipBaseName")
        }

        override fun onRecordingStopped(clipBaseName: String, durationMs: Long, frameCount: Long) {
            hud?.setRecording(false, false)
            hud?.setStatus("Saved $clipBaseName")
            hud?.refreshAll() // take auto-bump happened in RecordingController
            toast(
                String.format(
                    Locale.US, "SAVED %s  %.1fs  %d fr",
                    clipBaseName, durationMs / 1000f, frameCount
                )
            )
        }

        override fun onError(stage: String, throwable: Throwable) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Engine error @ $stage", throwable)
            if (recController?.isRecording() == true) {
                runCatching { recController?.stopRecording() }
                hud?.setRecording(false, false)
            }
            hud?.setStatus("ERR: ${stage.uppercase(Locale.US)}")
            toast(getString(R.string.err_camera) + " ($stage)")
        }

        override fun onInfo(message: String) {
            hud?.setStatus(message)
        }

        override fun onFrameCount(frameIndex: Long, fps: Int) {
            hud?.updateTimecode(frameIndex, fps)
        }

        override fun onAudioLevels(peakLeftDb: Float, rmsLeftDb: Float, peakRightDb: Float, rmsRightDb: Float) {
            hud?.onLevels(peakLeftDb, rmsLeftDb, peakRightDb, rmsRightDb)
        }

        override fun onHistogram(bins: java.nio.ByteBuffer) {
            hud?.onHistogram(bins)
        }

        override fun onCameraTelemetry(aeState: Int, afState: Int, sceneFlicker: Int) {
            settings.sceneFlicker = sceneFlicker
            hud?.onTelemetry(aeState, afState, sceneFlicker)
        }

        override fun onSystemStatus(batteryPct: Int, tempC: Float, thermalHeadroom: Float, freeGb: Float) {
            hud?.onSystemStatus(batteryPct, tempC, thermalHeadroom, freeGb)
        }
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // HudController.Hooks — transport decisions
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    override fun onRecordToggle() {
        val rec = recController ?: return
        if (rec.isRecording()) {
            runCatching { rec.stopRecording() }
                .onFailure { Log.e(TAG, "stopRecording failed", it) }
            hud?.setRecording(false, false)
        } else {
            if (!sessionReady || engine == null) {
                hud?.setStatus("NO SESSION")
                toast("Camera session not ready")
                return
            }
            val base = runCatching { rec.slateManager.clipBaseName() }
                .getOrDefault("M14_A001_C001")
            runCatching { rec.startRecording(base) }
                .onFailure {
                    Log.e(TAG, "startRecording failed", it)
                    hud?.setStatus("REC FAILED")
                    toast(getString(R.string.err_camera) + " (record)")
                    return
                }
            hud?.setRecording(true, true)
        }
    }

    override fun onSlateOpen() {
        SlateDialog.show(this, settings) { hud?.refreshAll() }
    }

    override fun onEisToggled(on: Boolean) {
        gyroEis?.setEnabled(on)
        hud?.setStatus(if (on) "EIS ON" else "EIS OFF")
    }

    override fun onTorchToggled(on: Boolean) {
        val e = engine
        if (e == null) {
            hud?.setStatus("TORCH (NO CAM)")
            return
        }
        runCatching { e.setTorch(on) }
            .onFailure { Log.w(TAG, "setTorch failed", it) }
    }

    override fun onFormatChanged(preset: Models.ResPreset) {
        Log.i(TAG, "Format -> $preset; rebuilding session")
        rebuildSession()
    }

    override fun onBitrateChanged(p: Models.BitratePreset) {
        Log.i(TAG, "Bitrate -> $p; rebuilding session (encoder reconfig)")
        rebuildSession()
    }

    override fun onFocusTap(nx: Float, ny: Float) {
        val e = engine ?: return
        runCatching { e.focusAt(nx, ny) }
            .onFailure { Log.w(TAG, "focusAt failed", it) }
    }

    override fun onMarkerTap(index: Int) {
        val marker = settings.focusMarkers.getOrNull(index)
        if (marker == null) {
            toast("F${index + 1} not set — long-press to set")
            return
        }
        engine?.focusAt(marker[0], marker[1])
        hud?.showTapBoxAt(marker[0], marker[1])
    }

    override fun onMarkerLongPress(index: Int) {
        val pt = hud?.lastTapPoint()
        if (pt == null) {
            toast("Tap the viewfinder first")
            return
        }
        settings.saveMarker(index, pt[0], pt[1])
        hud?.refreshMarkers()
        toast("F${index + 1} SET")
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // LUT import (framework SAF — zero androidx)
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    private fun requestLutImport() {
        val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_OPEN_DOCUMENT).apply {
            addCategory(Intent.CATEGORY_OPENABLE)
            type = "*/*"
            putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_MIME_TYPES, arrayOf("text/plain", "application/octet-stream"))
        }
        runCatching { startActivityForResult(intent, REQ_LUT_IMPORT) }
            .onFailure {
                Log.w(TAG, "No document picker available", it)
                toast(getString(R.string.err_lut_import))
            }
    }

    @Deprecated("Deprecated in Java")
    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
    override fun onActivityResult(requestCode: Int, resultCode: Int, data: Intent?) {
        super.onActivityResult(requestCode, resultCode, data)
        if (requestCode != REQ_LUT_IMPORT) return
        val uri = data?.data
        if (resultCode != RESULT_OK || uri == null) {
            toast(getString(R.string.err_lut_import))
            return
        }
        runCatching {
            try {
                contentResolver.takePersistableUriPermission(
                    uri, Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION
                )
            } catch (_: SecurityException) {
                // Persistable grant not offered by the provider; the immediate read still works.
            }
            storage?.importCubeLut(uri)
        }.onSuccess { path ->
            if (path != null) {
                glView?.applyLut(Models.LutPreset.IMPORTED, path)
                hud?.refreshAfterLut(path)
                toast("LUT imported")
                Log.i(TAG, "LUT imported: $path")
            } else {
                toast(getString(R.string.err_lut_import))
            }
        }.onFailure {
            Log.e(TAG, "LUT import failed", it)
            toast(getString(R.string.err_lut_import))
        }
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Helpers
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    private fun toast(msg: String) {
        runOnUiThread {
            Toast.makeText(this, msg, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }
    }
}

==> m14_cinepro/app/src/main/java/com/m14/cinepro/recording/AudioEngine.kt <==
package com.m14.cinepro.recording

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.media.AudioFormat
import android.media.AudioRecord
import android.media.AudioTimestamp
import android.media.MediaCodec
import android.media.MediaCodecList
import android.media.MediaFormat
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.HandlerThread
import android.os.Looper
import android.os.Process
import android.util.Log
import com.m14.cinepro.core.EngineListener
import com.m14.cinepro.core.Models
import com.m14.cinepro.core.SettingsStore
import java.nio.ByteBuffer
import java.nio.ByteOrder
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentLinkedQueue
import java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean

/**
 * M14 CinePro audio subsystem (stage5_media.md §5 + PART 3 spec).
 *
 * Capture : AudioRecord 48 kHz stereo 16-bit, source fallback UNPROCESSED(9) -> CAMCORDER(5) -> MIC(1),
 *           buffer = max(minBufferSize, 8192) bytes, 512-frame blocking reads (~93 Hz) on the
 *           "AudioCapture" thread at THREAD_PRIORITY_URGENT_AUDIO.
 * Encode  : MediaCodec "audio/mp4a-latm", AAC-LC (aac-profile=2), 256 kbps, pcm-encoding=16-bit,
 *           priority 0, async mode on the "AudioEncode" HandlerThread; input PCM is queued with
 *           AudioTimestamp(TIMEBASE_MONOTONIC).nanoTime/1000 PTS (System.nanoTime()/1000 fallback)
 *           and MediaCodec carries the input PTS through to the output buffers.
 * Muxing  : encoded samples are handed to the [AudioSink] (RecordingController) — the controller
 *           owns all MediaMuxer writes on its single "EncoderDrain" thread.
 * Meters  : per buffer per channel peak = max|sample|; rolling RMS over the last 50 ms (2400 frames);
 *           dBFS = 20*log10(linear) - Models.DBFS_REF_16BIT (90.308998), floored at -60;
 *           peak hold decays 0.5 dB after 48 idle buffers (~0.5 s); levels emitted at most every 50 ms.
 *
 * Live preview metering without recording: [startMeteringOnly] / [stopMeteringOnly] run the same
 * capture thread in meter-only mode; both paths are mutually exclusive with [start].
 */
class AudioEngine(
    private val settings: SettingsStore,
    private val listener: EngineListener
) {

    /** Consumer of encoded AAC samples. The ByteBuffer is a fresh direct buffer owned by the sink. */
    interface AudioSink {
        fun onAudioSample(buffer: ByteBuffer, info: MediaCodec.BufferInfo)
    }

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "M14Audio"
        private const val MIME_AAC = "audio/mp4a-latm"
        private const val AAC_OBJECT_LC = 2
        private const val READ_FRAMES = 512
        private const val MIN_BUFFER_BYTES = 8192
        private const val RMS_WINDOW_FRAMES = 2400 // 50 ms @ 48 kHz
        private const val HOLD_RELEASE_FRAMES = 48 // ~0.5 s of buffers
        private const val HOLD_DECAY_DB = 0.5f
        private const val METER_FLOOR_DB = -60f
        private const val EMIT_INTERVAL_NS = 50_000_000L
        private const val MAX_PCM_QUEUE = 128
        private const val MAX_BAD_READS = 500
        private const val START_POLL_MS = 50L
        private const val START_TIMEOUT_MS = 1500L
        private const val RETRY_FEED_MS = 10L
    }

    private enum class Mode { IDLE, METER_ONLY, RECORDING }

    private val mainHandler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())

    @Volatile private var sink: AudioSink? = null

    // ---- capture state ----
    @Volatile private var recorder: AudioRecord? = null
    private var recorderSource = 0
    @Volatile private var captureThread: Thread? = null
    private val running = AtomicBoolean(false)

    // ---- encoder state ----
    private var codec: MediaCodec? = null
    private var encodeThread: HandlerThread? = null
    private var encodeHandler: Handler? = null

    // ---- queueing ----
    private class Chunk(val pcm: ShortArray, val ptsUs: Long)
    private val pcmQueue = ConcurrentLinkedQueue<Chunk>()
    private val freeInputs = ConcurrentLinkedQueue<Int>()

    // ---- mode ----
    @Volatile private var mode: Mode = Mode.IDLE
    private var formatReady: ((MediaFormat) -> Unit)? = null

    // ---- meter state (owned by AudioCapture thread; snapshots @Volatile for emitters) ----
    private val windowL = FloatArray(RMS_WINDOW_FRAMES)
    private val windowR = FloatArray(RMS_WINDOW_FRAMES)
    private var winIdx = 0
    private var winCount = 0
    private var sumSqL = 0.0
    private var sumSqR = 0.0
    private val heldPeak = floatArrayOf(METER_FLOOR_DB, METER_FLOOR_DB)
    private val holdFrames = intArrayOf(0, 0)
    @Volatile private var outPeakL = METER_FLOOR_DB
    @Volatile private var outRmsL = METER_FLOOR_DB
    @Volatile private var outPeakR = METER_FLOOR_DB
    @Volatile private var outRmsR = METER_FLOOR_DB
    @Volatile private var lastEmitNanos = 0L
    @Volatile private var metersLive = false

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Public API
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    /** Wires the encoded-sample consumer. Call before [start]. */
    fun setSink(sink: AudioSink?) {
        this.sink = sink
    }

    /**
     * Starts (or re-targets) the capture+encode pipeline for a recording take.
     * [onFormatReady] receives the encoder output MediaFormat (with csd-0) exactly once,
     * from the AudioEncode thread — RecordingController adds the muxer audio track from it.
     */
    @Synchronized
    fun start(onFormatReady: (MediaFormat) -> Unit) {
        formatReady = onFormatReady
        if (mode == Mode.RECORDING) {
            Log.w(TAG, "start ignored — already recording")
            return
        }
        if (!ensureCaptureRunning()) return
        if (encodeThread == null) {
            val t = HandlerThread("AudioEncode")
            t.start()
            encodeThread = t
            encodeHandler = Handler(t.looper)
        }
        pcmQueue.clear()
        freeInputs.clear()
        if (!openEncoder()) {
            // No AAC encoder: capture keeps running for meters; RecordingController muxes video-only.
            Log.e(TAG, "AAC encoder unavailable — take will be video-only")
            return
        }
        mode = Mode.RECORDING
