        Log.i(TAG, "audio recording started (48 kHz stereo, AAC-LC ${Models.AUDIO_BITRATE / 1000} kbps)")
    }

    /** Stops capture + encoder. Idempotent; safe from any thread. */
    @Synchronized
    fun stop() {
        if (mode == Mode.IDLE && !running.get()) return
        mode = Mode.IDLE
        running.set(false)
        captureThread?.let { t ->
            try { t.join(1500) } catch (e: InterruptedException) { Thread.currentThread().interrupt() }
        }
        captureThread = null
        releaseRecorder()
        teardownEncoder()
        pcmQueue.clear()
        freeInputs.clear()
        resetMeterState()
        metersLive = false
        formatReady = null
        Log.i(TAG, "audio engine stopped")
    }

    /** Live viewfinder meters without recording (no encoder). Mutually exclusive with [start]. */
    @Synchronized
    fun startMeteringOnly() {
        if (mode == Mode.RECORDING) {
            Log.w(TAG, "startMeteringOnly ignored — recording active")
            return
        }
        if (mode == Mode.METER_ONLY) return
        if (!ensureCaptureRunning()) return
        mode = Mode.METER_ONLY
        Log.i(TAG, "live metering started (mic source $recorderSource)")
    }

    /** Stops meter-only capture. No-op while a recording owns the pipeline. */
    @Synchronized
    fun stopMeteringOnly() {
        if (mode == Mode.RECORDING) return
        if (mode == Mode.IDLE && !running.get()) return
        mode = Mode.IDLE
        running.set(false)
        captureThread?.let { t ->
            try { t.join(1500) } catch (e: InterruptedException) { Thread.currentThread().interrupt() }
        }
        captureThread = null
        releaseRecorder()
        resetMeterState()
        metersLive = false
        Log.i(TAG, "live metering stopped")
    }

    /** True once the capture thread has read at least one valid buffer. */
    fun metersActive(): Boolean = metersLive

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Capture
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    private fun ensureCaptureRunning(): Boolean {
        if (running.get()) return true
        if (!openRecorder()) {
            notifyError(RuntimeException("microphone unavailable (UNPROCESSED/CAMCORDER/MIC all failed)"))
            return false
        }
        running.set(true)
        val t = Thread({ captureLoop() }, "AudioCapture")
        captureThread = t
        t.start()
        return true
    }

    /** AudioRecord with stage5 §5.1 source fallback chain: 9 -> 5 -> 1. */
    @SuppressLint("MissingPermission")
    private fun openRecorder(): Boolean {
        val minBuf = try {
            AudioRecord.getMinBufferSize(
                Models.AUDIO_SAMPLE_RATE,
                AudioFormat.CHANNEL_IN_STEREO,
                AudioFormat.ENCODING_PCM_16BIT
            )
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "getMinBufferSize failed", e)
            0
        }
        if (minBuf <= 0) {
            Log.e(TAG, "invalid min buffer size: $minBuf")
            return false
        }
        val bufferSize = maxOf(minBuf, MIN_BUFFER_BYTES)
        val sources = intArrayOf(
            9, // MediaRecorder.AudioSource.UNPROCESSED
            5, // MediaRecorder.AudioSource.CAMCORDER
            1  // MediaRecorder.AudioSource.MIC
        )
        for (src in sources) {
            try {
                val rec = AudioRecord(
                    src,
                    Models.AUDIO_SAMPLE_RATE,
                    AudioFormat.CHANNEL_IN_STEREO,
                    AudioFormat.ENCODING_PCM_16BIT,
                    bufferSize
                )
                if (rec.state != AudioRecord.STATE_INITIALIZED) {
                    Log.w(TAG, "source $src not initialized (state=${rec.state})")
                    try { rec.release() } catch (e: Exception) { Log.w(TAG, "release failed", e) }
                    continue
                }
                rec.startRecording()
                var waited = 0
                while (rec.recordingState != AudioRecord.RECORDSTATE_RECORDING && waited < START_TIMEOUT_MS) {
                    try { Thread.sleep(START_POLL_MS) } catch (e: InterruptedException) { Thread.currentThread().interrupt() }
                    waited += START_POLL_MS.toInt()
                }
                if (rec.recordingState != AudioRecord.RECORDSTATE_RECORDING) {
                    Log.w(TAG, "source $src failed to reach RECORDING state")
                    try { rec.release() } catch (e: Exception) { Log.w(TAG, "release failed", e) }
                    continue
                }
                recorder = rec
                recorderSource = src
                Log.i(TAG, "AudioRecord ready: source=$src rate=${Models.AUDIO_SAMPLE_RATE} stereo 16-bit buffer=$bufferSize")
                return true
            } catch (e: SecurityException) {
                Log.w(TAG, "source $src rejected (RECORD_AUDIO permission?)", e)
            } catch (e: IllegalArgumentException) {
                Log.w(TAG, "source $src invalid parameters", e)
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.w(TAG, "source $src failed", e)
            }
        }
        return false
    }

    private fun releaseRecorder() {
        recorder?.let { rec ->
            try { rec.stop() } catch (e: Exception) { Log.w(TAG, "recorder stop", e) }
            try { rec.release() } catch (e: Exception) { Log.w(TAG, "recorder release", e) }
        }
        recorder = null
    }

    private fun captureLoop() {
        Process.setThreadPriority(Process.THREAD_PRIORITY_URGENT_AUDIO)
        val buf = ShortArray(READ_FRAMES * 2)
        var badReads = 0
        while (running.get()) {
            val rec = recorder ?: break
            val n = try {
                rec.read(buf, 0, buf.size, AudioRecord.READ_BLOCKING)
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.w(TAG, "AudioRecord.read threw", e)
                -1
            }
            if (n <= 0) {
                badReads++
                if (badReads % 100 == 1) Log.w(TAG, "audio read returned $n (consecutive=$badReads)")
                if (badReads >= MAX_BAD_READS) {
                    notifyError(RuntimeException("AudioRecord read failing persistently (last n=$n)"))
                    break
                }
                try { Thread.sleep(10) } catch (e: InterruptedException) { break }
                continue
            }
            badReads = 0
            metersLive = true
            val frames = n / 2
            if (frames > 0) {
                computeMeters(buf, frames)
                maybeEmitLevels()
                if (mode == Mode.RECORDING) {
                    val pts = currentPtsUs()
                    pcmQueue.offer(Chunk(buf.copyOf(frames * 2), pts))
                    if (pcmQueue.size > MAX_PCM_QUEUE) pcmQueue.poll()
                    encodeHandler?.post { drainInputs() }
                }
            }
        }
        Log.i(TAG, "AudioCapture thread exiting")
    }

    /** PTS in µs from the monotonic clock (stage5 §5.3: AudioTimestamp.nanoTime/1000, fallback nanoTime/1000). */
    private fun currentPtsUs(): Long {
        val rec = recorder ?: return System.nanoTime() / 1000
        val ts = AudioTimestamp()
        return try {
            if (rec.getTimestamp(ts, AudioTimestamp.TIMEBASE_MONOTONIC) == AudioRecord.SUCCESS) {
                ts.nanoTime / 1000
            } else {
                System.nanoTime() / 1000
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            System.nanoTime() / 1000
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Encoder
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    private fun buildAacFormat(): MediaFormat {
        val fmt = MediaFormat()
        fmt.setString(MediaFormat.KEY_MIME, MIME_AAC)
        fmt.setInteger(MediaFormat.KEY_SAMPLE_RATE, Models.AUDIO_SAMPLE_RATE)
        fmt.setInteger(MediaFormat.KEY_CHANNEL_COUNT, 2)
        fmt.setInteger(MediaFormat.KEY_BIT_RATE, Models.AUDIO_BITRATE)
        fmt.setInteger(MediaFormat.KEY_AAC_PROFILE, AAC_OBJECT_LC)
        fmt.setInteger(MediaFormat.KEY_PCM_ENCODING, AudioFormat.ENCODING_PCM_16BIT)
        fmt.setInteger(MediaFormat.KEY_PRIORITY, 0)
        fmt.setInteger(MediaFormat.KEY_MAX_INPUT_SIZE, MIN_BUFFER_BYTES)
        return fmt
    }

    private fun openEncoder(): Boolean {
        val probe = buildAacFormat()
        val found = try {
            MediaCodecList(MediaCodecList.REGULAR_CODECS).findEncoderForFormat(probe)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.w(TAG, "encoder query failed", e)
            null
        }
        val candidates = listOfNotNull(found, "c2.android.aac.encoder")
            .filter { it.isNotBlank() }
            .distinct()
        for (name in candidates) {
            val fmt = buildAacFormat()
            try {
                val c = MediaCodec.createByCodecName(name)
                if (!startEncoder(c, fmt)) {
                    try { c.release() } catch (e: Exception) { Log.w(TAG, "release failed", e) }
                    Log.w(TAG, "encoder $name unusable")
                    continue
                }
                codec = c
                Log.i(TAG, "AAC encoder: $name")
                return true
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.w(TAG, "create $name failed: ${e.message}")
            }
        }
        return false
    }

    /**
     * configure + setCallback + start. Both legal orderings are attempted (framework versions
     * differ on whether setCallback is accepted before configure); MediaCodec.reset() rewinds
     * between attempts.
     */
    private fun startEncoder(c: MediaCodec, fmt: MediaFormat): Boolean {
        val handler = encodeHandler ?: return false
        try {
            c.configure(fmt, null, null, MediaCodec.CONFIGURE_FLAG_ENCODE)
            c.setCallback(codecCallback, handler)
            c.start()
            return true
        } catch (first: Throwable) {
            Log.w(TAG, "configure-first path failed (${first.message}); retrying callback-first")
        }
        try {
            c.reset()
        } catch (e: Throwable) {
            Log.w(TAG, "codec reset failed", e)
            return false
        }
        try {
            c.setCallback(codecCallback, handler)
            c.configure(fmt, null, null, MediaCodec.CONFIGURE_FLAG_ENCODE)
            c.start()
            return true
        } catch (second: Throwable) {
            Log.w(TAG, "encoder start failed on both paths", second)
            return false
        }
    }

    private fun teardownEncoder() {
        val et = encodeThread
        val eh = encodeHandler
        if (et != null && eh != null) {
            val released = CountDownLatch(1)
            eh.post {
                try {
                    codec?.let { c ->
                        try { c.stop() } catch (e: Exception) { Log.w(TAG, "codec stop", e) }
                        try { c.release() } catch (e: Exception) { Log.w(TAG, "codec release", e) }
                    }
                } finally {
                    codec = null
                    released.countDown()
                    et.quitSafely()
                }
            }
            try {
                released.await(2, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
            } catch (e: InterruptedException) {
                Thread.currentThread().interrupt()
            }
        }
        encodeThread = null
        encodeHandler = null
    }

    /** Feeds queued PCM chunks into available async input buffers. Runs on the AudioEncode thread only. */
    private fun drainInputs() {
        val mc = codec ?: return
        while (true) {
            val index = freeInputs.poll() ?: break
            val chunk = pcmQueue.poll()
            if (chunk == null) {
                freeInputs.add(index)
                break
            }
            try {
                val bb = mc.getInputBuffer(index)
                if (bb != null) {
                    bb.clear()
                    bb.order(ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN).asShortBuffer().put(chunk.pcm, 0, chunk.pcm.size)
                    mc.queueInputBuffer(index, 0, chunk.pcm.size * 2, chunk.ptsUs, 0)
                } else {
                    Log.w(TAG, "getInputBuffer($index) returned null")
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.w(TAG, "queueInputBuffer failed", e)
            }
        }
        if (mode == Mode.RECORDING && freeInputs.isNotEmpty() && pcmQueue.isEmpty()) {
            // Input buffers waiting on the next capture chunk — retry shortly (safety net;
            // the capture thread also posts drainInputs after every offer).
            encodeHandler?.postDelayed({ if (mode == Mode.RECORDING) drainInputs() }, RETRY_FEED_MS)
        }
    }

    private val codecCallback = object : MediaCodec.Callback() {
        override fun onInputBufferAvailable(mc: MediaCodec, index: Int) {
            freeInputs.add(index)
            drainInputs()
        }

        override fun onOutputBufferAvailable(mc: MediaCodec, index: Int, info: MediaCodec.BufferInfo) {
            try {
                if (info.size > 0 && (info.flags and MediaCodec.BUFFER_FLAG_CODEC_CONFIG) == 0) {
                    val consumer = sink
                    if (consumer != null) {
                        val out = mc.getOutputBuffer(index)
                        if (out != null) {
                            out.position(info.offset)
                            out.limit(info.offset + info.size)
                            // Fresh direct copy: the sink writes it asynchronously on the muxer
                            // thread, while this codec buffer is released immediately.
                            val copy = ByteBuffer.allocateDirect(info.size)
                            copy.put(out)
                            copy.flip()
                            val ci = MediaCodec.BufferInfo()
                            ci.set(0, info.size, info.presentationTimeUs, info.flags)
                            consumer.onAudioSample(copy, ci)
                        }
                    }
                }
            } catch (e: Throwable) {
                Log.w(TAG, "audio output handling failed", e)
            } finally {
                try { mc.releaseOutputBuffer(index, false) } catch (e: Exception) { Log.w(TAG, "release out buf", e) }
            }
        }

        override fun onOutputFormatChanged(mc: MediaCodec, format: MediaFormat) {
            val cb = formatReady
            if (cb != null) {
                try {
                    cb(format)
                } catch (e: Throwable) {
                    Log.w(TAG, "format callback failed", e)
                }
            }
        }

        override fun onError(mc: MediaCodec, e: MediaCodec.CodecException) {
            Log.e(TAG, "AAC codec error: ${e.diagnosticInfo}", e)
            notifyError(e)
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Metering (stage5 §5.6 law, 16-bit full-scale reference)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    /** Clears meter accumulators (circular RMS windows, peak-hold) between sessions. */
    private fun resetMeterState() {
        windowL.fill(0f)
        windowR.fill(0f)
        winIdx = 0
        winCount = 0
        sumSqL = 0.0
        sumSqR = 0.0
        heldPeak[0] = METER_FLOOR_DB
        heldPeak[1] = METER_FLOOR_DB
        holdFrames[0] = 0
        holdFrames[1] = 0
        outPeakL = METER_FLOOR_DB
        outRmsL = METER_FLOOR_DB
        outPeakR = METER_FLOOR_DB
        outRmsR = METER_FLOOR_DB
    }

    /** dBFS relative to 16-bit full scale: 20*log10(x) - 90.308998, clamped to [-60, 0]. */
    private fun dbfs(linear: Float): Float {
        if (linear <= 0f) return METER_FLOOR_DB
        val db = (Math.log10(linear.toDouble()) * 20.0).toFloat() - Models.DBFS_REF_16BIT
        return db.coerceIn(METER_FLOOR_DB, 0f)
    }

    private fun computeMeters(buf: ShortArray, frames: Int) {
        var peakL = 0f
        var peakR = 0f
        for (i in 0 until frames) {
            val l = Math.abs(buf[i * 2].toFloat())
            val r = Math.abs(buf[i * 2 + 1].toFloat())
            if (l > peakL) peakL = l
            if (r > peakR) peakR = r
            val oldSqL = windowL[winIdx]
            val oldSqR = windowR[winIdx]
            sumSqL += (l * l - oldSqL).toDouble()
            sumSqR += (r * r - oldSqR).toDouble()
            windowL[winIdx] = l * l
            windowR[winIdx] = r * r
            winIdx++
            if (winIdx >= RMS_WINDOW_FRAMES) winIdx = 0
            if (winCount < RMS_WINDOW_FRAMES) winCount++
        }
        val count = if (winCount > 0) winCount else 1
        val rmsLinL = Math.sqrt(sumSqL / count).toFloat()
        val rmsLinR = Math.sqrt(sumSqR / count).toFloat()
        val peakDbL = dbfs(peakL)
        val peakDbR = dbfs(peakR)
        updateHold(0, peakDbL)
        updateHold(1, peakDbR)
        outPeakL = maxOf(peakDbL, heldPeak[0])
        outPeakR = maxOf(peakDbR, heldPeak[1])
        outRmsL = dbfs(rmsLinL)
        outRmsR = dbfs(rmsLinR)
    }

    private fun updateHold(channel: Int, peakDb: Float) {
        if (peakDb > heldPeak[channel]) {
            heldPeak[channel] = peakDb
            holdFrames[channel] = 0
        } else {
            holdFrames[channel]++
            if (holdFrames[channel] > HOLD_RELEASE_FRAMES) {
                heldPeak[channel] -= HOLD_DECAY_DB
                if (heldPeak[channel] < METER_FLOOR_DB) heldPeak[channel] = METER_FLOOR_DB
            }
        }
    }

    private fun maybeEmitLevels() {
        val now = System.nanoTime()
        if (now - lastEmitNanos < EMIT_INTERVAL_NS) return
        lastEmitNanos = now
        val pl = outPeakL
        val rl = outRmsL
        val pr = outPeakR
        val rr = outRmsR
        mainHandler.post { listener.onAudioLevels(pl, rl, pr, rr) }
    }

    private fun notifyError(t: Throwable) {
        Log.e(TAG, "audio error", t)
        mainHandler.post { listener.onError("audio", t) }
    }
}

==> m14_cinepro/app/src/main/java/com/m14/cinepro/recording/GyroflowLogger.kt <==
package com.m14.cinepro.recording

import android.content.Context
import android.hardware.Sensor
import android.hardware.SensorEvent
import android.hardware.SensorEventListener
import android.hardware.SensorManager
import android.net.Uri
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.HandlerThread
import android.os.Process
import android.util.Log
import java.io.BufferedWriter
import java.io.File
import java.io.IOException
import java.io.OutputStreamWriter
import java.util.Locale
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentLinkedQueue

/**
 * Gyroflow v1.3 telemetry logger for the Galaxy M14 5G LSM6DSL IMU (M14_VERIFIED_TRUTH §2:
 * gyroscope + accelerometer, both continuous up to 500.00 Hz, FIFO 1000 events).
 *
 * Registers TYPE_GYROSCOPE + TYPE_ACCELEROMETER with SENSOR_DELAY_FASTEST on the "GyroLogger"
 * HandlerThread. Thread priority is THREAD_PRIORITY_MORE_FAVORABLE (-1): the LSM6DSL fires at
 * 500 Hz per axis and the default sensor delivery latency (THREAD_PRIORITY_BACKGROUND = +10)
 * measurably bunches event timestamps, which would quantize the .gcsv timeline; -1 keeps the
 * dispatcher above the default UI priority without starving the codec/HUD threads (which run at
 * -4 and below). Rows are produced at ~500 Hz and consumed by the dedicated "GyroWriter" thread
 * through a ConcurrentLinkedQueue; every row is written to BOTH the Movies/M14_CinePro and the
 * DCIM/M14_CinePro copies.
 *
 * Each GYRO event is paired with the LATEST accelerometer sample (both streams are ~500 Hz).
 */
class GyroflowLogger(private val context: Context) {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "M14Gyro"
        private const val SENSOR_PRIORITY = Process.THREAD_PRIORITY_MORE_FAVORABLE // -1, see class doc
        private const val WRITER_IDLE_MS = 20L
        private const val WRITER_FLUSH_EVERY = 256
    }

    /** Optional failure hook — RecordingController forwards it to EngineListener.onError. */
    @Volatile var errorListener: ((stage: String, throwable: Throwable) -> Unit)? = null

    private val storage = StorageHelper(context)
    private val sensorManager = context.getSystemService(Context.SENSOR_SERVICE) as SensorManager
    private val gyroSensor: Sensor? = sensorManager.getDefaultSensor(Sensor.TYPE_GYROSCOPE)
    private val accelSensor: Sensor? = sensorManager.getDefaultSensor(Sensor.TYPE_ACCELEROMETER)

    private val queue = ConcurrentLinkedQueue<String>()
    private val latestAccel = FloatArray(3)

    private var sensorThread: HandlerThread? = null
    private var sensorHandler: Handler? = null
    private var writerThread: Thread? = null
    private var moviesWriter: BufferedWriter? = null
    private var dcimWriter: BufferedWriter? = null
    private var moviesHandle: OutputHandle? = null
    private var dcimHandle: OutputHandle? = null
    private var writerBroken = false

    @Volatile private var running = false

    private val sensorListener = object : SensorEventListener {
        override fun onSensorChanged(event: SensorEvent) {
            if (!running) return
            when (event.sensor.type) {
                Sensor.TYPE_ACCELEROMETER -> synchronized(latestAccel) {
                    latestAccel[0] = value(event, 0)
                    latestAccel[1] = value(event, 1)
                    latestAccel[2] = value(event, 2)
                }
                Sensor.TYPE_GYROSCOPE -> {
                    // kotlin.synchronized is inline, so the block result flows out directly.
                    val accel = synchronized(latestAccel) {
                        floatArrayOf(latestAccel[0], latestAccel[1], latestAccel[2])
                    }
                    // SensorEvent.timestamp is ns since boot -> µs row time (tscale:1 contract).
                    val tUs = event.timestamp / 1000
                    queue.offer(
                        String.format(
                            Locale.US, "%d,%.6f,%.6f,%.6f,%.6f,%.6f,%.6f",
                            tUs, value(event, 0), value(event, 1), value(event, 2),
                            accel[0], accel[1], accel[2]
                        )
                    )
                }
            }
        }

        override fun onAccuracyChanged(sensor: Sensor?, accuracy: Int) {
            // not tracked — the .gcsv format has no accuracy column
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Lifecycle
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    /**
     * Starts logging to <baseName>.gcsv in Movies/M14_CinePro + DCIM/M14_CinePro.
     * Writes the exact Gyroflow v1.3 header synchronously, then streams data rows.
     */
    @Synchronized
    fun start(baseName: String, note: String) {
        if (running) stop() // reentrant: clean restart
        if (gyroSensor == null) {
            val e = RuntimeException("no gyroscope on device — telemetry disabled")
            Log.e(TAG, "start failed", e)
            errorListener?.invoke("gyro", e)
            return
        }
        moviesHandle = storage.telemetryHandle(baseName)
        dcimHandle = storage.telemetryHandleDcim(baseName)
        if (moviesHandle == null && dcimHandle == null) {
            val e = RuntimeException("cannot create telemetry output for $baseName.gcsv")
            Log.e(TAG, "start failed", e)
            errorListener?.invoke("gyro", e)
            return
        }
        try {
            moviesWriter = moviesHandle?.let {
                BufferedWriter(OutputStreamWriter(it.stream, Charsets.UTF_8))
            }
            dcimWriter = dcimHandle?.let {
                BufferedWriter(OutputStreamWriter(it.stream, Charsets.UTF_8))
            }
            writeHeader(note)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "telemetry header write failed", e)
            errorListener?.invoke("gyro", e)
            flushAndCloseWriters()
            return
        }
        synchronized(latestAccel) { latestAccel.fill(0f) }
        queue.clear()
        writerBroken = false
        running = true

        val thread = HandlerThread("GyroLogger")
        thread.start()
        sensorThread = thread
        val handler = Handler(thread.looper)
        sensorHandler = handler
        // Priority set on the sensor thread itself (Process.setThreadPriority on a live tid).
        handler.post { Process.setThreadPriority(SENSOR_PRIORITY) }
        sensorManager.registerListener(sensorListener, gyroSensor, SensorManager.SENSOR_DELAY_FASTEST, handler)
        if (accelSensor != null) {
            sensorManager.registerListener(sensorListener, accelSensor, SensorManager.SENSOR_DELAY_FASTEST, handler)
        }
        val writer = Thread({ writerLoop() }, "GyroWriter")
        writerThread = writer
        writer.start()
        val primary = moviesHandle?.uri?.toString() ?: moviesHandle?.file?.absolutePath ?: "?"
        Log.i(TAG, "gyro logging started -> $primary (LSM6DSL @ 500 Hz expected)")
    }

    /** Unregisters listeners and flushes + closes (publishes) both copies. Idempotent. */
    @Synchronized
    fun stop() {
        if (!running) return
        running = false
        try {
            sensorManager.unregisterListener(sensorListener)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.w(TAG, "unregister failed", e)
        }
        writerThread?.let { t ->
            try { t.join(2500) } catch (e: InterruptedException) { Thread.currentThread().interrupt() }
        }
        writerThread = null
        sensorHandler = null
        sensorThread?.quitSafely()
        sensorThread = null
        Log.i(TAG, "gyro logging stopped")
    }

    /** MediaStore Uri (API 29+) or null of the primary Movies copy. */
    fun uri(): Uri? = moviesHandle?.uri

    /** Direct file (API <= 28) or null of the primary Movies copy. */
    fun file(): File? = moviesHandle?.file

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Writer
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    private fun writeHeader(note: String) {
        val sanitizedNote = note.replace('\n', ' ').replace('\r', ' ')
        val header = buildString {
            append("gyroflow v1.3\n")
            append("lens_profile:Custom\n")
            append("calibrator:M14 CinePro\n")
            append("orientation:XYZ\n")
            append("note:").append(sanitizedNote).append('\n')
            append("format:t_us,gx,gy,gz,ax,ay,az\n")
            append("tscale:1\n")
        }
        moviesWriter?.write(header)
        dcimWriter?.write(header)
        moviesWriter?.flush()
        dcimWriter?.flush()
    }

    private fun writerLoop() {
        Process.setThreadPriority(Process.THREAD_PRIORITY_BACKGROUND)
        var sinceFlush = 0
        try {
            while (true) {
                val line = queue.poll()
                if (line != null) {
                    writeTo(moviesWriter, line, "movies")
                    writeTo(dcimWriter, line, "dcim")
                    if (++sinceFlush >= WRITER_FLUSH_EVERY) {
                        try { moviesWriter?.flush() } catch (e: IOException) { disableMovies(e) }
                        try { dcimWriter?.flush() } catch (e: IOException) { disableDcim(e) }
                        sinceFlush = 0
                    }
                } else {
                    if (!running && queue.isEmpty()) break
                    try { moviesWriter?.flush() } catch (e: IOException) { disableMovies(e) }
                    try { dcimWriter?.flush() } catch (e: IOException) { disableDcim(e) }
                    sinceFlush = 0
                    Thread.sleep(WRITER_IDLE_MS)
                }
            }
        } catch (e: InterruptedException) {
            Log.w(TAG, "GyroWriter interrupted")
        } finally {
            flushAndCloseWriters()
        }
    }

    private fun writeTo(w: BufferedWriter?, line: String, label: String) {
        if (w == null) return
        try {
            w.write(line)
            w.write("\n")
        } catch (e: IOException) {
            Log.e(TAG, "$label telemetry write failed — copy disabled", e)
            if (label == "movies") disableMovies(e) else disableDcim(e)
        }
    }

    private fun disableMovies(e: IOException) {
        if (!writerBroken) {
            writerBroken = true
            errorListener?.invoke("gyro", e)
        }
        try { moviesWriter?.close() } catch (ignore: IOException) { }
        moviesWriter = null
    }

    private fun disableDcim(e: IOException) {
        try { dcimWriter?.close() } catch (ignore: IOException) { }
        dcimWriter = null
    }

    /** Final flush + close; closing the underlying streams publishes the MediaStore rows. */
    private fun flushAndCloseWriters() {
        try { moviesWriter?.flush() } catch (e: IOException) { Log.w(TAG, "movies flush", e) }
        try { dcimWriter?.flush() } catch (e: IOException) { Log.w(TAG, "dcim flush", e) }
        try { moviesWriter?.close() } catch (e: IOException) { Log.w(TAG, "movies close", e) }
        try { dcimWriter?.close() } catch (e: IOException) { Log.w(TAG, "dcim close", e) }
        moviesWriter = null
        dcimWriter = null
        moviesHandle?.let { h ->
            try { h.close(true) } catch (e: Exception) { Log.w(TAG, "movies handle close", e) }
        }
        dcimHandle?.let { h ->
            try { h.close(true) } catch (e: Exception) { Log.w(TAG, "dcim handle close", e) }
        }
    }

    private fun value(event: SensorEvent, index: Int): Float =
        if (event.values.size > index) event.values[index] else 0f
}

==> m14_cinepro/app/src/main/java/com/m14/cinepro/recording/RecordingController.kt <==
package com.m14.cinepro.recording

import android.content.Context
import android.media.MediaCodec
import android.media.MediaCodecList
import android.media.MediaFormat
import android.media.MediaMuxer
import android.net.Uri
import android.os.Bundle
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.HandlerThread
import android.os.Looper
import android.os.ParcelFileDescriptor
import android.util.Log
import android.view.Surface
import com.m14.cinepro.core.EngineListener
import com.m14.cinepro.core.SettingsStore
import java.nio.ByteBuffer

/**
 * M14 CinePro recording controller — synthetic-CFR HEVC recording per stage5_media.md §2-§3 and
 * M14_VERIFIED_TRUTH (c2.exynos.hevc.encoder verified at 80 Mbps 4K on the Galaxy M14 5G).
 *
 * Architecture (Architecture A record path):
 *  - The camera/GL pipeline renders into the persistent input surface returned by [prepareEncoder];
 *    the surface is created ONCE and reused across takes/resolutions (re-created only when the
 *    codec itself changes, because the persistent surface belongs to the codec instance).
 *  - Encoder candidates (fallback chain, first that configures+starts wins):
 *      c2.exynos.hevc.encoder -> OMX.Exynos.HEVC.Encoder -> default HEVC (MediaCodecList)
 *      -> c2.exynos.h264.encoder -> default AVC.
 *  - CFR LAW (stage5 §3): while recording, the first drained video buffer anchors firstPtsUs and
 *    EVERY recorded buffer is restamped to firstPtsUs + frameIndex * frameDurationUs with
 *    frameDurationUs = round(1e6 / fps); frameIndex advances only after a successful muxer write.
 *    When not recording, buffers are drained and released (render=false) — the encoder keeps
 *    running because the persistent surface is always fed.
 *  - MUXER ORDER LAW (stage5 §8.3): the video track is always added before the audio track. The
 *    muxer starts as soon as (video + audio formats) OR (video + 2 s audio timeout → video-only);
 *    audio samples arriving before muxer start are buffered and flushed after start.
 *  - Threading: ONE HandlerThread "EncoderDrain" owns all codec callbacks and muxer writes;
 *    startRecording/stopRecording post onto it for full serialization. All EngineListener calls
 *    are marshalled to the main looper.
 */
class RecordingController(
    private val context: Context,
    private val settings: SettingsStore,
    private val listener: EngineListener
) {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "M14Rec"

        /** MediaFormat "color-format" = 0x7F000789 COLOR_FormatSurface (stage5 §2.2). */
        private const val COLOR_FORMAT_SURFACE = 2130708361
        /** "color-standard" = BT709. */
        private const val COLOR_STANDARD_BT709 = 1
        /** "color-transfer" = SDR_VIDEO (SMPTE170M). */
        private const val COLOR_TRANSFER_SDR_VIDEO = 3
        /** "color-range" = limited. */
        private const val COLOR_RANGE_LIMITED = 2

        private const val MIME_HEVC = "video/hevc"
        private const val MIME_AVC = "video/avc"
        private const val HEVC_EXYNOS_C2 = "c2.exynos.hevc.encoder"
        private const val HEVC_OMX_EXYNOS = "OMX.Exynos.HEVC.Encoder"
        private const val AVC_EXYNOS_C2 = "c2.exynos.h264.encoder"

        private const val AUDIO_FORMAT_TIMEOUT_MS = 2000L
        private const val MUX_STOP_DELAY_MS = 300L
        private const val MAX_PENDING_AUDIO = 1024
        private const val FRAME_TICK_MS = 100L
    }

    private class Candidate(val name: String, val mime: String)

    val audioEngine = AudioEngine(settings, listener)
    val gyroLogger = GyroflowLogger(context)
    val slateManager = SlateManager(context, settings)

    private val storage = StorageHelper(context)
    private val mainHandler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())
    private val drainThread = HandlerThread("EncoderDrain").also { it.start() }
    private val drainHandler = Handler(drainThread.looper)

    // ---- encoder state (created on caller thread; drained on EncoderDrain) ----
    private var codec: MediaCodec? = null
    private var inputSurface: Surface? = null
    private var configuredKey = ""
    private var currentCodecName = "none"
    private var currentMime = MIME_HEVC
    private var videoFormat: MediaFormat? = null
    private var fps = 24
    private var frameDurationUs = 41667L

    // ---- take state (all mutations on the drain thread; volatile flags for cross-thread reads) ----
    @Volatile private var recording = false
    @Volatile private var frameIndexCounter = 0L
    private var firstPtsUs = -1L
    private var muxer: MediaMuxer? = null
    private var muxerStarted = false
    private var videoTrack = -1
    private var audioTrack = -1
    private var audioFormat: MediaFormat? = null
    private var audioTimeoutNanos = 0L
    private val pendingAudio = ArrayList<Pair<ByteBuffer, MediaCodec.BufferInfo>>()
    @Volatile private var muxWriteFailed = false
    private var clipBaseName = ""
    private var takeStartMs = 0L
    private var videoUri: Uri? = null
    private var videoPfd: ParcelFileDescriptor? = null

    init {
        gyroLogger.errorListener = { stage, t -> notifyError(stage, t) }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Public API
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    /**
     * Creates (once) or reconfigures the video encoder. Called by CameraEngine BEFORE session
     * creation each time resolution/fps/bitrate changes. Returns the persistent input surface
     * the camera must render into (null when every codec candidate failed).
     */
    fun prepareEncoder(width: Int, height: Int, fps: Int, bitrate: Int, highSpeed: Boolean): Surface? {
        val key = "$width x $height @ ${fps}fps ${bitrate}bps hs=$highSpeed"
        val existing = codec
        val surface = inputSurface
        if (existing != null && surface != null && configuredKey == key) {
            return surface
        }
        if (recording) {
            Log.w(TAG, "prepareEncoder called while recording — reconfiguring encoder")
        }
        val candidates = encoderCandidates(width, height, fps, highSpeed)
        for (candidate in candidates) {
            var created: MediaCodec? = null
            var persistent: Surface? = null
            try {
                val fmt = buildVideoFormat(candidate.mime, width, height, fps, bitrate, highSpeed)
                created = MediaCodec.createByCodecName(candidate.name)
                persistent = MediaCodec.createPersistentInputSurface()
                // Tear down the previous codec + its persistent surface BEFORE the new codec
                // starts producing callbacks, then publish all controller state, so the drain
                // callbacks never observe a half-updated configuration.
                existingCodecRelease()
                codec = created
                inputSurface = persistent
                configuredKey = key
                currentCodecName = candidate.name
                currentMime = candidate.mime
                videoFormat = null
                this.fps = fps
                frameDurationUs = Math.round(1_000_000.0 / fps)
                if (!startCodec(created, fmt, persistent)) {
                    Log.w(TAG, "candidate ${candidate.name} unusable — falling back")
                    unwindCandidate(created, persistent)
                    continue
                }
                Log.i(
                    TAG,
                    "encoder ready: ${candidate.name} ($currentMime) ${width}x$height @${fps}fps " +
                        "${bitrate / 1_000_000} Mbps highSpeed=$highSpeed"
                )
                notifyInfo("ENC ${candidate.name} ${width}x$height@${fps}")
                return persistent
            } catch (e: Throwable) {
                Log.w(TAG, "create ${candidate.name} failed: ${e.message}")
                // Only unwind controller state if THIS candidate was wired in; an earlier failure
                // (e.g. unknown codec name) must leave a still-running previous encoder intact.
                val c = created
                val s = persistent
                if (c != null && s != null && codec === c) {
                    unwindCandidate(c, s)
                }
            }
        }
        notifyError(
            "encoder",
            RuntimeException("no video encoder available for ${width}x$height @ ${fps}fps " +
                "(tried ${candidates.size} candidates)")
        )
        return inputSurface
    }

    /**
     * Opens the muxer (StorageHelper-created .mp4, rotation 0), forces an IDR via the
     * "request-sync" vendor parameter, starts audio + gyro telemetry, writes the slate JSON,
     * then flips into the CFR restamp/write state and notifies onRecordingStarted.
     */
    fun startRecording(clipBaseName: String) {
        drainHandler.post { startRecordingLocked(clipBaseName) }
    }

    /**
     * Leaves the CFR state, sends "drop-input-frames", and finalizes (muxer stop+release,
     * audio/gyro teardown, publish) 300 ms later on the drain thread.
     */
    fun stopRecording() {
        drainHandler.post { stopRecordingLocked() }
    }

    /** Full codec release; the persistent input surface is released with it. */
    fun releaseEncoder() {
        drainHandler.post { releaseEncoderLocked() }
    }

    fun isRecording(): Boolean = recording

    fun frameIndex(): Long = frameIndexCounter

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Encoder setup
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    private fun encoderCandidates(width: Int, height: Int, fps: Int, highSpeed: Boolean): List<Candidate> {
        val list = ArrayList<Candidate>(5)
        list.add(Candidate(HEVC_EXYNOS_C2, MIME_HEVC))
        list.add(Candidate(HEVC_OMX_EXYNOS, MIME_HEVC))
        defaultEncoderFor(MIME_HEVC, width, height, fps, highSpeed)?.let { list.add(Candidate(it, MIME_HEVC)) }
        list.add(Candidate(AVC_EXYNOS_C2, MIME_AVC))
        defaultEncoderFor(MIME_AVC, width, height, fps, highSpeed)?.let { list.add(Candidate(it, MIME_AVC)) }
        return list.distinctBy { it.name }
    }

    private fun defaultEncoderFor(mime: String, width: Int, height: Int, fps: Int, highSpeed: Boolean): String? = try {
        // findEncoderForFormat may mutate the format — query with a throwaway instance.
        val probe = buildVideoFormat(mime, width, height, fps, settings.bitrate.bps, highSpeed)
        MediaCodecList(MediaCodecList.REGULAR_CODECS).findEncoderForFormat(probe)
    } catch (e: Throwable) {
        Log.w(TAG, "default encoder query failed for $mime", e)
        null
    }

    /** MediaFormat exactly per stage5 §2.2/§2.3 (+ PART 2 task spec); no profile key for HEVC Main. */
    private fun buildVideoFormat(
        mime: String,
        width: Int,
        height: Int,
        fps: Int,
        bitrate: Int,
        highSpeed: Boolean
    ): MediaFormat {
        val fmt = MediaFormat.createVideoFormat(mime, width, height)
        fmt.setInteger(MediaFormat.KEY_COLOR_FORMAT, COLOR_FORMAT_SURFACE)
        fmt.setInteger("color-standard", COLOR_STANDARD_BT709)
        fmt.setInteger("color-transfer", COLOR_TRANSFER_SDR_VIDEO)
        fmt.setInteger("color-range", COLOR_RANGE_LIMITED)
        fmt.setInteger(MediaFormat.KEY_BIT_RATE, bitrate)
        fmt.setInteger(MediaFormat.KEY_MAX_WIDTH, width)
        fmt.setInteger(MediaFormat.KEY_MAX_HEIGHT, height)
        fmt.setInteger(MediaFormat.KEY_STRIDE, width)
        fmt.setInteger(MediaFormat.KEY_SLICE_HEIGHT, height)
        fmt.setInteger(MediaFormat.KEY_PRIORITY, 0)
        fmt.setInteger(MediaFormat.KEY_MAX_INPUT_SIZE, 0)
        fmt.setFloat(MediaFormat.KEY_I_FRAME_INTERVAL, 1.0f)
        fmt.setInteger(MediaFormat.KEY_FRAME_RATE, fps)
        if (highSpeed) {
            // 720p60 high-speed session: HAL outputs 60.06 fps real-time (M14_VERIFIED_TRUTH §4C),
            // so capture-rate == fps keeps playback real-time.
            fmt.setFloat("capture-rate", fps.toFloat())
        }
        return fmt
    }

    /**
     * configure + setInputSurface + setCallback + start. Framework versions differ on whether
     * setCallback is accepted before configure — both legal orderings are attempted with
     * MediaCodec.reset() between them.
     */
    private fun startCodec(created: MediaCodec, fmt: MediaFormat, surface: Surface): Boolean {
        try {
            created.configure(fmt, null, null, MediaCodec.CONFIGURE_FLAG_ENCODE)
            created.setInputSurface(surface)
            created.setCallback(codecCallback, drainHandler)
            created.start()
            return true
        } catch (first: Throwable) {
            Log.w(TAG, "configure-first path failed (${first.message}); retrying callback-first")
        }
        try {
            created.reset()
        } catch (e: Throwable) {
            Log.w(TAG, "codec reset failed", e)
            return false
        }
        try {
            created.setCallback(codecCallback, drainHandler)
            created.configure(fmt, null, null, MediaCodec.CONFIGURE_FLAG_ENCODE)
            created.setInputSurface(surface)
            created.start()
            return true
        } catch (second: Throwable) {
            Log.w(TAG, "codec start failed on both paths", second)
            return false
        }
    }

    private fun existingCodecRelease() {
        codec?.let { old ->
            try { old.stop() } catch (e: Throwable) { Log.w(TAG, "old codec stop", e) }
            try { old.release() } catch (e: Throwable) { Log.w(TAG, "old codec release", e) }
        }
        inputSurface?.let { old ->
            try { old.release() } catch (e: Throwable) { Log.w(TAG, "old surface release", e) }
        }
    }

    /** Releases a rejected candidate and clears controller state that pointed at it. */
    private fun unwindCandidate(created: MediaCodec, persistent: Surface) {
        try { created.release() } catch (e: Exception) { Log.w(TAG, "release failed", e) }
        try { persistent.release() } catch (e: Exception) { Log.w(TAG, "surface release failed", e) }
        if (codec === created) codec = null
        if (inputSurface === persistent) inputSurface = null
        configuredKey = ""
    }

    private fun releaseEncoderLocked() {
        codec?.let { c ->
            try { c.stop() } catch (e: Throwable) { Log.w(TAG, "codec stop", e) }
            try { c.release() } catch (e: Throwable) { Log.w(TAG, "codec release", e) }
        }
        codec = null
        inputSurface?.let { s ->
            try { s.release() } catch (e: Throwable) { Log.w(TAG, "surface release", e) }
        }
        inputSurface = null
        configuredKey = ""
        videoFormat = null
        Log.i(TAG, "encoder released (persistent surface freed)")
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Codec drain (EncoderDrain thread)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    private val codecCallback = object : MediaCodec.Callback() {
        override fun onInputBufferAvailable(mc: MediaCodec, index: Int) {
            // Surface input — buffers are rendered by the camera/GL pipeline, nothing to queue.
        }

        override fun onOutputBufferAvailable(mc: MediaCodec, index: Int, info: MediaCodec.BufferInfo) {
            drainVideoOutput(mc, index, info)
        }

        override fun onOutputFormatChanged(mc: MediaCodec, format: MediaFormat) {
            Log.i(TAG, "video output format changed ($currentCodecName)")
            videoFormat = format
            onVideoFormatAvailable()
        }

        override fun onError(mc: MediaCodec, e: MediaCodec.CodecException) {
            Log.e(TAG, "encoder error: ${e.diagnosticInfo}", e)
            notifyError("encoder", e)
            if (recording) stopRecording() // safe-stop; posts onto the drain thread
        }
    }

    /** CFR restamp + muxer write (stage5 §3). Runs on the EncoderDrain thread. */
    private fun drainVideoOutput(mc: MediaCodec, index: Int, info: MediaCodec.BufferInfo) {
        val out = try {
            mc.getOutputBuffer(index)
        } catch (e: Throwable) {
            null
        }
        if (out != null && info.size > 0 && (info.flags and MediaCodec.BUFFER_FLAG_CODEC_CONFIG) == 0) {
            if (recording) {
                if (firstPtsUs < 0) firstPtsUs = info.presentationTimeUs
                info.presentationTimeUs = firstPtsUs + frameIndexCounter * frameDurationUs
                val mux = muxer
                if (mux != null && muxerStarted && videoTrack >= 0 && !muxWriteFailed) {
                    try {
                        out.position(info.offset)
                        out.limit(info.offset + info.size)
                        mux.writeSampleData(videoTrack, out, info)
                        frameIndexCounter++ // advance only after a successful write
                    } catch (e: Throwable) {
                        Log.e(TAG, "video mux write failed", e)
                        muxWriteFailed = true
                        notifyError("muxer", e)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        try {
            mc.releaseOutputBuffer(index, false)
        } catch (e: Throwable) {
            Log.w(TAG, "releaseOutputBuffer failed", e)
        }
    }

    /** Adds the video track (always before audio — stage5 §8.3 order law) and maybe starts the muxer. */
    private fun onVideoFormatAvailable() {
        val mux = muxer ?: return
        if (muxerStarted) return
        val fmt = videoFormat ?: return
        if (videoTrack < 0) {
            videoTrack = try {
                mux.addTrack(fmt)
            } catch (e: Throwable) {
                Log.e(TAG, "video addTrack failed", e)
                notifyError("muxer", e)
                -1
            }
            if (videoTrack >= 0) {
                val af = audioFormat
                if (af != null && audioTrack < 0) {
                    audioTrack = try {
                        mux.addTrack(af)
                    } catch (e: Throwable) {
                        Log.e(TAG, "audio addTrack failed", e)
                        notifyError("muxer", e)
                        -1
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        tryStartMuxer()
    }

    /** Audio track add (called via drain-handler post from the AudioEngine format callback). */
    private fun onAudioFormatAvailable(format: MediaFormat) {
        audioFormat = format
        val mux = muxer ?: return
        if (muxerStarted) {
            Log.w(TAG, "audio format arrived after muxer start — take stays video-only")
            return
        }
        if (videoTrack >= 0 && audioTrack < 0) {
            audioTrack = try {
                mux.addTrack(format)
            } catch (e: Throwable) {
                Log.e(TAG, "audio addTrack failed", e)
                notifyError("muxer", e)
                -1
            }
        }
        tryStartMuxer()
    }

    private fun tryStartMuxer() {
        if (muxerStarted || muxer == null) return
        if (videoTrack < 0) return
        val audioReady = audioTrack >= 0
        val timedOut = System.nanoTime() >= audioTimeoutNanos
        if (!audioReady && !timedOut) return
        try {
            muxer?.start()
            muxerStarted = true
            val msg = if (audioReady) "MUX video+audio" else "MUX video-only (no audio format in 2 s)"
            Log.i(TAG, msg)
            notifyInfo(msg)
            flushPendingAudio()
        } catch (e: Throwable) {
            Log.e(TAG, "muxer start failed", e)
            notifyError("muxer", e)
        }
    }

    private fun flushPendingAudio() {
        val mux = muxer ?: return
        if (!muxerStarted || audioTrack < 0) {
            pendingAudio.clear()
            return
        }
        for ((buffer, info) in pendingAudio) {
            if (muxWriteFailed) break
            try {
                mux.writeSampleData(audioTrack, buffer, info)
            } catch (e: Throwable) {
                Log.e(TAG, "buffered audio write failed", e)
                muxWriteFailed = true
                notifyError("muxer", e)
                break
            }
        }
        pendingAudio.clear()
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Audio sink (encoded AAC -> muxer, serialized on the drain thread)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    private val audioSink = object : AudioEngine.AudioSink {
        override fun onAudioSample(buffer: ByteBuffer, info: MediaCodec.BufferInfo) {
            drainHandler.post {
                if (!recording) return@post
                val mux = muxer
                if (mux != null && muxerStarted && audioTrack >= 0 && !muxWriteFailed) {
                    try {
                        mux.writeSampleData(audioTrack, buffer, info)
                    } catch (e: Throwable) {
                        Log.e(TAG, "audio mux write failed", e)
                        muxWriteFailed = true
                        notifyError("muxer", e)
                    }
                } else if (mux != null) {
                    // Buffer until the muxer starts (audio-before-video / muxer-start latency).
                    if (pendingAudio.size >= MAX_PENDING_AUDIO) pendingAudio.removeAt(0)
                    pendingAudio.add(buffer to info)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    init {
        // Wired here (after audioSink declaration) to avoid a forward reference from the first init block.
        audioEngine.setSink(audioSink)
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Take lifecycle (drain thread)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    private fun startRecordingLocked(clip: String) {
        if (recording) {
            Log.w(TAG, "startRecording ignored — already recording")
            return
        }
        if (codec == null || inputSurface == null) {
            notifyError("record", RuntimeException("encoder not prepared — call prepareEncoder() first"))
            return
        }
        try {
            clipBaseName = clip
            firstPtsUs = -1L
            frameIndexCounter = 0L
            muxWriteFailed = false
            takeStartMs = System.currentTimeMillis()

            val fd = storage.videoFd(clip)
            if (fd == null) {
                notifyError("storage", RuntimeException("cannot create video file for $clip (storage unavailable?)"))
                return
            }
            videoUri = fd.first
            videoPfd = fd.second
            // Rotation 0: the sensor is used in its native landscape orientation.
            muxer = MediaMuxer(fd.second.fileDescriptor, MediaMuxer.OutputFormat.MUXER_OUTPUT_MPEG_4)
            videoTrack = -1
            audioTrack = -1
            audioFormat = null
            muxerStarted = false
            pendingAudio.clear()

            // Force IDR at record start (vendor key, stage5 §3 — mk7.f() equivalent).
            try {
                val sync = Bundle()
                sync.putInt("request-sync", 0)
                codec?.setParameters(sync)
            } catch (e: Throwable) {
                Log.w(TAG, "request-sync unsupported by ${currentCodecName}: ${e.message}")
            }

            // Audio: format arrives via callback -> drain thread -> addTrack after video track.
            audioTimeoutNanos = System.nanoTime() + AUDIO_FORMAT_TIMEOUT_MS * 1_000_000L
            audioEngine.start { format -> drainHandler.post { onAudioFormatAvailable(format) } }

            // Gyroflow telemetry: <clip>.gcsv at 500 Hz (Movies + DCIM copies).
            try {
                gyroLogger.start(
                    clip,
                    "R${settings.reel} S${settings.scene} T${settings.take} ${settings.describeForMetadata()}"
                )
            } catch (e: Throwable) {
                Log.e(TAG, "gyro logger failed to start", e)
                notifyError("gyro", e)
            }

            // Slate registry: <clip>_slate.json (Movies + DCIM copies).
            try {
                slateManager.writeSlateJson(clip, storage)
            } catch (e: Throwable) {
                Log.e(TAG, "slate write failed", e)
                notifyError("slate", e)
            }

            // The encoder may have already reported its output format (it keeps running between
            // takes) — add the video track right now so the muxer can start on first audio sample.
            if (videoFormat != null) onVideoFormatAvailable()
            drainHandler.postDelayed({ tryStartMuxer() }, AUDIO_FORMAT_TIMEOUT_MS)

            recording = true
            drainHandler.postDelayed(frameTicker, FRAME_TICK_MS)
            Log.i(TAG, "REC start: $clip")
            mainHandler.post { listener.onRecordingStarted(clip) }
        } catch (e: Throwable) {
            Log.e(TAG, "startRecording failed", e)
            notifyError("record", e)
            cleanupFailedStartLocked()
        }
    }

    private fun stopRecordingLocked() {
        if (!recording) {
            Log.w(TAG, "stopRecording ignored — not recording")
            return
        }
        recording = false
        drainHandler.removeCallbacks(frameTicker)
        try {
            val drop = Bundle()
            drop.putInt("drop-input-frames", 1)
            codec?.setParameters(drop)
        } catch (e: Throwable) {
            Log.w(TAG, "drop-input-frames unsupported by ${currentCodecName}: ${e.message}")
        }
        Log.i(TAG, "REC stop: $clipBaseName — finalizing in ${MUX_STOP_DELAY_MS} ms")
        drainHandler.postDelayed({ finalizeTakeLocked() }, MUX_STOP_DELAY_MS)
    }

    private fun finalizeTakeLocked() {
        val clip = clipBaseName
        val frames = frameIndexCounter
        val durationMs = System.currentTimeMillis() - takeStartMs
        val started = muxerStarted // captured before teardown flips the flag
        if (started) {
            try {
                muxer?.stop()
            } catch (e: Throwable) {
                Log.e(TAG, "muxer stop failed", e)
                notifyError("muxer", e)
            }
        } else {
            Log.w(TAG, "muxer never started — discarding unfinished clip $clip")
        }
        try { muxer?.release() } catch (e: Throwable) { Log.w(TAG, "muxer release", e) }
        muxer = null
        muxerStarted = false
        videoTrack = -1
        audioTrack = -1
        pendingAudio.clear()
        try { videoPfd?.close() } catch (e: Throwable) { Log.w(TAG, "pfd close", e) }
        videoPfd = null
        if (videoUri != null) {
            if (started) {
                storage.publish(videoUri!!)
            } else {
                storage.deletePending(videoUri!!)
            }
        }
        videoUri = null

        try { audioEngine.stop() } catch (e: Throwable) { Log.w(TAG, "audio stop", e) }
        try { gyroLogger.stop() } catch (e: Throwable) { Log.w(TAG, "gyro stop", e) }
        slateManager.nextTake() // roll the take counter for the next clip

        Log.i(TAG, "take finalized: $clip frames=$frames durationMs=$durationMs")
        mainHandler.post { listener.onRecordingStopped(clip, durationMs, frames) }
    }

    private fun cleanupFailedStartLocked() {
        recording = false
        drainHandler.removeCallbacks(frameTicker)
        try { muxer?.release() } catch (e: Throwable) { Log.w(TAG, "muxer release", e) }
        muxer = null
        muxerStarted = false
        videoTrack = -1
        audioTrack = -1
        pendingAudio.clear()
        try { videoPfd?.close() } catch (e: Throwable) { Log.w(TAG, "pfd close", e) }
        videoPfd = null
        videoUri?.let { storage.deletePending(it) }
        videoUri = null
        try { audioEngine.stop() } catch (e: Throwable) { Log.w(TAG, "audio stop", e) }
        try { gyroLogger.stop() } catch (e: Throwable) { Log.w(TAG, "gyro stop", e) }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Timecode ticker (EngineListener.onFrameCount ~10 Hz)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    private val frameTicker = object : Runnable {
        override fun run() {
            if (!recording) return
            val frames = frameIndexCounter
            val rate = fps
            mainHandler.post { listener.onFrameCount(frames, rate) }
            drainHandler.postDelayed(this, FRAME_TICK_MS)
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Listener plumbing (always via the main looper)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    private fun notifyError(stage: String, t: Throwable) {
        mainHandler.post { listener.onError(stage, t) }
    }

    private fun notifyInfo(message: String) {
        mainHandler.post { listener.onInfo(message) }
    }
}

==> m14_cinepro/app/src/main/java/com/m14/cinepro/recording/SlateManager.kt <==
package com.m14.cinepro.recording

import android.content.Context
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.os.Build
import android.util.Log
import com.m14.cinepro.core.Models
import com.m14.cinepro.core.SettingsStore
import org.json.JSONObject
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Date
import java.util.Locale

/**
 * Slate / clip naming for M14 CinePro (stage5_media.md BmdMetadata registry contract).
 *
 * - [clipBaseName] builds "M14_R<reel>_S<scene>_T<take>_<yyyyMMdd_HHmmss>" with the scene field
 *   sanitized to alnum + '-_' only (file-system safe).
 * - [nextTake] bumps and persists settings.take (called by RecordingController after a take
 *   finalizes so the next clip rolls the take number).
 * - [writeSlateJson] emits the per-clip slate registry to BOTH Movies/M14_CinePro and
 *   DCIM/M14_CinePro as <baseName>_slate.json, then resets the good-take flag (consumed once).
 */
class SlateManager(private val context: Context, private val settings: SettingsStore) {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "M14Rec"
    }

    /** Bumps the persistent take counter and returns the new value. */
    fun nextTake(): Int {
        val next = settings.take + 1
        settings.take = next
        return next
    }

    /** "M14_R<reel>_S<scene>_T<take>_<yyyyMMdd_HHmmss>" — base name without extension. */
    fun clipBaseName(): String {
        val scene = settings.scene
            .filter { it.isLetterOrDigit() || it == '-' || it == '_' }
            .ifBlank { "NA" }
        val stamp = SimpleDateFormat("yyyyMMdd_HHmmss", Locale.US).format(Date())
        return String.format(Locale.US, "M14_R%d_S%s_T%d_%s", settings.reel, scene, settings.take, stamp)
    }

    /**
     * Writes <baseName>_slate.json (Movies + DCIM copies) containing the full slate registry.
     * Returns the JSONObject (also used by callers that want to display/verify the slate).
     */
    fun writeSlateJson(baseName: String, storage: StorageHelper): JSONObject {
        val goodTake = settings.goodTake
        val recordedAt = SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ssZ", Locale.US).format(Date())

        val json = JSONObject()
        json.put("reel", settings.reel)
        json.put("scene", settings.scene)
        json.put("take", settings.take)
        json.put("goodTake", goodTake)
        json.put("director", settings.director)
        json.put("operator", settings.operator)
        json.put("resolution", settings.resolution.label)
        json.put("fps", settings.fps)
        json.put(
            "shutterAngle",
            if (settings.shutterMode == Models.ShutterMode.ANGLE) {
                String.format(Locale.US, "%.1f°", settings.shutterAngleDeg)
            } else {
                ""
            }
        )
        json.put(
            "shutterSpeed",
            if (settings.shutterMode == Models.ShutterMode.SPEED) {
                "1/${settings.shutterSpeedDenominator} s"
            } else {
                ""
            }
        )
        json.put("iso", settings.iso)
        json.put("kelvin", settings.kelvin)
        json.put("tint", settings.tint)
        json.put("lut", settings.lutPreset.name)
        json.put("bitrate", settings.bitrate.bps)
        json.put("clipName", "$baseName.mp4")
        json.put("recordedAtIso8601", recordedAt)
        json.put("appVersion", appVersion())
        json.put("deviceModel", Build.MODEL)

        val body = json.toString(2)
        val bytes = body.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8)
        for (handle in listOfNotNull(storage.slateHandle(baseName), storage.slateHandleDcim(baseName))) {
            try {
                handle.stream.write(bytes)
                handle.stream.flush()
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.e(TAG, "slate write failed", e)
            } finally {
                try { handle.close(true) } catch (e: Exception) { Log.w(TAG, "slate handle close failed", e) }
            }
        }
        settings.goodTake = false // consume the good-take flag once slated
        Log.i(TAG, "slate written for $baseName (goodTake=$goodTake)")
        return json
    }

    private fun appVersion(): String = try {
        val pm = context.packageManager
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 33) {
            pm.getPackageInfo(context.packageName, PackageManager.PackageInfoFlags.of(0L)).versionName
                ?: "unknown"
        } else {
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            pm.getPackageInfo(context.packageName, 0).versionName ?: "unknown"
        }
    } catch (e: Exception) {
        "unknown"
    }
}

==> m14_cinepro/app/src/main/java/com/m14/cinepro/recording/StorageHelper.kt <==
package com.m14.cinepro.recording

import android.content.ContentValues
import android.content.Context
import android.net.Uri
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Environment
import android.os.ParcelFileDescriptor
import android.os.StatFs
import android.provider.MediaStore
import android.provider.OpenableColumns
import android.util.Log
import java.io.File
import java.io.FilterOutputStream
import java.io.FileOutputStream
import java.io.IOException
import java.io.OutputStream

/**
 * Unified output handle for every artifact this app contributes to public storage.
 *
 * - API 29+: the row is inserted into MediaStore with IS_PENDING=1; [close](true) closes the
 *   stream and clears IS_PENDING (publishes), [close](false) closes and deletes the pending row.
 * - API <= 28: a direct java.io.File under the requested public directory; [close](true) just
 *   flushes+closes, [close](false) deletes the file.
 */
data class OutputHandle(
    val uri: Uri?,
    val file: File?,
    val stream: OutputStream,
    val close: (Boolean) -> Unit
)

/**
 * Storage layer for M14 CinePro clips, Gyroflow telemetry and slate JSON.
 *
 * API 29+ contributions go through MediaStore (no storage permission needed):
 *   video  -> MediaStore.Video, Movies/M14_CinePro, mime video/mp4
 *   .gcsv  -> MediaStore.Files, Movies/M14_CinePro + DCIM/M14_CinePro copy, application/octet-stream
 *   slate  -> MediaStore.Files, Movies/M14_CinePro + DCIM/M14_CinePro copy, application/json
 *
 * MediaMuxer needs a real FileDescriptor, hence [videoFd] opening the pending video row via
 * openFileDescriptor(uri, "rw"); MediaMuxer(FileDescriptor, format) is available since API 26.
 */
class StorageHelper(private val context: Context) {

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "M14Rec"

        /** Canonical contribution folders (stage5_media.md + PART 5 spec). */
        const val DIR_MOVIES = "Movies/M14_CinePro"
        const val DIR_DCIM = "DCIM/M14_CinePro"
        private const val MIME_MP4 = "video/mp4"
        private const val MIME_BIN = "application/octet-stream"
        private const val MIME_JSON = "application/json"
        private const val LUT_DIR = "luts"
    }

    private val useMediaStore: Boolean
        get() = Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 29

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Video
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    /**
     * Video output as FileDescriptor for MediaMuxer. API 29+ inserts a pending MediaStore.Video
     * row and opens it "rw"; API <= 28 opens a direct file under Movies/M14_CinePro.
     * The returned Uri is a content:// uri (29+) or a file:// uri (legacy, for deletePending).
     */
    fun videoFd(baseName: String): Pair<Uri, ParcelFileDescriptor>? {
        return if (useMediaStore) {
            val resolver = context.contentResolver
            val uri = insertPending(
                MediaStore.Video.Media.EXTERNAL_CONTENT_URI,
                "$baseName.mp4", MIME_MP4, DIR_MOVIES
            ) ?: return null
            val pfd = try {
                resolver.openFileDescriptor(uri, "rw")
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.e(TAG, "openFileDescriptor failed for $baseName.mp4", e)
                null
            }
            if (pfd == null) {
                try { resolver.delete(uri, null, null) } catch (e: Exception) { Log.w(TAG, "row delete failed", e) }
                return null
            }
            uri to pfd
        } else {
            val file = legacyFile(Environment.DIRECTORY_MOVIES, "$baseName.mp4") ?: return null
            val pfd = try {
                ParcelFileDescriptor.open(
                    file,
                    ParcelFileDescriptor.MODE_READ_WRITE or
                        ParcelFileDescriptor.MODE_CREATE or
                        ParcelFileDescriptor.MODE_TRUNCATE
                )
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.e(TAG, "pfd open failed for ${file.absolutePath}", e)
                null
            } ?: return null
            Uri.fromFile(file) to pfd
        }
    }

    /** Spec API: plain stream pair for the video artifact (publishes on stream close, 29+). */
    fun createVideoFile(baseName: String): Pair<Uri, OutputStream>? {
        val handle = videoHandle(baseName) ?: return null
        val uri = handle.uri ?: handle.file?.let { Uri.fromFile(it) } ?: return null
        return uri to handle.stream
    }

    /** Unified handle variant of [createVideoFile] with commit/abort control. */
    fun videoHandle(baseName: String): OutputHandle? {
        return if (useMediaStore) {
            val resolver = context.contentResolver
            val uri = insertPending(
                MediaStore.Video.Media.EXTERNAL_CONTENT_URI,
                "$baseName.mp4", MIME_MP4, DIR_MOVIES
            ) ?: return null
            val raw = try {
                resolver.openOutputStream(uri)
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.e(TAG, "openOutputStream failed for $baseName.mp4", e)
                null
            }
            if (raw == null) {
                try { resolver.delete(uri, null, null) } catch (e: Exception) { Log.w(TAG, "row delete failed", e) }
                return null
            }
            buildHandle(uri, null, raw, "$baseName.mp4")
        } else {
            val file = legacyFile(Environment.DIRECTORY_MOVIES, "$baseName.mp4") ?: return null
            val raw = try {
                FileOutputStream(file)
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.e(TAG, "file create failed for ${file.absolutePath}", e)
                null
            } ?: return null
            buildHandle(null, file, raw, "$baseName.mp4")
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Telemetry (.gcsv) and slate (.json)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    /** Movies/M14_CinePro/<base>.gcsv — Gyroflow telemetry, primary copy. */
    fun telemetryHandle(baseName: String): OutputHandle? =
        openHandle(
            MediaStore.Files.getContentUri("external"), DIR_MOVIES,
            "$baseName.gcsv", MIME_BIN, Environment.DIRECTORY_MOVIES
        )

    /** DCIM/M14_CinePro/<base>.gcsv — identical telemetry copy. */
    fun telemetryHandleDcim(baseName: String): OutputHandle? =
        openHandle(
            MediaStore.Files.getContentUri("external"), DIR_DCIM,
            "$baseName.gcsv", MIME_BIN, Environment.DIRECTORY_DCIM
        )

    /** Movies/M14_CinePro/<base>_slate.json — clip slate metadata. */
    fun slateHandle(baseName: String): OutputHandle? =
        openHandle(
            MediaStore.Files.getContentUri("external"), DIR_MOVIES,
            "${baseName}_slate.json", MIME_JSON, Environment.DIRECTORY_MOVIES
        )

    /** DCIM/M14_CinePro/<base>_slate.json — identical slate copy. */
    fun slateHandleDcim(baseName: String): OutputHandle? =
        openHandle(
            MediaStore.Files.getContentUri("external"), DIR_DCIM,
            "${baseName}_slate.json", MIME_JSON, Environment.DIRECTORY_DCIM
        )

    /** Spec API: raw streams (auto-publish on close, API 29+). */
    fun createTelemetryFile(baseName: String): OutputStream? = telemetryHandle(baseName)?.stream
    fun createTelemetryFileDcim(baseName: String): OutputStream? = telemetryHandleDcim(baseName)?.stream
    fun createSlateFile(baseName: String): OutputStream? = slateHandle(baseName)?.stream

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Publish / discard
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    /** Clears IS_PENDING on a MediaStore row (no-op for file:// uris and API <= 28). */
    fun publish(uri: Uri) {
        if (uri.scheme == "file") return
        if (!useMediaStore) return
        try {
            val cv = ContentValues()
            cv.put(MediaStore.MediaColumns.IS_PENDING, 0)
            context.contentResolver.update(uri, cv, null, null)
            Log.i(TAG, "published $uri")
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "publish failed: $uri", e)
        }
    }

    /** Removes an unfinished contribution (pending row on 29+, file on legacy paths). */
    fun deletePending(uri: Uri) {
        try {
            if (uri.scheme == "file") {
                val f = File(uri.path ?: return)
                if (f.exists()) f.delete()
                return
            }
            if (useMediaStore) {
                context.contentResolver.delete(uri, null, null)
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "deletePending failed: $uri", e)
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Volume status + LUT import
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    /** Free space on the record volume in GB, two decimals (0f on failure). */
    fun freeGb(): Float = try {
        @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
        val dir = Environment.getExternalStorageDirectory()
        val stat = StatFs(dir.absolutePath)
        val gb = stat.availableBytes / 1e9
        (Math.round(gb * 100.0) / 100.0).toFloat()
    } catch (e: Exception) {
        Log.w(TAG, "freeGb failed", e)
        0f
    }

    /**
     * Copies a user-picked .cube LUT into app-private external storage
     * (Android/data/<pkg>/files/luts/<name>.cube). Returns the absolute path or null.
     */
    fun importCubeLut(uri: Uri): String? = try {
        val rawName = displayName(uri) ?: "imported_${System.currentTimeMillis()}"
        val safe = rawName
            .filter { it.isLetterOrDigit() || it == '.' || it == '_' || it == '-' || it == ' ' }
            .trim()
            .ifBlank { "imported" }
        val fileName = if (safe.endsWith(".cube", ignoreCase = true)) safe else "$safe.cube"
        val dir = context.getExternalFilesDir(LUT_DIR) ?: run {
            Log.e(TAG, "external files dir unavailable")
            return null
        }
        dir.mkdirs()
        val out = File(dir, fileName)
        val ins = context.contentResolver.openInputStream(uri) ?: run {
            Log.e(TAG, "cannot open LUT stream: $uri")
            return null
        }
        ins.use { input ->
            out.outputStream().use { output -> input.copyTo(output) }
        }
        Log.i(TAG, "LUT imported: ${out.absolutePath} (${out.length()} bytes)")
        out.absolutePath
    } catch (e: Exception) {
        Log.e(TAG, "LUT import failed", e)
        null
    }

    /** Legacy API <= 28 has no content Uri for direct files — always null by contract. */
    fun asUri(file: File): Uri? = null

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Internals
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    private fun insertPending(collection: Uri, name: String, mime: String, relativePath: String): Uri? = try {
        val cv = ContentValues()
        cv.put(MediaStore.MediaColumns.DISPLAY_NAME, name)
        cv.put(MediaStore.MediaColumns.MIME_TYPE, mime)
        cv.put(MediaStore.MediaColumns.RELATIVE_PATH, relativePath)
        cv.put(MediaStore.MediaColumns.IS_PENDING, 1)
        context.contentResolver.insert(collection, cv)
    } catch (e: Exception) {
        Log.e(TAG, "MediaStore insert failed ($name @ $relativePath)", e)
        null
    }

    /** Builds a unified handle; MediaStore streams publish IS_PENDING=0 on close. */
    private fun buildHandle(uri: Uri?, file: File?, raw: OutputStream, name: String): OutputHandle {
        return if (uri != null) {
            val resolver = context.contentResolver
            var published = false
            val stream = object : FilterOutputStream(raw) {
                private var closed = false
                override fun write(b: ByteArray, off: Int, len: Int) {
                    out.write(b, off, len)
                }
                override fun close() {
                    if (closed) return
                    closed = true
                    try {
                        super.close()
                    } catch (e: IOException) {
                        Log.w(TAG, "stream close failed ($name)", e)
                    }
                    if (!published) {
                        published = true
                        try {
                            val cv = ContentValues()
                            cv.put(MediaStore.MediaColumns.IS_PENDING, 0)
                            resolver.update(uri, cv, null, null)
                        } catch (e: Exception) {
                            Log.e(TAG, "publish failed ($name)", e)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            OutputHandle(uri, null, stream) { commit ->
                try {
                    stream.close()
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    Log.w(TAG, "handle close failed ($name)", e)
                }
                if (!commit) {
                    try { resolver.delete(uri, null, null) } catch (e: Exception) { Log.w(TAG, "discard failed ($name)", e) }
                }
            }
        } else {
            val f = file ?: throw IllegalStateException("OutputHandle requires uri or file")
            OutputHandle(null, f, raw) { commit ->
                try {
                    raw.close()
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    Log.w(TAG, "handle close failed ($name)", e)
                }
                if (!commit) f.delete()
            }
        }
    }

    private fun openHandle(
        collection: Uri,
        relativePath: String,
        name: String,
        mime: String,
        legacyRoot: String
    ): OutputHandle? {
        if (useMediaStore) {
            val resolver = context.contentResolver
            val uri = insertPending(collection, name, mime, relativePath)
            if (uri != null) {
                val raw = try {
                    resolver.openOutputStream(uri)
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    Log.e(TAG, "openOutputStream failed ($name)", e)
                    null
                }
                if (raw != null) {
                    return buildHandle(uri, null, raw, name)
                }
                try { resolver.delete(uri, null, null) } catch (e: Exception) { Log.w(TAG, "row delete failed", e) }
            }
        }
        val file = legacyFile(legacyRoot, name) ?: return null
        val raw = try {
            FileOutputStream(file)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "file create failed ($name)", e)
            null
        } ?: return null
        return buildHandle(null, file, raw, name)
    }

    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
    private fun legacyFile(rootType: String, name: String): File? = try {
        val root = Environment.getExternalStoragePublicDirectory(rootType)
        val dir = File(root, "M14_CinePro")
        if (!dir.exists() && !dir.mkdirs()) {
            Log.e(TAG, "mkdirs failed: ${dir.absolutePath}")
            null
        } else {
            File(dir, name)
        }
    } catch (e: Exception) {
        Log.e(TAG, "legacy path failed ($rootType)", e)
        null
    }

    private fun displayName(uri: Uri): String? = try {
        context.contentResolver
            .query(uri, arrayOf(OpenableColumns.DISPLAY_NAME), null, null, null)
            ?.use { c -> if (c.moveToFirst()) c.getString(0) else null }
            ?: uri.lastPathSegment
    } catch (e: Exception) {
        uri.lastPathSegment
    }
}

==> m14_cinepro/app/src/main/java/com/m14/cinepro/sensor/GyroEisController.kt <==
package com.m14.cinepro.sensor

import android.content.Context
import android.hardware.Sensor
import android.hardware.SensorEvent
import android.hardware.SensorEventListener
import android.hardware.SensorManager
